// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME
// CRYPT NETWORK TELEVISION WORKER
//
// MADE WITH CRUELTY ENGINE
//
// Loki's Kingdom
// by LUCIFER MEDIA CORPORATION
//
// MASTER BUILD — PART 1
// =========================================================
//
// IMPORTANT ASSEMBLY RULE:
//
// This is ONE contiguous Cloudflare Worker.
//
// 1. Paste PART 1 into a NEW EMPTY text document.
// 2. Append PART 2 directly after the final line of PART 1.
// 3. Continue in numerical order.
// 4. Do NOT place separate Workers between parts.
// 5. Do NOT deploy until the final part is present.
//
// The complete Worker will implement:
//
// - "recall thy name" television show;
// - autonomous two-character conversation;
// - [NAME_VAR] host;
// - the Nameless Man;
// - GPT-5.4 conversation;
// - player/audience interruption;
// - TTS;
// - randomized conversation modes;
// - the three conversational locks;
// - 1985 looping calendar;
// - historical daily discussion seeds;
// - forward vision;
// - real-time show clock;
// - episode/watch-time statistics;
// - 1h35 hostility escalation;
// - 2-hour combat transition;
// - persistent combat;
// - Nameless Man XP / exponential growth;
// - fixed-power hosts;
// - host permadeath;
// - Host Store with randomized casting;
// - cosmetics largely decoupled from combat power;
// - extremely short through absurdly long matchups;
// - shared Loki's Kingdom economy;
// - persistent D1 state;
// - long-duration memory;
// - THE EFSMB JUKEBOX integration;
// - refresh-safe continuation;
// - TURN OFF THE TV.
//
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// WORKER ROUTER
// =========================================================

export default {
  async fetch(
    request,
    env
  ) {
    const url =
      new URL(
        request.url
      );

    const origin =
      request.headers.get(
        "Origin"
      ) || "";

    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * CORS
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * The TV may eventually be opened directly as its own
     * Worker page OR called from lokiskingdom.com.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "OPTIONS"
    ) {
      return handleCorsPreflight(
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * HEALTH CHECK
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/health"
    ) {
      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            true,

          resident:
            "recall-thy-name",

          network:
            "CRYPT NETWORK",

          year:
            1985,

          model:
            RECALL_MODEL,

          databaseAvailable:
            Boolean(
              env.WORLD_DB
            ),

          openAIConfigured:
            Boolean(
              env.OPENAI_API_KEY
            ),

          version:
            RECALL_ENGINE_VERSION
        },
        200,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * COMPLETE PUBLIC SHOW STATE
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Used when:
     *
     * - the page opens;
     * - the TV turns on;
     * - the page refreshes;
     * - the UI needs authoritative counters;
     * - the client needs current combat/show state.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/state"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleState(
          request,
          env,
          url
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * TURN TELEVISION ON
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/tv/on"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleTurnTvOn(
          request,
          env
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * TURN TELEVISION OFF
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/tv/off"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleTurnTvOff(
          request,
          env
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * LIVE WATCH-TIME HEARTBEAT
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * The browser periodically reports that the television
     * is still actively open.
     *
     * The server remains authoritative for cumulative
     * watch time.
     *
     * We do NOT trust a browser-supplied number of seconds.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/tv/heartbeat"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleWatchHeartbeat(
          request,
          env
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * NEXT AUTONOMOUS HOST TURN
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Later parts implement:
     *
     * - conversation modes;
     * - character selection;
     * - personality prompts;
     * - memory;
     * - topic drift;
     * - TTS completion sequencing;
     * - combat dialogue.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/show/next"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleNextShowTurn(
          request,
          env
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Player input has conversational priority.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/show/interject"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleAudienceInterjection(
          request,
          env
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * CURRENT HOST
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/host"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleCurrentHost(
          request,
          env,
          url
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * HOST STORE
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Generates the current casting selection.
     *
     * Default:
     *
     *     7 candidates
     *
     * Price is NOT a simple combat-power ladder.
     *
     * Clothing, novelty, rarity, appearance, absurdity,
     * luxury and cosmetics may make a weak host cost more
     * than an apocalyptic god.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/host-store"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleHostStore(
          request,
          env,
          url
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * HIRE HOST
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "POST" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/host-store/hire"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleHireHost(
          request,
          env
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * TODAY IN 1985
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/today-in-1985"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleTodayIn1985(
          request,
          env,
          url
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * JUKEBOX PUBLIC CATALOG
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * The actual catalogue/player integration arrives
     * in a later part.
     */

    if (
      request.method ===
        "GET" &&
      url.pathname ===
        "/jukebox/catalog"
    ) {
      const response =
        await handleJukeboxCatalog(
          request,
          env,
          url
        );

      return addCorsToResponse(
        response,
        origin
      );
    }


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * MAIN TELEVISION PAGE
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * HTML is defined in a later part.
     */

    return new Response(
      HTML,
      {
        headers: {
          "content-type":
            "text/html; charset=UTF-8",

          "cache-control":
            "no-store"
        }
      }
    );
  }
};

  "recall-thy-name-v1";


/*
 * We deliberately use the same GPT model family already

const SHOW_YEAR =
  1985;


/*
 * The historical calendar is intentionally trapped
 * inside 1985.
 *
 * December 31, 1985 does NOT proceed to 1986.
 *
 * It returns to:
 *
 * January 1, 1985.
 */

const SHOW_CALENDAR_START =
  "1985-01-01";

  365;

 * Hostility begins after:
 *
 * 1 hour 35 minutes
 */

const HOSTILITY_START_SECONDS =
  (
    1 * 60 * 60
  ) +
  (
    35 * 60
  );

 * Physical combat begins after:
 *
 * 2 hours
 */

  2 * 60 * 60;

 * Major Nameless Man escalation occurs every:
 *
 * 12 combat hours
 */

const MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS =
  12 * 60 * 60;

 * Every major escalation:
 *
 * +100 levels
 * +3 new high-tier damage spells
 */

const MAJOR_LEVEL_GAIN =
  100;


const MAJOR_SPELL_GAIN =
  3;


// =========================================================
// WATCH STATISTICS
// =========================================================

/*
 * These two statistics are permanent:
 *
 * NUMBER OF EPISODES WATCHED
 *
 * NUMBER OF HOURS WATCHING
 *
 * The second value is stored internally as seconds.
 *
 * Display examples:
 *
 * 412 hours, 47 minutes, 6 seconds
 * [17 days, 4 hours, 47 minutes, 6 seconds]
 */

const WATCH_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS =
  10;


/*
 * If the heartbeat disappears for too long,
 * stop awarding continuous viewing time.
 *
 * This prevents closing the browser and returning
 * three days later from becoming three days of
 * "watching."
 */

const WATCH_HEARTBEAT_GRACE_SECONDS =
  35;

// =========================================================
// COSMETIC PRICE PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * THIS IS IMPORTANT:
 *
 * A weak host wearing expensive sneakers may cost:
 *
 *     5,000 gold
 *
 * A naked God-Titan capable of dominating the
 * Nameless Man for years may cost:
 *
 *     1,000 gold
 *
 * That is intentional.
 *
 * Price primarily represents:
 *
 * - cosmetics;
 * - clothing;
 * - luxury;
 * - novelty;
 * - rarity;
 * - presentation;
 * - absurdity;
 * - collectability.
 *
 * Raw combat effectiveness can influence price,
 * but MUST NOT dominate it.
 *
 * The Host Store is casting.
 *
 * It is NOT a linear difficulty ladder.
 */

// =========================================================
// DEFAULT HOST
// =========================================================

/*
 * Before the Host Store exists or before the player
 * replaces anyone, we need one valid initial host.
 *
 * [NAME_VAR] will later be generated more elaborately.
 */

function createDefaultHost() {
  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  return {
    id:
      crypto.randomUUID(),

    name:
      "NAME_VAR",

    sex:
      "female",

    species:
      "human",

    archetype:
      "television-host",

    appearance: {
      hair:
        "brown",

      hairstyle:
        "1985 television hair",

      clothing:
        "CRYPT NETWORK studio clothes",

      footwear:
        "ordinary shoes",

      accessories:
        []
    },

    cosmetics: {
      rarity:
        "common",

      cosmeticValue:
        0,

      description:
        "The original CRYPT NETWORK host."
    },

    combat: {
      level:
        1,

      hp:
        100,

      maxHp:
        100,

      attack:
        10,

      defense:
        10,

      magic:
        0,

      resistance:
        5,

      speed:
        10,

      canLevel:
        false,

      alive:
        true,

      damageInfinity:
        false,

      spells:
        [],

      statusEffects:
        []
    },

    store: {
      purchasePrice:
        0,

      candidateId:
        null,

      hiredAt:
        now
    },

    relationship: {
      affectionTowardNameless:
        0,

      irritationTowardNameless:
        0,

      fearOfNameless:
        5,

      respectForNameless:
        0
    },

    createdAt:
      now,

    updatedAt:
      now
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT NAMELESS MAN STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultNamelessState() {
  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  return {
    id:
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

    /*
     * He does NOT receive a canonical name.
     */

    canonicalName:
      null,

    currentTemporaryName:
      null,

    invincible:
      true,

    combat: {
      level:
        1,

      xp:
        0,

      hp:
        100,

      maxHp:
        100,

      attack:
        8,

      defense:
        8,

      magic:
        4,

      resistance:
        8,

      speed:
        10,

      spells:
        [],

      statusEffects:
        [],

      majorEscalations:
        0,

      totalCombatActions:
        0,

      totalHostsKilled:
        0
    },

    createdAt:
      now,

    updatedAt:
      now
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT WATCH STATISTICS
// =========================================================

function createDefaultWatchStats() {
  return {
    episodesWatched:
      0,

    totalWatchSeconds:
      0,

    currentEpisodeStartedAt:
      null,

    currentViewingStartedAt:
      null,

    lastHeartbeatAt:
      null,

    lastAccountedAt:
      null
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT HISTORICAL CALENDAR STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultCalendarState() {
  return {
    /*
     * Day zero is:
     *
     * January 1, 1985.
     */

    dayIndex:
      0,

    currentDate:
      SHOW_CALENDAR_START,

    lastRealDateAdvanceKey:
      null,

    discussedEventIds:
      []
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT CONVERSATION STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultConversationState() {
  return {
    mode:
      "conversation",

    currentTopic:
      null,

    previousTopic:
      null,

    lockType:
      LOCK_NONE,

    locked:
      false,

    activeSpeaker:
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

    lastSpeaker:
      null,

    awaitingTtsCompletion:
      false,

    audienceInterruptPending:
      false,

    turnNumber:
      0,

    currentEpisodeTurnNumber:
      0,

    longTermSummary:
      "",

    lastModeChangeAt:
      null,

    lastTopicChangeAt:
      null
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT COMBAT STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultCombatState() {
  return {
    active:
      false,

    startedAt:
      null,

    elapsedCombatSeconds:
      0,

    lastAccountedAt:
      null,

    nextMajorEscalationAtSeconds:
      MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS,

    currentAttacker:
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

    actionNumber:
      0,

    stalemate:
      false,

    lastAttack:
      null,

    lastDamage:
      0,

    lastSpell:
      null
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT HOST STORE STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultHostStoreState() {
  return {
    candidates:
      [],

    generatedAt:
      null,

    generationNumber:
      0,

    lastHiredCandidateId:
      null
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT JUKEBOX STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultJukeboxState() {
  return {
    enabled:
      true,

    currentAlbumId:
      null,

    currentSongId:
      null,

    currentSongTitle:
      null,

    playing:
      false,

    positionSeconds:
      0,

    volume:
      1,

    shuffle:
      false,

    repeat:
      false
  };
}


// =========================================================
// COMPLETE DEFAULT RECALL STATE
// =========================================================

function createDefaultRecallState() {
  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  return {
    version:
      RECALL_ENGINE_VERSION,

    television: {
      poweredOn:
        false,

      showState:
        SHOW_STATE_OFF,

      showStartedAt:
        null,

      elapsedShowSeconds:
        0,

      lastShowTimeAccountedAt:
        null,

      hostilityStarted:
        false,

      combatTriggered:
        false
    },

    host:
      createDefaultHost(),

    nameless:
      createDefaultNamelessState(),

    watch:
      createDefaultWatchStats(),

    calendar:
      createDefaultCalendarState(),

    conversation:
      createDefaultConversationState(),

    combat:
      createDefaultCombatState(),

    hostStore:
      createDefaultHostStoreState(),

    jukebox:
      createDefaultJukeboxState(),

    createdAt:
      now,

    updatedAt:
      now
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DATABASE ACCESS
// =========================================================

function requireWorldDatabase(
  env
) {
  if (!env.WORLD_DB) {
    throw new Error(
      'Missing D1 binding. Add the shared Loki\'s Kingdom database as "WORLD_DB".'
    );
  }

  return env.WORLD_DB;
}


// =========================================================
// DATABASE CREATION
// =========================================================

async function ensureRecallDatabase(
  env
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db.batch([
    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * MASTER SHOW STATE
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_show_state (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        state_json TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * CONVERSATION LOG
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_conversation_log (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        episode_number INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        speaker TEXT NOT NULL,
        speaker_name TEXT,
        content TEXT NOT NULL,
        conversation_mode TEXT,
        topic TEXT,
        lock_type TEXT,
        show_state TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_conversation_visitor
      ON recall_conversation_log(
        visitor_id,
        id
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * DURABLE MEMORIES
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_memories (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        character_key TEXT NOT NULL,
        summary TEXT NOT NULL,
        importance INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_memories_visitor_character
      ON recall_memories(
        visitor_id,
        character_key,
        id
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * EPISODE HISTORY
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_episodes (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        episode_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
        host_id TEXT,
        host_name TEXT,
        started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        ended_at TEXT,
        historical_date TEXT,
        opening_topic TEXT,
        combat_started INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        host_died INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_episodes_visitor
      ON recall_episodes(
        visitor_id,
        episode_number
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * HOST ARCHIVE
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Dead or former hosts remain historically real.
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_host_archive (
        host_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        host_name TEXT NOT NULL,
        host_json TEXT NOT NULL,
        hired_at TEXT,
        died_at TEXT,
        death_episode INTEGER,
        cause_of_death TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_host_archive_visitor
      ON recall_host_archive(
        visitor_id,
        created_at
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * HOST STORE SNAPSHOTS
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * The seven candidates remain stable until the store
     * is intentionally rerolled or consumed.
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_host_store (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        generation_number INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        candidates_json TEXT NOT NULL,
        generated_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * HISTORICAL 1985 EVENT CACHE
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Later parts may populate this manually or through
     * controlled historical retrieval.
     *
     * We store original factual summaries, not copied
     * newspaper articles.
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_1985_events (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        date_key TEXT NOT NULL,
        category TEXT NOT NULL,
        title TEXT NOT NULL,
        summary TEXT NOT NULL,
        source_name TEXT,
        source_reference TEXT,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    db.prepare(`
      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS
      idx_recall_1985_events_date
      ON recall_1985_events(
        date_key,
        id
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * DAILY EVENT DISCUSSION HISTORY
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_event_history (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        visitor_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        event_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
        date_key TEXT NOT NULL,
        episode_number INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        discussed_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * WATCH HEARTBEATS
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * This supports server-authoritative accumulated
     * viewing statistics.
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_watch_sessions (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        session_started_at TEXT,
        last_heartbeat_at TEXT,
        accumulated_seconds REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `),


    /*
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     * REQUEST LOCK
     * -------------------------------------------------------
     *
     * Prevent overlapping AI turns for one visitor.
     *
     * Especially important because autonomous dialogue can
     * otherwise race itself when the browser fires multiple
     * completion events.
     */

    db.prepare(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS recall_request_locks (
        visitor_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
        locked_until_ms INTEGER NOT NULL,
        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL
      )
    `)
  ]);
}


// =========================================================
// STATE NORMALIZATION — SMALL GENERIC HELPERS
// =========================================================

function finiteNumber(
  value,
  fallback = 0
) {
  const number =
    Number(value);

  return Number.isFinite(
    number
  )
    ? number
    : fallback;
}


function integerNumber(
  value,
  fallback = 0
) {
  return Math.floor(
    finiteNumber(
      value,
      fallback
    )
  );
}


function clamp(
  value,
  minimum,
  maximum
) {
  return Math.min(
    maximum,
    Math.max(
      minimum,
      value
    )
  );
}


function normalizeBoolean(
  value,
  fallback = false
) {
  return typeof value ===
    "boolean"
      ? value
      : fallback;
}


function normalizeString(
  value,
  fallback = "",
  maxLength = 10_000
) {
  const text =
    typeof value ===
      "string"
      ? value
      : fallback;

  return text
    .slice(
      0,
      maxLength
    );
}


function validIsoDate(
  value
) {
  if (
    typeof value !==
      "string"
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const timestamp =
    Date.parse(
      value
    );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      timestamp
    )
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return new Date(
    timestamp
  )
    .toISOString();
}


// =========================================================
// VISITOR ID NORMALIZATION
// =========================================================

function normalizeVisitorId(
  input
) {
  const value =
    String(
      input || ""
    )
      .trim();

  if (
    value.length < 8 ||
    value.length > 200
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  if (
    !/^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+$/
      .test(
        value
      )
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return value;
}


// =========================================================
// SHARED PLAYER WORLD STATE
// =========================================================

/*
 * recall thy name uses the SAME world_state wallet
 * as Loki's Kingdom's other residents.
 *
 * We do not create a second fake pile of gold.
 */

function createFallbackWorldState() {
  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  return {
    player: {
      hp:
        100,

      maxHp:
        100,

      hunger:
        0,

      money:
        0,

      conditions:
        [],

      inventory:
        [],

      equipment:
        {},

      spellsKnown:
        []
    },

    lastUpdatedAt:
      now
  };
}


// =========================================================
// RECALL STATE NORMALIZATION
// =========================================================

function normalizeRecallState(
  candidate
) {
  const fallback =
    createDefaultRecallState();

  const source =
    candidate &&
    typeof candidate ===
      "object"
      ? candidate
      : {};

  const televisionSource =
    source.television &&
    typeof source.television ===
      "object"
      ? source.television
      : {};

  const watchSource =
    source.watch &&
    typeof source.watch ===
      "object"
      ? source.watch
      : {};

  const calendarSource =
    source.calendar &&
    typeof source.calendar ===
      "object"
      ? source.calendar
      : {};

  const conversationSource =
    source.conversation &&
    typeof source.conversation ===
      "object"
      ? source.conversation
      : {};

  const combatSource =
    source.combat &&
    typeof source.combat ===
      "object"
      ? source.combat
      : {};

  const hostStoreSource =
    source.hostStore &&
    typeof source.hostStore ===
      "object"
      ? source.hostStore
      : {};

  const jukeboxSource =
    source.jukebox &&
    typeof source.jukebox ===
      "object"
      ? source.jukebox
      : {};

  /*
   * More detailed host/nameless normalization is added
   * in the Host Store and combat parts.
   *
   * For now preserve valid object state or use defaults.
   */

  const host =
    source.host &&
    typeof source.host ===
      "object"
      ? source.host
      : fallback.host;

  const nameless =
    source.nameless &&
    typeof source.nameless ===
      "object"
      ? source.nameless
      : fallback.nameless;

  const allowedShowStates =
    new Set([
      SHOW_STATE_OFF,
      SHOW_STATE_CONVERSATION,
      SHOW_STATE_HOSTILITY,
      SHOW_STATE_COMBAT,
      SHOW_STATE_HOST_DEAD,
      SHOW_STATE_HOST_STORE
    ]);

  const showState =
    allowedShowStates.has(
      televisionSource.showState
    )
      ? televisionSource.showState
      : fallback
          .television
          .showState;

  const lockType =
    (
      conversationSource
        .lockType ===
          LOCK_NONE ||
      LOCK_TYPES.includes(
        conversationSource
          .lockType
      )
    )
      ? conversationSource
          .lockType
      : LOCK_NONE;

  return {
    version:
      RECALL_ENGINE_VERSION,

    television: {
      poweredOn:
        normalizeBoolean(
          televisionSource
            .poweredOn,
          false
        ),

      showState,

      showStartedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          televisionSource
            .showStartedAt
        ),

      elapsedShowSeconds:
        Math.max(
          0,
          finiteNumber(
            televisionSource
              .elapsedShowSeconds,
            0
          )
        ),

      lastShowTimeAccountedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          televisionSource
            .lastShowTimeAccountedAt
        ),

      hostilityStarted:
        normalizeBoolean(
          televisionSource
            .hostilityStarted,
          false
        ),

      combatTriggered:
        normalizeBoolean(
          televisionSource
            .combatTriggered,
          false
        )
    },

    host,

    nameless,

    watch: {
      episodesWatched:
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            watchSource
              .episodesWatched,
            0
          )
        ),

      totalWatchSeconds:
        Math.max(
          0,
          finiteNumber(
            watchSource
              .totalWatchSeconds,
            0
          )
        ),

      currentEpisodeStartedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          watchSource
            .currentEpisodeStartedAt
        ),

      currentViewingStartedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          watchSource
            .currentViewingStartedAt
        ),

      lastHeartbeatAt:
        validIsoDate(
          watchSource
            .lastHeartbeatAt
        ),

      lastAccountedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          watchSource
            .lastAccountedAt
        )
    },

    calendar: {
      dayIndex:
        clamp(
          integerNumber(
            calendarSource
              .dayIndex,
            0
          ),
          0,
          SHOW_CALENDAR_DAYS - 1
        ),

      currentDate:
        normalizeString(
          calendarSource
            .currentDate,
          SHOW_CALENDAR_START,
          10
        ),

      lastRealDateAdvanceKey:
        normalizeString(
          calendarSource
            .lastRealDateAdvanceKey,
          "",
          20
        ) || null,

      discussedEventIds:
        Array.isArray(
          calendarSource
            .discussedEventIds
        )
          ? calendarSource
              .discussedEventIds
              .slice(
                -200
              )
          : []
    },

    conversation: {
      mode:
        CONVERSATION_MODES
          .includes(
            conversationSource
              .mode
          )
            ? conversationSource
                .mode
            : "conversation",

      currentTopic:
        normalizeString(
          conversationSource
            .currentTopic,
          "",
          500
        ) || null,

      previousTopic:
        normalizeString(
          conversationSource
            .previousTopic,
          "",
          500
        ) || null,

      lockType,

      locked:
        lockType !==
          LOCK_NONE,

      activeSpeaker:
        (
          conversationSource
            .activeSpeaker ===
              CHARACTER_HOST
        )
          ? CHARACTER_HOST
          : CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

      lastSpeaker:
        (
          conversationSource
            .lastSpeaker ===
              CHARACTER_HOST ||
          conversationSource
            .lastSpeaker ===
              CHARACTER_NAMELESS ||
          conversationSource
            .lastSpeaker ===
              CHARACTER_AUDIENCE
        )
          ? conversationSource
              .lastSpeaker
          : null,

      awaitingTtsCompletion:
        normalizeBoolean(
          conversationSource
            .awaitingTtsCompletion,
          false
        ),

      audienceInterruptPending:
        normalizeBoolean(
          conversationSource
            .audienceInterruptPending,
          false
        ),

      turnNumber:
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            conversationSource
              .turnNumber,
            0
          )
        ),

      currentEpisodeTurnNumber:
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            conversationSource
              .currentEpisodeTurnNumber,
            0
          )
        ),

      longTermSummary:
        normalizeString(
          conversationSource
            .longTermSummary,
          "",
          MAX_LONG_TERM_SUMMARY_LENGTH
        ),

      lastModeChangeAt:
        validIsoDate(
          conversationSource
            .lastModeChangeAt
        ),

      lastTopicChangeAt:
        validIsoDate(
          conversationSource
            .lastTopicChangeAt
        )
    },

    combat: {
      active:
        normalizeBoolean(
          combatSource
            .active,
          false
        ),

      startedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          combatSource
            .startedAt
        ),

      elapsedCombatSeconds:
        Math.max(
          0,
          finiteNumber(
            combatSource
              .elapsedCombatSeconds,
            0
          )
        ),

      lastAccountedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          combatSource
            .lastAccountedAt
        ),

      nextMajorEscalationAtSeconds:
        Math.max(
          MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS,
          finiteNumber(
            combatSource
              .nextMajorEscalationAtSeconds,
            MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS
          )
        ),

      currentAttacker:
        combatSource
          .currentAttacker ===
            CHARACTER_HOST
          ? CHARACTER_HOST
          : CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

      actionNumber:
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            combatSource
              .actionNumber,
            0
          )
        ),

      stalemate:
        normalizeBoolean(
          combatSource
            .stalemate,
          false
        ),

      lastAttack:
        combatSource
          .lastAttack ??
        null,

      lastDamage:
        Math.max(
          0,
          finiteNumber(
            combatSource
              .lastDamage,
            0
          )
        ),

      lastSpell:
        combatSource
          .lastSpell ??
        null
    },

    hostStore: {
      candidates:
        Array.isArray(
          hostStoreSource
            .candidates
        )
          ? hostStoreSource
              .candidates
              .slice(
                0,
                HOST_STORE_CANDIDATE_COUNT
              )
          : [],

      generatedAt:
        validIsoDate(
          hostStoreSource
            .generatedAt
        ),

      generationNumber:
        Math.max(
          0,
          integerNumber(
            hostStoreSource
              .generationNumber,
            0
          )
        ),

      lastHiredCandidateId:
        normalizeString(
          hostStoreSource
            .lastHiredCandidateId,
          "",
          200
        ) || null
    },

    jukebox: {
      enabled:
        normalizeBoolean(
          jukeboxSource
            .enabled,
          true
        ),

      currentAlbumId:
        normalizeString(
          jukeboxSource
            .currentAlbumId,
          "",
          500
        ) || null,

      currentSongId:
        normalizeString(
          jukeboxSource
            .currentSongId,
          "",
          500
        ) || null,

      currentSongTitle:
        normalizeString(
          jukeboxSource
            .currentSongTitle,
          "",
          1000
        ) || null,

      playing:
        normalizeBoolean(
          jukeboxSource
            .playing,
          false
        ),

      positionSeconds:
        Math.max(
          0,
          finiteNumber(
            jukeboxSource
              .positionSeconds,
            0
          )
        ),

      volume:
        clamp(
          finiteNumber(
            jukeboxSource
              .volume,
            1
          ),
          0,
          1
        ),

      shuffle:
        normalizeBoolean(
          jukeboxSource
            .shuffle,
          false
        ),

      repeat:
        normalizeBoolean(
          jukeboxSource
            .repeat,
          false
        )
    },

    createdAt:
      validIsoDate(
        source.createdAt
      ) ||
      fallback.createdAt,

    updatedAt:
      validIsoDate(
        source.updatedAt
      ) ||
      fallback.updatedAt
  };
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD RECALL STATE
// =========================================================

async function loadRecallState(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  await ensureRecallDatabase(
    env
  );

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const row =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          state_json,
          created_at,
          updated_at

        FROM recall_show_state

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId
      )
      .first();

  if (!row) {
    const state =
      createDefaultRecallState();

    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

    return state;
  }

  let parsed =
    null;

  try {
    parsed =
      JSON.parse(
        row.state_json
      );
  } catch {
    parsed =
      null;
  }

  const state =
    normalizeRecallState(
      parsed
    );

  return state;
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE RECALL STATE
// =========================================================

async function saveRecallState(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  const normalized =
    normalizeRecallState(
      {
        ...state,

        updatedAt:
          now
      }
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_show_state (
        visitor_id,
        state_json,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)

      ON CONFLICT(visitor_id)
      DO UPDATE SET

        state_json =
          excluded.state_json,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      JSON.stringify(
        normalized
      ),
      normalized.createdAt ||
        now,
      now
    )
    .run();

  return normalized;
}


// =========================================================
// SHARED WORLD STATE — LOAD
// =========================================================

async function loadSharedWorldState(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  /*
   * Other Loki's Kingdom systems already use world_state.
   *
   * Do not replace or recreate the table here.
   */

  try {
    const row =
      await db
        .prepare(`
          SELECT state_json

          FROM world_state

          WHERE visitor_id = ?

          LIMIT 1
        `)
        .bind(
          visitorId
        )
        .first();

    if (!row) {
      return createFallbackWorldState();
    }

    let parsed;

    try {
      parsed =
        JSON.parse(
          row.state_json
        );
    } catch {
      return createFallbackWorldState();
    }

    if (
      !parsed ||
      typeof parsed !==
        "object"
    ) {
      return createFallbackWorldState();
    }

    return parsed;

  } catch (error) {
    /*
     * The TV can still display development information
     * if an old test database does not yet have world_state.
     *
     * Host purchasing later will require the real shared
     * world state.
     */

    console.error(
      "Shared world state load failed:",
      error
    );

    return createFallbackWorldState();
  }
}


// =========================================================
// PUBLIC PLAYER WALLET
// =========================================================

function getPublicPlayerState(
  worldState
) {
  const player =
    (
      worldState &&
      typeof worldState ===
        "object" &&
      worldState.player &&
      typeof worldState.player ===
        "object"
    )
      ? worldState.player
      : {};

  return {
    hp:
      Math.max(
        0,
        finiteNumber(
          player.hp,
          100
        )
      ),

    maxHp:
      Math.max(
        1,
        finiteNumber(
          player.maxHp,
          100
        )
      ),

    hunger:
      finiteNumber(
        player.hunger,
        0
      ),

    money:
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          player.money,
          0
        )
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// WATCH-TIME FORMATTER
// =========================================================

function formatWatchDuration(
  totalSeconds
) {
  const secondsTotal =
    Math.max(
      0,
      Math.floor(
        finiteNumber(
          totalSeconds,
          0
        )
      )
    );

  const seconds =
    secondsTotal %
    60;

  const totalMinutes =
    Math.floor(
      secondsTotal /
      60
    );

  const minutes =
    totalMinutes %
    60;

  const totalHours =
    Math.floor(
      totalMinutes /
      60
    );

  const days =
    Math.floor(
      totalHours /
      24
    );

  const remainderHours =
    totalHours %
    24;

  return {
    totalSeconds:
      secondsTotal,

    totalHours,

    minutes,

    seconds,

    days,

    remainderHours,

    /*
     * Primary display:
     *
     * 412 hours, 47 minutes, 6 seconds
     */

    hoursDisplay:
      (
        `${totalHours} hours, ` +
        `${minutes} minutes, ` +
        `${seconds} seconds`
      ),

    /*
     * Secondary display:
     *
     * [17 days, 4 hours, 47 minutes, 6 seconds]
     */

    daysDisplay:
      (
        `[${days} days, ` +
        `${remainderHours} hours, ` +
        `${minutes} minutes, ` +
        `${seconds} seconds]`
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// PUBLIC RECALL STATE
// =========================================================

function buildPublicRecallState(
  recallState,
  worldState
) {
  const watchDuration =
    formatWatchDuration(
      recallState
        .watch
        .totalWatchSeconds
    );

  return {
    ok:
      true,

    network:
      SHOW_NETWORK,

    show:
      SHOW_TITLE,

    year:
      SHOW_YEAR,

    player:
      getPublicPlayerState(
        worldState
      ),

    television: {
      poweredOn:
        recallState
          .television
          .poweredOn,

      showState:
        recallState
          .television
          .showState,

      elapsedShowSeconds:
        Math.floor(
          recallState
            .television
            .elapsedShowSeconds
        ),

      hostilityStarted:
        recallState
          .television
          .hostilityStarted,

      combatTriggered:
        recallState
          .television
          .combatTriggered
    },

    statistics: {
      episodesWatched:
        recallState
          .watch
          .episodesWatched,

      totalWatchSeconds:
        watchDuration
          .totalSeconds,

      totalHours:
        watchDuration
          .totalHours,

      hoursDisplay:
        watchDuration
          .hoursDisplay,

      daysDisplay:
        watchDuration
          .daysDisplay
    },

    calendar: {
      currentDate:
        recallState
          .calendar
          .currentDate,

      dayIndex:
        recallState
          .calendar
          .dayIndex
    },

    host:
      recallState.host,

    nameless: {
      /*
       * Do NOT expose a hidden canonical name,
       * because no such game value exists.
       */

      temporaryName:
        recallState
          .nameless
          .currentTemporaryName,

      invincible:
        true,

      combat:
        recallState
          .nameless
          .combat
    },

    conversation:
      recallState
        .conversation,

    combat:
      recallState
        .combat,

    hostStore:
      recallState
        .hostStore,

    jukebox:
      recallState
        .jukebox
  };
}


// =========================================================
// GENERIC RESPONSE HELPERS
// =========================================================

function jsonResponse(
  data,
  status = 200,
  origin = ""
) {
  const headers =
    new Headers({
      "content-type":
        "application/json; charset=UTF-8",

      "cache-control":
        "no-store"
    });

  if (
    isAllowedOrigin(
      origin
    )
  ) {
    headers.set(
      "Access-Control-Allow-Origin",
      origin
    );

    headers.set(
      "Vary",
      "Origin"
    );
  }

  return new Response(
    JSON.stringify(
      data
    ),
    {
      status,
      headers
    }
  );
}


function isAllowedOrigin(
  origin
) {
  return (
    origin ===
      "https://lokiskingdom.com" ||
    origin ===
      "https://www.lokiskingdom.com"
  );
}


function handleCorsPreflight(
  origin
) {
  const headers =
    new Headers({
      "Access-Control-Allow-Methods":
        "GET, POST, OPTIONS",

      "Access-Control-Allow-Headers":
        "Content-Type",

      "Access-Control-Max-Age":
        "86400",

      "Vary":
        "Origin"
    });

  if (
    isAllowedOrigin(
      origin
    )
  ) {
    headers.set(
      "Access-Control-Allow-Origin",
      origin
    );
  }

  return new Response(
    null,
    {
      status:
        204,

      headers
    }
  );
}


function addCorsToResponse(
  response,
  origin
) {
  if (
    !isAllowedOrigin(
      origin
    )
  ) {
    return response;
  }

  const headers =
    new Headers(
      response.headers
    );

  headers.set(
    "Access-Control-Allow-Origin",
    origin
  );

  headers.set(
    "Vary",
    "Origin"
  );

  return new Response(
    response.body,
    {
      status:
        response.status,

      statusText:
        response.statusText,

      headers
    }
  );
}
