// =========================================================
// TORONTO DATE KEY
// =========================================================

/*
 * The game needs a stable real-world date key so that
 * the fictional 1985 calendar advances once per actual
 * Toronto calendar day rather than once every 24 hours
 * from an arbitrary timestamp.
 */

function getTorontoDateKey(
  date = new Date()
) {
  const formatter =
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
      "en-CA",
      {
        timeZone:
          WORLD_TIME_ZONE,

        year:
          "numeric",

        month:
          "2-digit",

        day:
          "2-digit"
      }
    );

  const parts =
    formatter.formatToParts(
      date
    );

  const values =
    {};

  for (
    const part
    of parts
  ) {
    if (
      part.type !==
        "literal"
    ) {
      values[
        part.type
      ] =
        part.value;
    }
  }

  return (
    `${values.year}-` +
    `${values.month}-` +
    `${values.day}`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// 1985 DATE FROM DAY INDEX
// =========================================================

function get1985DateFromDayIndex(
  dayIndex
) {
  const normalizedIndex =
    (
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          dayIndex,
          0
        )
      ) %
      SHOW_CALENDAR_DAYS
    );

  const start =
    Date.UTC(
      1985,
      0,
      1
    );

  const date =
    new Date(
      start +
      (
        normalizedIndex *
        24 *
        60 *
        60 *
        1000
      )
    );

  return date
    .toISOString()
    .slice(
      0,
      10
    );
}


// =========================================================
// HUMAN 1985 DATE
// =========================================================

function format1985Date(
  dateKey
) {
  const parsed =
    new Date(
      `${dateKey}T12:00:00Z`
    );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      parsed.getTime()
    )
  ) {
    return (
      "January 1, 1985"
    );
  }

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
    "en-US",
    {
      timeZone:
        "UTC",

      weekday:
        "long",

      month:
        "long",

      day:
        "numeric",

      year:
        "numeric"
    }
  )
    .format(
      parsed
    );
}


// =========================================================
// ADVANCE FICTIONAL 1985 CALENDAR
// =========================================================

/*
 * The calendar is deliberately impossible.
 *
 * It advances one fictional day whenever a new real
 * Toronto calendar day is observed.
 *
 * Once December 31, 1985 passes:
 *
 *     January 1, 1985
 *
 * begins again.
 *
 * There is no 1986.
 */

function advance1985CalendarIfNeeded(
  state,
  now = new Date()
) {
  const realDateKey =
    getTorontoDateKey(
      now
    );

  const calendar =
    state.calendar;

  /*
   * First observation establishes the real-day anchor.
   *
   * We do NOT immediately skip January 1.
   */

  if (
    !calendar
      .lastRealDateAdvanceKey
  ) {
    calendar
      .lastRealDateAdvanceKey =
        realDateKey;

    calendar.currentDate =
      get1985DateFromDayIndex(
        calendar.dayIndex
      );

    return false;
  }

  if (
    calendar
      .lastRealDateAdvanceKey ===
        realDateKey
  ) {
    calendar.currentDate =
      get1985DateFromDayIndex(
        calendar.dayIndex
      );

    return false;
  }


  /*
   * Determine how many real calendar dates passed.
   *
   * This matters if the television is not visited for
   * several days.
   *
   * The fictional calendar continues to advance with
   * real dates even while the TV is off.
   */

  const previousParts =
    calendar
      .lastRealDateAdvanceKey
      .split("-")
      .map(
        Number
      );

  const currentParts =
    realDateKey
      .split("-")
      .map(
        Number
      );

  let elapsedDays =
    1;

  if (
    previousParts.length ===
      3 &&
    currentParts.length ===
      3
  ) {
    const previousUtc =
      Date.UTC(
        previousParts[0],
        previousParts[1] - 1,
        previousParts[2]
      );

    const currentUtc =
      Date.UTC(
        currentParts[0],
        currentParts[1] - 1,
        currentParts[2]
      );

    const difference =
      Math.floor(
        (
          currentUtc -
          previousUtc
        ) /
        (
          24 *
          60 *
          60 *
          1000
        )
      );

    if (
      Number.isFinite(
        difference
      ) &&
      difference > 0
    ) {
      elapsedDays =
        difference;
    }
  }

  calendar.dayIndex =
    (
      calendar.dayIndex +
      elapsedDays
    ) %
    SHOW_CALENDAR_DAYS;

  calendar.currentDate =
    get1985DateFromDayIndex(
      calendar.dayIndex
    );

  calendar
    .lastRealDateAdvanceKey =
      realDateKey;

  /*
   * Old event IDs need not remain in the immediate
   * "today" pool forever.
   *
   * The permanent database history still remembers
   * what was discussed.
   */

  calendar.discussedEventIds =
    Array.isArray(
      calendar
        .discussedEventIds
    )
      ? calendar
          .discussedEventIds
          .slice(
            -200
          )
      : [];

  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// SHOW ELAPSED TIME ACCOUNTING
// =========================================================

/*
 * Show time is NOT the same thing as permanent watch time.
 *
 * show elapsed time controls:
 *
 *     1:35 hostility
 *     2:00 combat
 *
 * It belongs to the current episode.
 *
 * It only advances while the TV is actively being watched.
 */

function accountShowElapsedTime(
  state,
  now = new Date()
) {
  if (
    !state
      .television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  const lastIso =
    state
      .television
      .lastShowTimeAccountedAt;

  if (!lastIso) {
    state
      .television
      .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
        now.toISOString();

    return 0;
  }

  const previousMs =
    Date.parse(
      lastIso
    );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      previousMs
    )
  ) {
    state
      .television
      .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
        now.toISOString();

    return 0;
  }

  let elapsed =
    (
      now.getTime() -
      previousMs
    ) /
    1000;

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      elapsed
    ) ||
    elapsed <= 0
  ) {
    state
      .television
      .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
        now.toISOString();

    return 0;
  }


  /*
   * Never allow one stale browser request to award an
   * enormous chunk of show time.
   *
   * The heartbeat establishes that the television was
   * actually alive recently.
   */

  const heartbeatMs =
    state
      .watch
      .lastHeartbeatAt
        ? Date.parse(
            state
              .watch
              .lastHeartbeatAt
          )
        : NaN;

  if (
    Number.isFinite(
      heartbeatMs
    )
  ) {
    const heartbeatAge =
      (
        now.getTime() -
        heartbeatMs
      ) /
      1000;

    if (
      heartbeatAge >
        WATCH_HEARTBEAT_GRACE_SECONDS
    ) {
      elapsed =
        0;
    } else {
      elapsed =
        Math.min(
          elapsed,
          WATCH_HEARTBEAT_GRACE_SECONDS
        );
    }
  } else {
    elapsed =
      0;
  }

  state
    .television
    .elapsedShowSeconds +=
      elapsed;

  state
    .television
    .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
      now.toISOString();

  return elapsed;
}


// =========================================================
// WATCH-TIME ACCOUNTING
// =========================================================

/*
 * Permanent viewing statistics.
 *
 * The browser never tells us:
 *
 *     "I watched 10,000 seconds."
 *
 * It merely sends a heartbeat.
 *
 * The server calculates the elapsed interval itself.
 */

function accountWatchTime(
  state,
  now = new Date()
) {
  if (
    !state
      .television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  const watch =
    state.watch;

  const previousIso =
    watch
      .lastAccountedAt;

  if (!previousIso) {
    watch.lastAccountedAt =
      now.toISOString();

    watch.lastHeartbeatAt =
      now.toISOString();

    return 0;
  }

  const previousMs =
    Date.parse(
      previousIso
    );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      previousMs
    )
  ) {
    watch.lastAccountedAt =
      now.toISOString();

    watch.lastHeartbeatAt =
      now.toISOString();

    return 0;
  }

  let elapsed =
    (
      now.getTime() -
      previousMs
    ) /
    1000;

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      elapsed
    ) ||
    elapsed < 0
  ) {
    elapsed =
      0;
  }


  /*
   * A heartbeat arriving after a huge gap proves only
   * that the TV is open NOW.
   *
   * It does not prove the browser was watching throughout
   * the gap.
   */

  elapsed =
    Math.min(
      elapsed,
      WATCH_HEARTBEAT_GRACE_SECONDS
    );

  watch.totalWatchSeconds +=
    elapsed;

  watch.lastAccountedAt =
    now.toISOString();

  watch.lastHeartbeatAt =
    now.toISOString();

  return elapsed;
}


// =========================================================
// SHOW PHASE TRANSITIONS
// =========================================================

/*
 * This updates the deterministic phase boundaries.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * At 1h35 the conversation does not necessarily turn
 * instantly into screaming.
 *
 * It enters HOSTILITY mode, permitting the escalating
 * antagonism system in later parts.
 *
 * At 2h00 the combat system is triggered.
 */

function updateShowPhase(
  state,
  now = new Date()
) {
  const television =
    state.television;

  if (
    !television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    television.showState =
      SHOW_STATE_OFF;

    return {
      hostilityStarted:
        false,

      combatStarted:
        false
    };
  }

  const hostAlive =
    state.host
      ?.combat
      ?.alive !==
        false;

  if (!hostAlive) {
    television.showState =
      SHOW_STATE_HOST_DEAD;

    return {
      hostilityStarted:
        television
          .hostilityStarted,

      combatStarted:
        state
          .combat
          .active
    };
  }

  const elapsed =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        television
          .elapsedShowSeconds,
        0
      )
    );

  let hostilityStartedNow =
    false;

  let combatStartedNow =
    false;


  /*
   * 1 hour 35 minutes.
   */

  if (
    elapsed >=
      HOSTILITY_START_SECONDS &&
    !television
      .hostilityStarted
  ) {
    television
      .hostilityStarted =
        true;

    hostilityStartedNow =
      true;
  }


  /*
   * 2 hours.
   */

  if (
    elapsed >=
      COMBAT_START_SECONDS &&
    !television
      .combatTriggered
  ) {
    television
      .combatTriggered =
        true;

    state
      .combat
      .active =
        true;

    state
      .combat
      .startedAt =
        now.toISOString();

    state
      .combat
      .lastAccountedAt =
        now.toISOString();

    television.showState =
      SHOW_STATE_COMBAT;

    combatStartedNow =
      true;

  } else if (
    state
      .combat
      .active
  ) {
    television.showState =
      SHOW_STATE_COMBAT;

  } else if (
    television
      .hostilityStarted
  ) {
    television.showState =
      SHOW_STATE_HOSTILITY;

  } else {
    television.showState =
      SHOW_STATE_CONVERSATION;
  }

  return {
    hostilityStarted:
      hostilityStartedNow,

    combatStarted:
      combatStartedNow
  };
}


// =========================================================
// SYNCHRONIZE TEMPORAL STATE
// =========================================================

/*
 * Most public endpoints call this before returning state.
 *
 * It keeps:
 *
 * - calendar;
 * - watch time;
 * - show time;
 * - hostility;
 * - combat threshold
 *
 * synchronized.
 */

function synchronizeRecallTime(
  state,
  now = new Date()
) {
  advance1985CalendarIfNeeded(
    state,
    now
  );

  accountWatchTime(
    state,
    now
  );

  accountShowElapsedTime(
    state,
    now
  );

  return updateShowPhase(
    state,
    now
  );
}


// =========================================================
// CREATE EPISODE DATABASE RECORD
// =========================================================

async function createEpisodeRecord(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const episodeNumber =
    state
      .watch
      .episodesWatched;

  const host =
    state.host;

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_episodes (
        visitor_id,
        episode_number,
        host_id,
        host_name,
        started_at,
        historical_date,
        opening_topic,
        combat_started,
        host_died
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 0)
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      episodeNumber,
      host?.id ||
        null,
      host?.name ||
        null,
      now,
      state
        .calendar
        .currentDate,
      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic ||
        null
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// CLOSE EPISODE DATABASE RECORD
// =========================================================

async function closeEpisodeRecord(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const episodeNumber =
    state
      .watch
      .episodesWatched;

  const now =
    new Date()
      .toISOString();

  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_episodes

      SET
        ended_at = ?,
        combat_started = ?,
        host_died = ?

      WHERE
        visitor_id = ?
        AND
        episode_number = ?
        AND
        ended_at IS NULL
    `)
    .bind(
      now,

      state
        .television
        .combatTriggered
          ? 1
          : 0,

      state.host
        ?.combat
        ?.alive ===
          false
          ? 1
          : 0,

      visitorId,
      episodeNumber
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// RESET EPISODE-SCOPED STATE
// =========================================================

/*
 * Turning the television off ends an episode.
 *
 * It does NOT:
 *
 * - resurrect a dead host;
 * - erase memories;
 * - erase Nameless Man XP;
 * - reset Nameless Man level;
 * - erase historical calendar progress;
 * - erase watch statistics;
 * - erase Host Store history;
 * - erase the shared player wallet.
 *
 * The next episode begins with a fresh show clock.
 */

function resetEpisodeScopedState(
  state
) {
  state.television.showStartedAt =
    null;

  state.television.elapsedShowSeconds =
    0;

  state
    .television
    .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
      null;

  state.television.hostilityStarted =
    false;

  state.television.combatTriggered =
    false;


  state.conversation.mode =
    "conversation";

  state.conversation.currentTopic =
    null;

  state.conversation.previousTopic =
    null;

  state.conversation.lockType =
    LOCK_NONE;

  state.conversation.locked =
    false;

  state.conversation.activeSpeaker =
    CHARACTER_NAMELESS;

  state.conversation.lastSpeaker =
    null;

  state
    .conversation
    .awaitingTtsCompletion =
      false;

  state
    .conversation
    .audienceInterruptPending =
      false;

  state
    .conversation
    .currentEpisodeTurnNumber =
      0;


  state.combat.active =
    false;

  state.combat.startedAt =
    null;

  state.combat.elapsedCombatSeconds =
    0;

  state.combat.lastAccountedAt =
    null;

  state
    .combat
    .nextMajorEscalationAtSeconds =
      MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS;

  state.combat.currentAttacker =
    CHARACTER_NAMELESS;

  state.combat.actionNumber =
    0;

  state.combat.stalemate =
    false;

  state.combat.lastAttack =
    null;

  state.combat.lastDamage =
    0;

  state.combat.lastSpell =
    null;


  /*
   * Temporary names are conversational ephemera.
   *
   * They are NOT identity.
   */

  state
    .nameless
    .currentTemporaryName =
      null;
}


// =========================================================
// TURN TV ON
// =========================================================

async function handleTurnTvOn(
  request,
  env
) {
  let body;

  try {
    body =
      await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Invalid television request."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      body?.visitorId ??
      body?.visitor_id
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  await ensureRecallDatabase(
    env
  );

  const [
    state,
    worldState
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadSharedWorldState(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  const now =
    new Date();

  advance1985CalendarIfNeeded(
    state,
    now
  );


  /*
   * If the TV is already on, this is merely a state
   * recovery request.
   *
   * Do not create another episode.
   */

  if (
    state
      .television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    synchronizeRecallTime(
      state,
      now
    );

    const saved =
      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );

    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ...buildPublicRecallState(
          saved,
          worldState
        ),

        alreadyOn:
          true
      }
    );
  }


  /*
   * A dead host cannot magically return because the TV
   * was switched off and back on.
   *
   * The Host Store must resolve that condition.
   */

  if (
    state.host
      ?.combat
      ?.alive ===
        false
  ) {
    state
      .television
      .poweredOn =
        true;

    state
      .television
      .showState =
        SHOW_STATE_HOST_STORE;

    state
      .watch
      .lastHeartbeatAt =
        now.toISOString();

    state
      .watch
      .lastAccountedAt =
        now.toISOString();

    const saved =
      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );

    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ...buildPublicRecallState(
          saved,
          worldState
        ),

        requiresHost:
          true
      }
    );
  }


  /*
   * NEW EPISODE.
   */

  state
    .watch
    .episodesWatched +=
      1;

  state
    .watch
    .currentEpisodeStartedAt =
      now.toISOString();

  state
    .watch
    .currentViewingStartedAt =
      now.toISOString();

  state
    .watch
    .lastHeartbeatAt =
      now.toISOString();

  state
    .watch
    .lastAccountedAt =
      now.toISOString();


  state
    .television
    .poweredOn =
      true;

  state
    .television
    .showState =
      SHOW_STATE_CONVERSATION;

  state
    .television
    .showStartedAt =
      now.toISOString();

  state
    .television
    .elapsedShowSeconds =
      0;

  state
    .television
    .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
      now.toISOString();

  state
    .television
    .hostilityStarted =
      false;

  state
    .television
    .combatTriggered =
      false;


  state
    .conversation
    .mode =
      "conversation";

  state
    .conversation
    .currentEpisodeTurnNumber =
      0;

  state
    .conversation
    .activeSpeaker =
      Math.random() <
        0.5
          ? CHARACTER_NAMELESS
          : CHARACTER_HOST;

  state
    .conversation
    .lastSpeaker =
      null;

  state
    .conversation
    .awaitingTtsCompletion =
      false;

  state
    .conversation
    .audienceInterruptPending =
      false;


  /*
   * Combat from a PREVIOUS episode does not continue
   * unless future game logic deliberately establishes
   * otherwise.
   *
   * The Nameless Man's permanent progression DOES.
   */

  state.combat =
    createDefaultCombatState();


  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

  await createEpisodeRecord(
    env,
    visitorId,
    saved
  );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ...buildPublicRecallState(
        saved,
        worldState
      ),

      episodeStarted:
        true,

      episodeNumber:
        saved
          .watch
          .episodesWatched,

      dateDisplay:
        format1985Date(
          saved
            .calendar
            .currentDate
        )
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// TURN TV OFF
// =========================================================

async function handleTurnTvOff(
  request,
  env
) {
  let body;

  try {
    body =
      await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Invalid television request."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      body?.visitorId ??
      body?.visitor_id
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const [
    state,
    worldState
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadSharedWorldState(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  const now =
    new Date();


  /*
   * Account the final legitimate viewing interval.
   */

  if (
    state
      .television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    synchronizeRecallTime(
      state,
      now
    );

    await closeEpisodeRecord(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );
  }


  state
    .television
    .poweredOn =
      false;

  state
    .television
    .showState =
      SHOW_STATE_OFF;

  state
    .watch
    .currentViewingStartedAt =
      null;

  state
    .watch
    .lastHeartbeatAt =
      null;

  state
    .watch
    .lastAccountedAt =
      null;


  /*
   * Preserve permanent state while clearing the episode.
   */

  resetEpisodeScopedState(
    state
  );


  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ...buildPublicRecallState(
        saved,
        worldState
      ),

      televisionTurnedOff:
        true
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HEARTBEAT
// =========================================================

async function handleWatchHeartbeat(
  request,
  env
) {
  let body;

  try {
    body =
      await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Invalid heartbeat."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      body?.visitorId ??
      body?.visitor_id
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const state =
    await loadRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  if (
    !state
      .television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          true,

        poweredOn:
          false,

        countedSeconds:
          0
      }
    );
  }

  const now =
    new Date();


  /*
   * accountShowElapsedTime() checks the PREVIOUS heartbeat,
   * so it must execute before accountWatchTime() replaces
   * lastHeartbeatAt with "now".
   */

  const showSeconds =
    accountShowElapsedTime(
      state,
      now
    );

  const countedSeconds =
    accountWatchTime(
      state,
      now
    );

  advance1985CalendarIfNeeded(
    state,
    now
  );

  const phaseChange =
    updateShowPhase(
      state,
      now
    );


  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

  const duration =
    formatWatchDuration(
      saved
        .watch
        .totalWatchSeconds
    );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok:
        true,

      poweredOn:
        true,

      countedSeconds,

      showSeconds,

      elapsedShowSeconds:
        Math.floor(
          saved
            .television
            .elapsedShowSeconds
        ),

      showState:
        saved
          .television
          .showState,

      hostilityStartedNow:
        phaseChange
          .hostilityStarted,

      combatStartedNow:
        phaseChange
          .combatStarted,

      statistics: {
        episodesWatched:
          saved
            .watch
            .episodesWatched,

        totalWatchSeconds:
          duration
            .totalSeconds,

        hoursDisplay:
          duration
            .hoursDisplay,

        daysDisplay:
          duration
            .daysDisplay
      }
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// STATE ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleState(
  request,
  env,
  url
) {
  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitorId"
        ) ??
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitor_id"
        )
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const [
    state,
    worldState
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadSharedWorldState(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  const now =
    new Date();

  synchronizeRecallTime(
    state,
    now
  );

  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ...buildPublicRecallState(
        saved,
        worldState
      ),

      dateDisplay:
        format1985Date(
          saved
            .calendar
            .currentDate
        )
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// TODAY-IN-1985 EVENT LOADING
// =========================================================

async function loadHistoricalEventsForDate(
  env,
  dateKey
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          date_key,
          category,
          title,
          summary,
          source_name,
          source_reference

        FROM recall_1985_events

        WHERE date_key = ?

        ORDER BY id ASC
      `)
      .bind(
        dateKey
      )
      .all();

  return Array.isArray(
    result?.results
  )
    ? result.results
    : [];
}


// =========================================================
// BUILT-IN DAILY TOPIC FALLBACK
// =========================================================

/*
 * The historical-event database will eventually contain
 * specific day-level material.
 *
 * Until a date has entries, the show still needs something
 * period-appropriate to discuss.
 *
 * These are topic categories, NOT fabricated claims that a
 * specific historical event happened on a specific date.
 */

function build1985FallbackTopics(
  dateKey
) {
  const dateDisplay =
    format1985Date(
      dateKey
    );

  return [
    {
      id:
        `fallback:${dateKey}:news`,

      category:
        "news",

      title:
        "The day's news",

      summary:
        (
          "Discuss what people living on " +
          dateDisplay +
          " might consider important news, while refusing " +
          "to invent a specific historical event unless " +
          "factual event data has been supplied."
        ),

      factualEvent:
        false
    },

    {
      id:
        `fallback:${dateKey}:culture`,

      category:
        "culture",

      title:
        "Life in 1985",

      summary:
        (
          "Discuss ordinary culture, entertainment, " +
          "technology, fashion, habits, anxieties, or " +
          "social life from the perspective of people " +
          "who believe the year is 1985."
        ),

      factualEvent:
        false
    },

    {
      id:
        `fallback:${dateKey}:future`,

      category:
        "forward-vision",

      title:
        "The impossible future",

      summary:
        (
          "The Nameless Man may speak with inexplicable " +
          "knowledge of events, inventions, culture, or " +
          "history beyond 1985. The host may interpret " +
          "this as prediction, insanity, performance, " +
          "prophecy, or something impossible."
        ),

      factualEvent:
        false
    }
  ];
}


// =========================================================
// TODAY-IN-1985 ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleTodayIn1985(
  request,
  env,
  url
) {
  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitorId"
        ) ??
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitor_id"
        )
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const state =
    await loadRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  advance1985CalendarIfNeeded(
    state
  );

  const dateKey =
    state
      .calendar
      .currentDate;

  const events =
    await loadHistoricalEventsForDate(
      env,
      dateKey
    );

  await saveRecallState(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok:
        true,

      date:
        dateKey,

      dateDisplay:
        format1985Date(
          dateKey
        ),

      events:
        events.length > 0
          ? events.map(
              (event) => ({
                id:
                  event.id,

                category:
                  event.category,

                title:
                  event.title,

                summary:
                  event.summary,

                factualEvent:
                  true
              })
            )
          : build1985FallbackTopics(
              dateKey
            )
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// TEMPORARY NAME SELECTION
// =========================================================

function chooseTemporaryName(
  previousName = null
) {
  if (
    NAMELESS_TEMPORARY_NAMES
      .length ===
        0
  ) {
    return "John";
  }

  if (
    NAMELESS_TEMPORARY_NAMES
      .length ===
        1
  ) {
    return (
      NAMELESS_TEMPORARY_NAMES[
        0
      ]
    );
  }

  let chosen =
    null;

  for (
    let attempt = 0;
    attempt < 10;
    attempt += 1
  ) {
    const index =
      Math.floor(
        Math.random() *
        NAMELESS_TEMPORARY_NAMES
          .length
      );

    chosen =
      NAMELESS_TEMPORARY_NAMES[
        index
      ];

    if (
      chosen !==
        previousName
    ) {
      break;
    }
  }

  return chosen ||
    NAMELESS_TEMPORARY_NAMES[
      0
    ];
}


// =========================================================
// ASSIGN TEMPORARY NAME
// =========================================================

function assignTemporaryNamelessName(
  state
) {
  const previous =
    state
      .nameless
      .currentTemporaryName;

  const next =
    chooseTemporaryName(
      previous
    );

  state
    .nameless
    .currentTemporaryName =
      next;

  state
    .nameless
    .updatedAt =
      new Date()
        .toISOString();

  return next;
}


// =========================================================
// CONVERSATION MODE SELECTION
// =========================================================

function chooseConversationMode(
  currentMode = null
) {
  if (
    CONVERSATION_MODES
      .length ===
        0
  ) {
    return "conversation";
  }

  let mode =
    currentMode;

  for (
    let attempt = 0;
    attempt < 10;
    attempt += 1
  ) {
    const index =
      Math.floor(
        Math.random() *
        CONVERSATION_MODES
          .length
      );

    const candidate =
      CONVERSATION_MODES[
        index
      ];

    if (
      candidate !==
        currentMode
    ) {
      mode =
        candidate;

      break;
    }
  }

  return mode ||
    "conversation";
}


// =========================================================
// RANDOM COMBAT ANNOUNCEMENT
// =========================================================

function chooseCombatAnnouncement() {
  if (
    COMBAT_ANNOUNCEMENTS
      .length ===
        0
  ) {
    return (
      "Total Carnage!"
    );
  }

  const index =
    Math.floor(
      Math.random() *
      COMBAT_ANNOUNCEMENTS
        .length
    );

  return (
    COMBAT_ANNOUNCEMENTS[
      index
    ]
  );
}


// =========================================================
// RANDOM UTILITY
// =========================================================

function randomInteger(
  minimum,
  maximum
) {
  const min =
    Math.ceil(
      minimum
    );

  const max =
    Math.floor(
      maximum
    );

  if (
    max <= min
  ) {
    return min;
  }

  return (
    Math.floor(
      Math.random() *
      (
        max -
        min +
        1
      )
    ) +
    min
  );
}


function randomChoice(
  array
) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      array
    ) ||
    array.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return array[
    Math.floor(
      Math.random() *
      array.length
    )
  ];
}


// =========================================================
// WEIGHTED RANDOM UTILITY
// =========================================================

function weightedChoice(
  entries
) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      entries
    ) ||
    entries.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const normalized =
    entries
      .map(
        (entry) => ({
          value:
            entry.value,

          weight:
            Math.max(
              0,
              finiteNumber(
                entry.weight,
                0
              )
            )
        })
      )
      .filter(
        (entry) =>
          entry.weight >
            0
      );

  if (
    normalized.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return entries[
      0
    ]?.value ??
      null;
  }

  const total =
    normalized
      .reduce(
        (
          sum,
          entry
        ) =>
          sum +
          entry.weight,
        0
      );

  let cursor =
    Math.random() *
    total;

  for (
    const entry
    of normalized
  ) {
    cursor -=
      entry.weight;

    if (
      cursor <= 0
    ) {
      return entry.value;
    }
  }

  return normalized[
    normalized.length -
    1
  ].value;
}


// =========================================================
// RANDOM ID
// =========================================================

function createGameId(
  prefix
) {
  return (
    `${prefix}-` +
    crypto.randomUUID()
  );
}


// =========================================================
// SAFE JSON PARSER
// =========================================================

function safeJsonParse(
  value,
  fallback = null
) {
  if (
    typeof value !==
      "string" ||
    !value
  ) {
    return fallback;
  }

  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      value
    );
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// UNIX / ISO TIME HELPERS
// =========================================================

function isoNow() {
  return new Date()
    .toISOString();
}


function secondsBetween(
  earlier,
  later = new Date()
) {
  const earlierMs =
    typeof earlier ===
      "string"
      ? Date.parse(
          earlier
        )
      : (
          earlier instanceof
            Date
            ? earlier.getTime()
            : NaN
        );

  const laterMs =
    typeof later ===
      "string"
      ? Date.parse(
          later
        )
      : (
          later instanceof
            Date
            ? later.getTime()
            : NaN
        );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      earlierMs
    ) ||
    !Number.isFinite(
      laterMs
    )
  ) {
    return 0;
  }

  return Math.max(
    0,
    (
      laterMs -
      earlierMs
    ) /
    1000
  );
}
