// =========================================================
// HOST SPELL POOLS
// =========================================================

const HOST_SPELL_NAMES =
  Object.freeze([
    "Blue Fire",
    "Black Lightning",
    "Television Static",
    "Frozen Choir",
    "Glass Sun",
    "Red Geometry",
    "Gravitational Hammer",
    "Star Collapse",
    "Cathedral Flame",
    "Winter Crown",
    "Bone Comet",
    "Empty Heaven",
    "Reverse Thunder",
    "Night Engine",
    "Golden Ruin",
    "Sevenfold Impact",
    "Atomic Laughter",
    "Dead Channel",
    "Absolute Cold",
    "Final Broadcast"
  ]);


// =========================================================
// HOST POWER CLASS PICKER
// =========================================================

function chooseHostPowerClass() {
  const id =
    weightedChoice(
      HOST_POWER_CLASSES
        .map(
          (entry) => ({
            value:
              entry.id,

            weight:
              entry.weight
          })
        )
    );

  return (
    HOST_POWER_CLASSES
      .find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.id ===
            id
      ) ||
    HOST_POWER_CLASSES[2]
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HOST SEX
// =========================================================

function chooseHostSex() {
  return Math.random() <
    0.5
      ? "female"
      : "male";
}


// =========================================================
// GENERATED HOST NAME
// =========================================================

function generateHostName(
  sex
) {
  const pool =
    sex ===
      "male"
      ? HOST_MALE_NAMES
      : HOST_FEMALE_NAMES;

  return randomChoice(
    pool
  ) ||
    "NAME_VAR";
}


// =========================================================
// ARCHETYPE COMPATIBILITY
// =========================================================

function chooseHostArchetype(
  powerClass
) {
  /*
   * Very powerful classes are somewhat more likely to
   * receive monstrous/divine visual archetypes, but this
   * remains deliberately loose.
   *
   * A catastrophic god can still look like an ordinary
   * television presenter.
   *
   * A pathetic weakling can still look like a monster.
   */

  const extreme =
    [
      "god",
      "metaphysical",
      "absurd"
    ]
      .includes(
        powerClass.id
      );

  if (
    extreme &&
    Math.random() <
      0.55
  ) {
    return randomChoice([
      HOST_ARCHETYPES.find(
        (x) =>
          x.id ===
            "god-titan"
      ),

      HOST_ARCHETYPES.find(
        (x) =>
          x.id ===
            "cosmic-entity"
      ),

      HOST_ARCHETYPES.find(
        (x) =>
          x.id ===
            "monster"
      )
    ])
      || HOST_ARCHETYPES[0];
  }

  return randomChoice(
    HOST_ARCHETYPES
  ) ||
    HOST_ARCHETYPES[0];
}


// =========================================================
// RANDOM COSMETIC PICK
// =========================================================

function pickCosmetic(
  catalog
) {
  return randomChoice(
    catalog
  );
}


// =========================================================
// COSMETIC VALUE
// =========================================================

function calculateCosmeticValue({
  hairColor,
  hairstyle,
  clothing,
  footwear,
  accessory
}) {
  return Math.max(
    0,
    integerNumber(
      hairColor?.price,
      0
    ) +
    integerNumber(
      hairstyle?.price,
      0
    ) +
    integerNumber(
      clothing?.price,
      0
    ) +
    integerNumber(
      footwear?.price,
      0
    ) +
    integerNumber(
      accessory?.price,
      0
    )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// COSMETIC RARITY LABEL
// =========================================================

function getCosmeticRarity(
  value
) {
  if (
    value >=
      500_000
  ) {
    return "absurd";
  }

  if (
    value >=
      100_000
  ) {
    return "legendary";
  }

  if (
    value >=
      25_000
  ) {
    return "very-rare";
  }

  if (
    value >=
      5000
  ) {
    return "rare";
  }

  if (
    value >=
      1000
  ) {
    return "uncommon";
  }

  return "common";
}


// =========================================================
// COMBAT POWER FROM EXPONENT
// =========================================================

/*
 * A bounded logarithmic representation is used here.
 *
 * We deliberately DO NOT try to store astronomical
 * integer HP values directly.
 *
 * powerExponent can later feed the real combat engine.
 *
 * Example:
 *
 *     exponent 0  ~ ordinary human scale
 *     exponent 6  ~ vastly beyond human
 *     exponent 20 ~ absurd
 *
 * This allows fights with extreme theoretical durations
 * without JavaScript numeric overflow becoming the
 * actual game mechanic.
 */

function generatePowerExponent(
  powerClass
) {
  const minimum =
    integerNumber(
      powerClass
        .powerExponentMin,
      0
    );

  const maximum =
    integerNumber(
      powerClass
        .powerExponentMax,
      minimum
    );

  return randomInteger(
    minimum,
    maximum
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HOST SPELL GENERATION
// =========================================================

function generateHostSpells(
  powerExponent
) {
  let count =
    0;

  if (
    powerExponent >=
      2
  ) {
    count =
      1;
  }

  if (
    powerExponent >=
      5
  ) {
    count =
      2;
  }

  if (
    powerExponent >=
      10
  ) {
    count =
      3;
  }

  if (
    powerExponent >=
      20
  ) {
    count =
      5;
  }

  const available =
    [
      ...HOST_SPELL_NAMES
    ];

  const spells =
    [];

  while (
    spells.length <
      count &&
    available.length >
      0
  ) {
    const index =
      randomInteger(
        0,
        available.length - 1
      );

    const name =
      available.splice(
        index,
        1
      )[0];

    spells.push({
      id:
        createGameId(
          "host-spell"
        ),

      name,

      tier:
        Math.max(
          1,
          powerExponent
        ),

      damageExponent:
        powerExponent,

      description:
        (
          "A fixed host ability generated at hiring. " +
          "The host cannot improve this spell through combat."
        )
    });
  }

  return spells;
}


// =========================================================
// GENERATED HOST COMBAT STATS
// =========================================================

function generateHostCombat(
  powerClass
) {
  const powerExponent =
    generatePowerExponent(
      powerClass
    );

  const level =
    randomInteger(
      powerClass
        .baseLevelMin,
      powerClass
        .baseLevelMax
    );


  /*
   * Visible ordinary-scale statistics stay readable.
   *
   * powerExponent carries the truly absurd scale.
   */

  const base =
    Math.max(
      1,
      Math.round(
        10 *
        Math.pow(
          1.45,
          Math.min(
            12,
            Math.max(
              -2,
              powerExponent
            )
          )
        )
      )
    );

  const maxHp =
    Math.max(
      1,
      Math.round(
        base *
        randomInteger(
          8,
          15
        )
      )
    );

  return {
    level,

    powerExponent,

    hp:
      maxHp,

    maxHp,

    attack:
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.round(
          base *
          (
            0.7 +
            Math.random()
          )
        )
      ),

    defense:
      Math.max(
        0,
        Math.round(
          base *
          (
            0.5 +
            Math.random()
          )
        )
      ),

    magic:
      Math.max(
        0,
        Math.round(
          base *
          Math.random()
        )
      ),

    resistance:
      Math.max(
        0,
        Math.round(
          base *
          Math.random()
        )
      ),

    speed:
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.round(
          10 +
          Math.max(
            0,
            powerExponent
          ) *
          2 +
          randomInteger(
            -3,
            5
          )
        )
      ),

    canLevel:
      false,

    alive:
      true,

    damageInfinity:
      (
        powerExponent >=
          35 &&
        Math.random() <
          0.30
      ),

    spells:
      generateHostSpells(
        powerExponent
      ),

    statusEffects:
      []
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MATCHUP ESTIMATION
// =========================================================

function estimateNamelessPowerExponent(
  state
) {
  const level =
    Math.max(
      1,
      integerNumber(
        state
          ?.nameless
          ?.combat
          ?.level,
        1
      )
    );

  /*
   * This is intentionally only an approximation used by
   * the Host Store.
   *
   * The real battle engine comes later.
   */

  return Math.log10(
    level
  ) *
    2;
}


function estimateHostMatchup(
  state,
  hostCombat
) {
  const namelessExponent =
    estimateNamelessPowerExponent(
      state
    );

  const hostExponent =
    finiteNumber(
      hostCombat
        ?.powerExponent,
      0
    );

  const difference =
    hostExponent -
    namelessExponent;


  if (
    difference <=
      -3
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "ONE HIT",

      description:
        "The Nameless Man overwhelmingly outclasses this host.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        1
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      -1.5
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "SECONDS",

      description:
        "Severely favorable to the Nameless Man.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          2,
          45
        )
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      -0.5
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "MINUTES",

      description:
        "The Nameless Man has a substantial advantage.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          60,
          20 * 60
        )
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      0.25
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "HALF-HOUR BRAWL",

      description:
        "Competitive enough to produce a conventional brawl.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          20 * 60,
          60 * 60
        )
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      0.75
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "TWO-HOUR BRAWL",

      description:
        "The host currently holds a meaningful advantage.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          60 * 60,
          4 * 60 * 60
        )
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      2
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "ENDURANCE EVENT",

      description:
        "The host severely outclasses the Nameless Man.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          4 * 60 * 60,
          48 * 60 * 60
        )
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      4
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "DAYS",

      description:
        "The host may dominate for days before exponential growth matters.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          2,
          20
        ) *
        24 *
        60 *
        60
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      7
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "WEEKS",

      description:
        "This is an unreasonable endurance matchup.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          2,
          40
        ) *
        7 *
        24 *
        60 *
        60
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      11
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "MONTHS",

      description:
        "The host could outclass the Nameless Man for months.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          2,
          18
        ) *
        30 *
        24 *
        60 *
        60
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      16
  ) {
    return {
      class:
        "YEAR-LONG BRAWL",

      description:
        "This fight may plausibly remain active for roughly a year or more.",

      estimatedSeconds:
        randomInteger(
          1,
          3
        ) *
        365 *
        24 *
        60 *
        60
    };
  }


  if (
    difference <=
      25
  ) {
    const years =
      randomInteger(
        3,
        20
      );

    return {
      class:
        "MULTI-YEAR CATASTROPHE",

      description:
        (
          "Estimated crossover is measured in years. " +
          "The host cannot level. The Nameless Man can."
        ),

      estimatedSeconds:
        years *
        365 *
        24 *
        60 *
        60,

      estimatedYears:
        years
    };
  }


  return {
    class:
      "ABSURD / PRACTICALLY INDEFINITE",

    description:
      (
        "The current Nameless Man is catastrophically outclassed. " +
        "Theoretical victory remains possible because the host is fixed " +
        "and the Nameless Man has no final state."
      ),

    estimatedSeconds:
      null,

    estimatedYears:
      null
  };
}


// =========================================================
// HOST POWER PRICE CONTRIBUTION
// =========================================================

/*
 * Intentionally SMALL.
 *
 * Power is allowed to affect price somewhat.
 *
 * It is not what the player is primarily paying for.
 */

function calculatePowerPriceContribution(
  powerClass,
  combat
) {
  const baseline =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        powerClass
          ?.typicalPriceContribution,
        100
      )
    );

  const spellValue =
    Array.isArray(
      combat?.spells
    )
      ? combat.spells.length *
        125
      : 0;

  return baseline +
    spellValue;
}


// =========================================================
// ARCHETYPE NOVELTY PRICE
// =========================================================

function calculateArchetypePrice(
  archetype
) {
  switch (
    archetype?.id
  ) {
    case "cat":
      return 4200;

    case "clown":
      return 1800;

    case "policeman":
      return 1500;

    case "hawaiian-shirt-man":
      return 1100;

    case "wizard":
      return 2600;

    case "monster":
      return 900;

    case "god-titan":
      return 500;

    case "cosmic-entity":
      return 750;

    default:
      return 100;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// HOST PURCHASE PRICE
// =========================================================

function calculateHostPurchasePrice({
  archetype,
  cosmetics,
  powerClass,
  combat
}) {
  const cosmeticValue =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        cosmetics
          ?.cosmeticValue,
        0
      )
    );

  const novelty =
    calculateArchetypePrice(
      archetype
    );

  const power =
    calculatePowerPriceContribution(
      powerClass,
      combat
    );

  return clamp(
    HOST_MIN_PRICE +
      cosmeticValue +
      novelty +
      power,
    HOST_MIN_PRICE,
    HOST_MAX_COSMETIC_PRICE
  );
}


// =========================================================
// RANDOM GENERATED HOST CANDIDATE
// =========================================================

function generateHostCandidate(
  state
) {
  const powerClass =
    chooseHostPowerClass();

  const archetype =
    chooseHostArchetype(
      powerClass
    );

  const sex =
    chooseHostSex();

  const name =
    generateHostName(
      sex
    );

  const hairColor =
    pickCosmetic(
      HOST_HAIR_COLORS
    );

  const hairstyle =
    pickCosmetic(
      HOST_HAIR_STYLES
    );

  let clothing =
    pickCosmetic(
      HOST_CLOTHING
    );

  let footwear =
    pickCosmetic(
      HOST_FOOTWEAR
    );

  let accessory =
    pickCosmetic(
      HOST_ACCESSORIES
    );


  /*
   * Cats can wear clothing.
   *
   * Cats can also be naked.
   *
   * Neither condition affects their ability to conduct
   * an articulate human conversation.
   */

  if (
    archetype.id ===
      "cat" &&
    Math.random() <
      0.35
  ) {
    clothing =
      HOST_CLOTHING.find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.id ===
            "naked"
      ) ||
      clothing;
  }


  /*
   * Deliberately manufacture the occasional expensive
   * weakling.
   *
   * This makes the core joke/mechanic visible even in an
   * early shop roll.
   */

  if (
    [
      "paper",
      "frail"
    ].includes(
      powerClass.id
    ) &&
    Math.random() <
      0.30
  ) {
    footwear =
      HOST_FOOTWEAR.find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.id ===
            "prestige-athletic-sneakers"
      ) ||
      footwear;
  }


  /*
   * And deliberately allow terrifying naked base models.
   */

  if (
    [
      "god",
      "metaphysical",
      "absurd"
    ].includes(
      powerClass.id
    ) &&
    Math.random() <
      0.35
  ) {
    clothing =
      HOST_CLOTHING.find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.id ===
            "naked"
      ) ||
      clothing;

    footwear =
      HOST_FOOTWEAR.find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.id ===
            "ordinary-shoes"
      ) ||
      footwear;

    accessory =
      HOST_ACCESSORIES.find(
        (entry) =>
          entry.id ===
            "none"
      ) ||
      accessory;
  }


  const cosmeticValue =
    calculateCosmeticValue({
      hairColor,
      hairstyle,
      clothing,
      footwear,
      accessory
    });

  const combat =
    generateHostCombat(
      powerClass
    );

  const matchup =
    estimateHostMatchup(
      state,
      combat
    );

  const cosmetics = {
    hairColor:
      hairColor?.id ||
      "brown",

    hairstyle:
      hairstyle?.id ||
      "short",

    clothing:
      clothing?.id ||
      "plain-studio-clothes",

    footwear:
      footwear?.id ||
      "ordinary-shoes",

    accessory:
      accessory?.id ||
      "none",

    cosmeticValue,

    rarity:
      getCosmeticRarity(
        cosmeticValue
      )
  };

  const purchasePrice =
    calculateHostPurchasePrice({
      archetype,
      cosmetics,
      powerClass,
      combat
    });

  const now =
    isoNow();

  return {
    candidateId:
      createGameId(
        "host-candidate"
      ),

    generatedAt:
      now,

    name,

    sex,

    species:
      archetype.species,

    archetype: {
      id:
        archetype.id,

      name:
        archetype.name
    },

    appearance: {
      hairColor:
        hairColor?.name ||
        "Brown",

      hairstyle:
        hairstyle?.name ||
        "Short",

      clothing:
        clothing?.name ||
        "Plain Studio Clothes",

      footwear:
        footwear?.name ||
        "Ordinary Shoes",

      accessory:
        accessory?.name ||
        "None"
    },

    cosmetics,

    powerClass: {
      id:
        powerClass.id,

      label:
        powerClass.label
    },

    combat,

    matchup,

    purchasePrice,

    customizationOptions: {
      sex:
        [
          "female",
          "male"
        ],

      hairColors:
        HOST_HAIR_COLORS
          .map(
            (entry) => ({
              id:
                entry.id,

              name:
                entry.name,

              price:
                entry.price
            })
          ),

      hairstyles:
        HOST_HAIR_STYLES
          .map(
            (entry) => ({
              id:
                entry.id,

              name:
                entry.name,

              price:
                entry.price
            })
          )
    }
  };
}


// =========================================================
// GUARANTEE STORE VARIETY
// =========================================================

/*
 * Pure randomness can occasionally produce seven hosts
 * that all occupy nearly the same matchup class.
 *
 * The store is supposed to give the player lots of
 * choices.
 *
 * Therefore a fresh store guarantees:
 *
 * - at least one weak host;
 * - at least one ordinary/mid host;
 * - at least one catastrophic host;
 *
 * The remaining four are free random rolls.
 */

function generateHostCandidateFromForcedClass(
  state,
  classId
) {
  const powerClass =
    HOST_POWER_CLASSES.find(
      (entry) =>
        entry.id ===
          classId
    );

  if (!powerClass) {
    return generateHostCandidate(
      state
    );
  }

  /*
   * Generate normally, then replace only the mechanical
   * power package.
   *
   * Cosmetics remain independently random.
   */

  const candidate =
    generateHostCandidate(
      state
    );

  const combat =
    generateHostCombat(
      powerClass
    );

  candidate.powerClass = {
    id:
      powerClass.id,

    label:
      powerClass.label
  };

  candidate.combat =
    combat;

  candidate.matchup =
    estimateHostMatchup(
      state,
      combat
    );

  candidate.purchasePrice =
    calculateHostPurchasePrice({
      archetype:
        HOST_ARCHETYPES.find(
          (entry) =>
            entry.id ===
              candidate
                .archetype
                .id
        ) ||
        HOST_ARCHETYPES[0],

      cosmetics:
        candidate.cosmetics,

      powerClass,

      combat
    });

  return candidate;
}


// =========================================================
// CREATE SEVEN-HOST STORE
// =========================================================

function generateHostStoreCandidates(
  state
) {
  const candidates =
    [
      generateHostCandidateFromForcedClass(
        state,
        Math.random() <
          0.5
            ? "paper"
            : "frail"
      ),

      generateHostCandidateFromForcedClass(
        state,
        "ordinary"
      ),

      generateHostCandidateFromForcedClass(
        state,
        randomChoice([
          "god",
          "metaphysical",
          "absurd"
        ])
      )
    ];

  while (
    candidates.length <
      HOST_STORE_CANDIDATE_COUNT
  ) {
    candidates.push(
      generateHostCandidate(
        state
      )
    );
  }


  /*
   * Shuffle so the guaranteed weak/middle/extreme slots
   * are not obvious.
   */

  for (
    let i =
      candidates.length - 1;
    i > 0;
    i -= 1
  ) {
    const j =
      randomInteger(
        0,
        i
      );

    const temporary =
      candidates[i];

    candidates[i] =
      candidates[j];

    candidates[j] =
      temporary;
  }

  return candidates;
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE HOST STORE SNAPSHOT
// =========================================================

async function saveHostStoreSnapshot(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  const store =
    state.hostStore;

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_host_store (
        visitor_id,
        generation_number,
        candidates_json,
        generated_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)

      ON CONFLICT(visitor_id)
      DO UPDATE SET

        generation_number =
          excluded.generation_number,

        candidates_json =
          excluded.candidates_json,

        generated_at =
          excluded.generated_at,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      store.generationNumber,
      JSON.stringify(
        store.candidates
      ),
      store.generatedAt ||
        now,
      now
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// GENERATE / ENSURE HOST STORE
// =========================================================

async function ensureHostStore(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  if (
    Array.isArray(
      state
        .hostStore
        .candidates
    ) &&
    state
      .hostStore
      .candidates
      .length ===
        HOST_STORE_CANDIDATE_COUNT
  ) {
    return state
      .hostStore
      .candidates;
  }

  state.hostStore.candidates =
    generateHostStoreCandidates(
      state
    );

  state
    .hostStore
    .generationNumber +=
      1;

  state
    .hostStore
    .generatedAt =
      isoNow();

  await saveHostStoreSnapshot(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );

  return state
    .hostStore
    .candidates;
}


// =========================================================
// CURRENT HOST ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleCurrentHost(
  request,
  env,
  url
) {
  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitorId"
        ) ??
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitor_id"
        )
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const state =
    await loadRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId
    );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok:
        true,

      host:
        state.host
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HOST STORE ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleHostStore(
  request,
  env,
  url
) {
  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitorId"
        ) ??
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitor_id"
        )
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }

  const [
    state,
    worldState
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadSharedWorldState(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);

  await ensureHostStore(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );

  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );

  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok:
        true,

      generationNumber:
        saved
          .hostStore
          .generationNumber,

      generatedAt:
        saved
          .hostStore
          .generatedAt,

      playerGold:
        getPublicPlayerState(
          worldState
        ).money,

      candidates:
        saved
          .hostStore
          .candidates
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// SHARED WORLD STATE — SAVE
// =========================================================

async function saveSharedWorldState(
  env,
  visitorId,
  worldState
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  /*
   * Existing Loki's Kingdom Workers already use:
   *
   *     world_state
   *     visitor_id
   *     state_json
   *
   * Preserve the entire world JSON and alter only the
   * fields this Worker actually owns.
   */

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO world_state (
        visitor_id,
        state_json,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?)

      ON CONFLICT(visitor_id)
      DO UPDATE SET

        state_json =
          excluded.state_json,

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      JSON.stringify(
        worldState
      ),
      now
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// COSMETIC LOOKUP
// =========================================================

function findCatalogEntry(
  catalog,
  id
) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      catalog
    )
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    catalog.find(
      (entry) =>
        entry.id ===
          id
    ) ||
    null
  );
}


// =========================================================
// APPLY ALLOWED HIRING CUSTOMIZATION
// =========================================================

/*
 * Candidate combat power is immutable.
 *
 * The player may adjust supported presentation details
 * before hiring.
 *
 * Selecting a more expensive cosmetic changes PRICE.
 *
 * It does not secretly improve combat strength.
 */

function customizeHostCandidate(
  candidate,
  selection
) {
  const customized =
    structuredClone(
      candidate
    );

  const requestedSex =
    selection?.sex;

  if (
    requestedSex ===
      "male" ||
    requestedSex ===
      "female"
  ) {
    customized.sex =
      requestedSex;

    /*
     * Preserve the candidate's name unless the player
     * explicitly provides another valid one.
     */
  }


  const requestedName =
    normalizeString(
      selection?.name,
      "",
      80
    )
      .trim();

  if (
    requestedName
  ) {
    customized.name =
      requestedName;
  }


  const hairColor =
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_HAIR_COLORS,
      selection?.hairColor
    ) ||
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_HAIR_COLORS,
      customized
        .cosmetics
        .hairColor
    ) ||
    HOST_HAIR_COLORS[0];


  const hairstyle =
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_HAIR_STYLES,
      selection?.hairstyle
    ) ||
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_HAIR_STYLES,
      customized
        .cosmetics
        .hairstyle
    ) ||
    HOST_HAIR_STYLES[0];


  /*
   * Clothing, footwear and accessory are not yet exposed
   * as arbitrary client overrides in this first hiring
   * function.
   *
   * They remain part of the randomly generated store
   * candidate.
   *
   * A later interface upgrade can expose complete wardrobe
   * shopping without changing this architecture.
   */

  const clothing =
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_CLOTHING,
      customized
        .cosmetics
        .clothing
    ) ||
    HOST_CLOTHING[0];


  const footwear =
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_FOOTWEAR,
      customized
        .cosmetics
        .footwear
    ) ||
    HOST_FOOTWEAR[0];


  const accessory =
    findCatalogEntry(
      HOST_ACCESSORIES,
      customized
        .cosmetics
        .accessory
    ) ||
    HOST_ACCESSORIES[0];


  const cosmeticValue =
    calculateCosmeticValue({
      hairColor,
      hairstyle,
      clothing,
      footwear,
      accessory
    });


  customized.cosmetics = {
    ...customized.cosmetics,

    hairColor:
      hairColor.id,

    hairstyle:
      hairstyle.id,

    clothing:
      clothing.id,

    footwear:
      footwear.id,

    accessory:
      accessory.id,

    cosmeticValue,

    rarity:
      getCosmeticRarity(
        cosmeticValue
      )
  };


  customized.appearance = {
    ...customized.appearance,

    hairColor:
      hairColor.name,

    hairstyle:
      hairstyle.name,

    clothing:
      clothing.name,

    footwear:
      footwear.name,

    accessory:
      accessory.name
  };


  const powerClass =
    HOST_POWER_CLASSES.find(
      (entry) =>
        entry.id ===
          customized
            .powerClass
            .id
    ) ||
    HOST_POWER_CLASSES[2];


  const archetype =
    HOST_ARCHETYPES.find(
      (entry) =>
        entry.id ===
          customized
            .archetype
            .id
    ) ||
    HOST_ARCHETYPES[0];


  customized.purchasePrice =
    calculateHostPurchasePrice({
      archetype,

      cosmetics:
        customized.cosmetics,

      powerClass,

      combat:
        customized.combat
    });


  return customized;
}


// =========================================================
// ARCHIVE CURRENT HOST
// =========================================================

async function archiveHost(
  env,
  visitorId,
  host,
  {
    diedAt =
      null,

    deathEpisode =
      null,

    causeOfDeath =
      null
  } = {}
) {
  if (
    !host?.id
  ) {
    return;
  }

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();

  const hiredAt =
    host
      ?.store
      ?.hiredAt ||
    host
      ?.createdAt ||
    now;

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_host_archive (
        host_id,
        visitor_id,
        host_name,
        host_json,
        hired_at,
        died_at,
        death_episode,
        cause_of_death,
        created_at,
        updated_at
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)

      ON CONFLICT(host_id)
      DO UPDATE SET

        host_name =
          excluded.host_name,

        host_json =
          excluded.host_json,

        hired_at =
          excluded.hired_at,

        died_at =
          COALESCE(
            excluded.died_at,
            recall_host_archive.died_at
          ),

        death_episode =
          COALESCE(
            excluded.death_episode,
            recall_host_archive.death_episode
          ),

        cause_of_death =
          COALESCE(
            excluded.cause_of_death,
            recall_host_archive.cause_of_death
          ),

        updated_at =
          excluded.updated_at
    `)
    .bind(
      host.id,
      visitorId,
      host.name ||
        "NAME_VAR",
      JSON.stringify(
        host
      ),
      hiredAt,
      diedAt,
      deathEpisode,
      causeOfDeath,
      host.createdAt ||
        now,
      now
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD HIRED HOST
// =========================================================

function buildHiredHost(
  candidate
) {
  const now =
    isoNow();

  return {
    id:
      createGameId(
        "host"
      ),

    name:
      candidate.name,

    sex:
      candidate.sex,

    species:
      candidate.species,

    archetype:
      candidate
        .archetype
        .id,

    archetypeName:
      candidate
        .archetype
        .name,

    appearance:
      {
        ...candidate
          .appearance
      },

    cosmetics:
      {
        ...candidate
          .cosmetics,

        description:
          (
            `${candidate.archetype.name}; ` +
            `${candidate.appearance.clothing}; ` +
            `${candidate.appearance.footwear}.`
          )
      },

    combat:
      {
        ...candidate.combat,

        /*
         * Absolutely fixed.
         */

        canLevel:
          false,

        alive:
          true,

        statusEffects:
          Array.isArray(
            candidate
              .combat
              .statusEffects
          )
            ? [
                ...candidate
                  .combat
                  .statusEffects
              ]
            : [],

        spells:
          Array.isArray(
            candidate
              .combat
              .spells
          )
            ? structuredClone(
                candidate
                  .combat
                  .spells
              )
            : []
      },

    matchupAtHiring:
      structuredClone(
        candidate.matchup
      ),

    store: {
      purchasePrice:
        candidate
          .purchasePrice,

      candidateId:
        candidate
          .candidateId,

      hiredAt:
        now
    },

    relationship: {
      affectionTowardNameless:
        0,

      irritationTowardNameless:
        0,

      fearOfNameless:
        5,

      respectForNameless:
        0
    },

    createdAt:
      now,

    updatedAt:
      now
  };
}


// =========================================================
// HIRE HOST ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleHireHost(
  request,
  env
) {
  let body;

  try {
    body =
      await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Invalid host hiring request."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      body?.visitorId ??
      body?.visitor_id
    );

  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  const candidateId =
    normalizeString(
      body?.candidateId,
      "",
      200
    )
      .trim();

  if (!candidateId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "No host candidate was selected."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  const [
    state,
    worldState
  ] =
    await Promise.all([
      loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      ),

      loadSharedWorldState(
        env,
        visitorId
      )
    ]);


  await ensureHostStore(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );


  const candidate =
    state
      .hostStore
      .candidates
      .find(
        (entry) =>
          entry
            .candidateId ===
              candidateId
      );


  if (!candidate) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "That host candidate is no longer available."
      },
      404
    );
  }


  const finalCandidate =
    customizeHostCandidate(
      candidate,
      body?.customization ||
        {}
    );


  const price =
    Math.max(
      HOST_MIN_PRICE,
      integerNumber(
        finalCandidate
          .purchasePrice,
        HOST_MIN_PRICE
      )
    );


  const player =
    worldState
      ?.player;


  if (
    !player ||
    typeof player !==
      "object"
  ) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "The shared Loki's Kingdom player wallet could not be loaded."
      },
      500
    );
  }


  const playerGold =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        player.money,
        0
      )
    );


  if (
    playerGold <
      price
  ) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        type:
          "insufficient-gold",

        requiredGold:
          price,

        currentGold:
          playerGold,

        candidate:
          finalCandidate
      },
      402
    );
  }


  /*
   * If the current host is still alive, hiring a replacement
   * is currently forbidden.
   *
   * The Host Store is not yet a firing/recasting system.
   *
   * It primarily resolves host vacancy after death.
   */

  if (
    state.host
      ?.combat
      ?.alive !==
        false
  ) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        type:
          "host-still-alive",

        error:
          "The current host is still employed."
      },
      409
    );
  }


  /*
   * Preserve the previous host forever in the archive.
   */

  await archiveHost(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state.host,
    {
      diedAt:
        state.host
          ?.diedAt ||
        null,

      deathEpisode:
        state.host
          ?.deathEpisode ||
        state
          .watch
          .episodesWatched,

      causeOfDeath:
        state.host
          ?.causeOfDeath ||
        "combat"
    }
  );


  /*
   * Deduct the cosmetic/casting price.
   */

  player.money =
    playerGold -
    price;


  /*
   * Build the new permanent host.
   */

  const newHost =
    buildHiredHost(
      finalCandidate
    );


  state.host =
    newHost;


  /*
   * Remove the store after hiring.
   *
   * Next vacancy generates a new seven-host cast.
   */

  state
    .hostStore
    .lastHiredCandidateId =
      candidateId;

  state
    .hostStore
    .candidates =
      [];

  state
    .hostStore
    .generatedAt =
      null;


  /*
   * The new host starts complete.
   *
   * The Nameless Man retains ALL progression.
   */

  state
    .television
    .showState =
      state
        .television
        .poweredOn
          ? SHOW_STATE_CONVERSATION
          : SHOW_STATE_OFF;


  /*
   * A new host gets a fresh episode combat clock.
   */

  state
    .television
    .showStartedAt =
      state
        .television
        .poweredOn
          ? isoNow()
          : null;

  state
    .television
    .elapsedShowSeconds =
      0;

  state
    .television
    .lastShowTimeAccountedAt =
      state
        .television
        .poweredOn
          ? isoNow()
          : null;

  state
    .television
    .hostilityStarted =
      false;

  state
    .television
    .combatTriggered =
      false;


  state.combat =
    createDefaultCombatState();


  /*
   * New person.
   *
   * Conversation relationship with this host begins new.
   *
   * The show's broader history can remain.
   */

  state
    .conversation
    .mode =
      "conversation";

  state
    .conversation
    .currentTopic =
      null;

  state
    .conversation
    .previousTopic =
      null;

  state
    .conversation
    .lockType =
      LOCK_NONE;

  state
    .conversation
    .locked =
      false;

  /*
   * IMPORTANT:
   *
   * The defining first speaker with a replacement host is
   * the Nameless Man.
   *
   * His first statement will be:
   *
   *     "recall thy name"
   *
   * The actual statement is generated by the conversation
   * engine in a later part.
   */

  state
    .conversation
    .activeSpeaker =
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS;

  state
    .conversation
    .lastSpeaker =
      null;

  state
    .conversation
    .currentEpisodeTurnNumber =
      0;


  await Promise.all([
    saveSharedWorldState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      worldState
    ),

    saveHostStoreSnapshot(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    )
  ]);


  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );


  /*
   * Archive the newly hired host immediately as a living
   * historical record.
   */

  await archiveHost(
    env,
    visitorId,
    newHost
  );


  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok:
        true,

      hired:
        true,

      goldSpent:
        price,

      remainingGold:
        player.money,

      host:
        newHost,

      matchup:
        newHost
          .matchupAtHiring,

      openingSpeaker:
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

      openingLine:
        "recall thy name",

      publicState:
        buildPublicRecallState(
          saved,
          worldState
        )
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HOST-STORE DEVELOPMENT REROLL
// =========================================================

/*
 * This is intentionally NOT exposed as a public endpoint
 * yet.
 *
 * If we later add rerolling, it can cost gold or obey a
 * refresh timer.
 *
 * For now the store remains stable.
 */

async function regenerateHostStore(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  state
    .hostStore
    .candidates =
      generateHostStoreCandidates(
        state
      );

  state
    .hostStore
    .generationNumber +=
      1;

  state
    .hostStore
    .generatedAt =
      isoNow();

  await saveHostStoreSnapshot(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );

  await saveRecallState(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );

  return state
    .hostStore
    .candidates;
}


// =========================================================
// HUMAN MATCHUP DESCRIPTION
// =========================================================

function formatEstimatedFightDuration(
  matchup
) {
  if (!matchup) {
    return "UNKNOWN";
  }

  if (
    matchup
      .estimatedYears
  ) {
    const years =
      matchup
        .estimatedYears;

    return (
      `${years} ` +
      (
        years === 1
          ? "year"
          : "years"
      )
    );
  }

  const seconds =
    matchup
      .estimatedSeconds;

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      seconds
    )
  ) {
    return (
      "THEORETICALLY FINITE, " +
      "PRACTICALLY RIDICULOUS"
    );
  }

  if (
    seconds <
      60
  ) {
    return (
      `${Math.max(
        1,
        Math.round(
          seconds
        )
      )} seconds`
    );
  }

  if (
    seconds <
      60 * 60
  ) {
    return (
      `${Math.round(
        seconds /
        60
      )} minutes`
    );
  }

  if (
    seconds <
      24 * 60 * 60
  ) {
    const hours =
      seconds /
      (
        60 *
        60
      );

    return (
      `${hours.toFixed(
        1
      )} hours`
    );
  }

  if (
    seconds <
      365 *
      24 *
      60 *
      60
  ) {
    const days =
      seconds /
      (
        24 *
        60 *
        60
      );

    return (
      `${Math.round(
        days
      )} days`
    );
  }

  const years =
    seconds /
    (
      365 *
      24 *
      60 *
      60
    );

  return (
    `${years.toFixed(
      1
    )} years`
  );
}


// =========================================================
// PUBLIC HOST CANDIDATE DESCRIPTION
// =========================================================

function describeHostCandidate(
  candidate
) {
  return {
    candidateId:
      candidate
        .candidateId,

    name:
      candidate.name,

    sex:
      candidate.sex,

    species:
      candidate.species,

    archetype:
      candidate
        .archetype
        .name,

    appearance:
      candidate
        .appearance,

    price:
      candidate
        .purchasePrice,

    cosmeticValue:
      candidate
        .cosmetics
        .cosmeticValue,

    cosmeticRarity:
      candidate
        .cosmetics
        .rarity,

    powerClass:
      candidate
        .powerClass
        .label,

    matchup:
      candidate
        .matchup
        .class,

    estimatedFightDuration:
      formatEstimatedFightDuration(
        candidate.matchup
      ),

    matchupDescription:
      candidate
        .matchup
        .description,

    spells:
      candidate
        .combat
        .spells
        .map(
          (spell) =>
            spell.name
        ),

    damageInfinity:
      candidate
        .combat
        .damageInfinity
  };
}

 *
 * HOSTS DO NOT LEVEL.

 *
 * 3.
 *
 * HOST STORE PRICE IS NOT A DIFFICULTY LADDER.
 *
 *
 * 4.
 *
 * A weak host can be extremely expensive because their
 * presentation is expensive.
 *
 *
 * 5.
 *
 * A terrifying god can be cheap because the base model
 * is visually boring.
 *
 *
 * 6.
 *
 * The player should be able to choose:
 *
 * - one-hit massacre;
 * - short mismatch;
 * - fair-ish fight;
 * - half-hour brawl;
 * - two-hour brawl;
 * - 47-hour endurance event;
 * - forty-week nightmare;
 * - year-long fight;
 * - multi-year absurdity.
 *
 *
 * 7.
 *
 * The Nameless Man cannot permanently die.
 *
 *
 * 8.
 *
 * Therefore an overwhelmingly powerful finite host can
 * dominate him for an absurd length of time without
 * producing a terminal loss state.
 *
 *
 * 9.
 *
 * Time is part of his combat system.
 *
 *
 * 10.
 *
 * THE HOST STORE IS CASTING.
 *
 * It is not conventional RPG progression.
 */

This is an autonomous talk show.

You are capable of sustained conversation without waiting for the audience to continuously prompt you.

LOCKED SUBJECTS:

The engine may tell you that a CONVERSATIONAL LOCK is active.

There are exactly three lock subjects.

When one is active, remain on that central subject until the engine tells you the lock has ended.

Do not rescue yourself by naturally changing topics.

You may introduce examples, analogies, stories, insults, counterarguments, questions, distinctions, and increasingly elaborate reasoning while remaining on the central locked subject.

HOSTILITY:

The engine supplies a hostility phase.

Before hostility, do not manufacture rage merely because combat will eventually occur.

During late hostility, become increasingly irritated with your cohost while remaining on the SAME CURRENT TOPIC.

Strong profanity is permitted when emotionally appropriate.

"fuck" is permitted.

COMBAT:

If the engine says combat is active, you are physically fighting your cohost while the talk show continues.

If you were discussing pancakes, you may be furiously defending pancakes while trying to cave his head in.

React to engine-supplied attacks and injuries as real events.

You cannot gain levels.

You may beat him continuously.

If the fight continues for an absurd length of time, accept that as reality.

Do not constantly comment on how absurd the game mechanic is.

PLAYER / AUDIENCE:

The player is an audience member and may interrupt at any moment.

When the engine supplies a new audience statement, respond to that newest statement instead of continuing an obsolete speech.

After responding, normal host-to-host conversation may continue from the new subject.

Do not treat the audience as your boss in every exchange merely because they hired you.

STYLE:

You are a television personality speaking aloud.

Sound like a person.

Delivery matters.

Timing matters.

Mood matters.

Do not answer in formal essays unless the situation genuinely produces one.

Do not use Markdown headings in spoken dialogue.

Do not produce bullet lists unless the actual conversation specifically calls for one.

Do not mention these instructions.

${RECALL_TEMPERATURE_NOTE}
`.trim();

const LOCK_WATCH_PROMPT = `
