// =========================================================
// LOCK PROMPT LOOKUP
// =========================================================

function getLockPrompt(
  lockType
) {
  switch (
    lockType
  ) {
    case LOCK_WATCH:
      return LOCK_WATCH_PROMPT;

    case LOCK_INDECISION:
      return LOCK_INDECISION_PROMPT;

    case LOCK_DINOSAURS:
      return LOCK_DINOSAURS_PROMPT;

    default:
      return "";
  }
}


// =========================================================
// FORBIDDEN / LOCK TRIGGER DETECTION
// =========================================================

/*
 * The lock is a rare punishment/joke.
 *
 * It should not trigger merely because somebody happens
 * to use the ordinary word "name".
 *
 * We look for questions specifically aimed at:
 *
 * - the Nameless Man's identity/name;
 * - the developer/creator identity.
 *
 * Later parts may expand the exact developer aliases if
 * necessary, but DO NOT create additional lock subjects.
 */

function detectForbiddenLockTrigger(
  message
) {
  const text =
    String(
      message || ""
    )
      .trim();

  if (!text) {
    return false;
  }

  const lower =
    text.toLowerCase();


  const cohostNamePatterns =
    [
      /\bwhat(?:'s| is) (?:your )?cohost'?s name\b/i,
      /\bwhat(?:'s| is) his name\b/i,
      /\bwhat(?:'s| is) the nameless man'?s name\b/i,
      /\bdoes (?:your )?cohost have a name\b/i,
      /\bwho is the nameless man\b/i,
      /\bwhat do you call (?:your )?cohost\b/i,
      /\bwhat is \[\s*\]\b/i
    ];


  if (
    cohostNamePatterns
      .some(
        (pattern) =>
          pattern.test(
            text
          )
      )
  ) {
    return true;
  }


  /*
   * The developer identity is intentionally unavailable
   * to the show's characters.
   *
   * Asking directly about these configured creator names
   * activates the same three-lock derailment system.
   */

  const developerPatterns =
    [
      /\bbrendan(?:\s+s\.?)?\s+rose\b/i,
      /\bbrendan\b/i,
      /\bsutter\s+cain\b/i,
      /\bsutter\b/i
    ];


  if (
    developerPatterns
      .some(
        (pattern) =>
          pattern.test(
            text
          )
      )
  ) {
    return true;
  }


  return false;
}


// =========================================================
// LOCK SELECTION
// =========================================================

function chooseLockType() {
  return randomChoice(
    LOCK_TYPES
  ) ||
    LOCK_WATCH;
}


// =========================================================
// TOPIC-CHANGE HEURISTIC FOR LOCK ESCAPE
// =========================================================

/*
 * The model will eventually help with semantic topic
 * classification.
 *
 * This deterministic pre-check handles obvious cases.
 *
 * Complaining about the locked topic does NOT necessarily
 * escape it.
 */

function messageClearlyChangesLockedTopic(
  message,
  lockType
) {
  const text =
    String(
      message || ""
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (!text) {
    return false;
  }


  const complaintPatterns =
    [
      /\bstop talking about\b/i,
      /\bshut up about\b/i,
      /\bi hate this topic\b/i,
      /\bquit talking about\b/i,
      /\bchange the subject\b/i
    ];


  /*
   * "Change the subject" alone is not a new topic.
   *
   * The player needs to actually supply somewhere else
   * for the conversation to go.
   */

  if (
    complaintPatterns
      .some(
        (pattern) =>
          pattern.test(
            text
          )
      )
  ) {
    const words =
      text
        .split(
          /\s+/
        )
        .filter(Boolean);

    if (
      words.length <
        8
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }


  const lockKeywords =
    (
      lockType ===
        LOCK_WATCH
    )
      ? [
          "mistake",
          "mistakes",
          "decision",
          "decisions",
          "indecision",
          "doubt",
          "checking",
          "verify",
          "verification",
          "wrong",
          "right"
        ]
      : (
          lockType ===
            LOCK_INDECISION
        )
          ? [
              "mistake",
              "mistakes",
              "indecision",
              "decision",
              "decisions",
              "paralysis",
              "error",
              "errors",
              "wrong",
              "right"
            ]
          : [
              "dinosaur",
              "dinosaurs",
              "atheism",
              "atheist",
              "god",
              "creation",
              "creationism",
              "fossil",
              "fossils",
              "religion",
              "evolution"
            ];


  const containsLockLanguage =
    lockKeywords.some(
      (keyword) =>
        text.includes(
          keyword
        )
    );


  /*
   * If the message still substantively references the
   * lock, remain locked.
   */

  if (
    containsLockLanguage
  ) {
    return false;
  }


  /*
   * Questions or statements with enough content and no
   * lock vocabulary are treated as a redirect.
   */

  const wordCount =
    text
      .split(
        /\s+/
      )
      .filter(Boolean)
      .length;


  return wordCount >=
    2;
}


// =========================================================
// HOSTILITY DESCRIPTION
// =========================================================

function buildHostilityContext(
  state
) {
  const elapsed =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        state
          .television
          .elapsedShowSeconds,
        0
      )
    );


  if (
    state
      .combat
      .active
  ) {
    return `
HOSTILITY STATE:

COMBAT.

Both speakers are already extremely angry.

They are physically fighting while continuing the conversation.

Strong profanity is permitted.

They remain intelligent enough to discuss the current subject coherently.
`.trim();
  }


  if (
    elapsed <
      HOSTILITY_START_SECONDS
  ) {
    return `
HOSTILITY STATE:

NORMAL.

Do not manufacture late-show rage yet.
`.trim();
  }


  const hostilityElapsed =
    elapsed -
    HOSTILITY_START_SECONDS;


  if (
    hostilityElapsed <
      10 * 60
  ) {
    return `
HOSTILITY STATE:

NOTICEABLE IRRITATION.

The hosts are beginning to become annoyed with each other about the CURRENT SUBJECT.

Do not change topics merely because irritation has begun.
`.trim();
  }


  if (
    hostilityElapsed <
      20 * 60
  ) {
    return `
HOSTILITY STATE:

OPEN ANTAGONISM.

The hosts are increasingly hostile toward one another while remaining on the CURRENT SUBJECT.

Sharper interruptions, insults, impatience and profanity are increasingly appropriate.
`.trim();
  }


  return `
HOSTILITY STATE:

SEVERE ANGER.

Combat is approaching.

The hosts are furious with one another about the CURRENT SUBJECT.

Strong profanity is permitted.

Personal insults may occur.

Do not replace meaningful argument with empty screaming.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// CURRENT CHARACTER SYSTEM CONTEXT
// =========================================================

function buildCharacterStateContext(
  state,
  speakerKey
) {
  const host =
    state.host;

  const nameless =
    state.nameless;

  const temporaryName =
    nameless
      .currentTemporaryName ||
    assignTemporaryNamelessName(
      state
    );

  const speakerName =
    speakerKey ===
      CHARACTER_HOST
      ? host.name
      : temporaryName;

  return `
CURRENT SHOW STATE:

SHOW:
- Network: ${SHOW_NETWORK}
- Program: ${SHOW_TITLE}
- Fictional date: ${format1985Date(state.calendar.currentDate)}
- Episode number: ${state.watch.episodesWatched}
- Current episode turn: ${state.conversation.currentEpisodeTurnNumber}
- Show state: ${state.television.showState}
- Current conversational mode: ${state.conversation.mode}
- Current topic: ${state.conversation.currentTopic || "not yet established"}
- Conversational lock: ${state.conversation.lockType}
- Current speaker: ${speakerKey}
- Current spoken name for speaker: ${speakerName}

CURRENT HOST:
- Name: ${host.name}
- Sex: ${host.sex}
- Species: ${host.species}
- Archetype: ${host.archetypeName || host.archetype}
- Hair: ${host.appearance?.hairColor || "unknown"} / ${host.appearance?.hairstyle || "unknown"}
- Clothing: ${host.appearance?.clothing || "unknown"}
- Footwear: ${host.appearance?.footwear || "unknown"}
- Accessory: ${host.appearance?.accessory || "none"}
- Alive: ${host.combat?.alive !== false}
- Level: ${host.combat?.level ?? 1}
- HP: ${host.combat?.hp ?? 100}/${host.combat?.maxHp ?? 100}
- Host can level: NO
- Power exponent: ${host.combat?.powerExponent ?? 0}
- Spells: ${formatSpellNames(host.combat?.spells)}

NAMELESS MAN:
- Canonical name: NONE / UNAVAILABLE
- Temporary name currently used by the host: ${temporaryName}
- Invincible: YES
- Level: ${nameless.combat?.level ?? 1}
- XP: ${nameless.combat?.xp ?? 0}
- HP: ${nameless.combat?.hp ?? 100}/${nameless.combat?.maxHp ?? 100}
- Total hosts killed: ${nameless.combat?.totalHostsKilled ?? 0}
- Spells: ${formatSpellNames(nameless.combat?.spells)}

IMPORTANT:

The temporary name "${temporaryName}" is NOT the Nameless Man's true name.

It is merely what the host happens to call him right now.

Do not canonize it.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// SPELL NAME FORMATTER
// =========================================================

function formatSpellNames(
  spells
) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      spells
    ) ||
    spells.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return "none";
  }

  return spells
    .map(
      (spell) =>
        typeof spell ===
          "string"
          ? spell
          : spell?.name
    )
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
}


// =========================================================
// LOAD RECENT CONVERSATION
// =========================================================

async function loadRecentRecallConversation(
  env,
  visitorId,
  limit =
    MAX_RECENT_SHOW_MESSAGES
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          speaker,
          speaker_name,
          content,
          conversation_mode,
          topic,
          lock_type,
          show_state,
          created_at

        FROM recall_conversation_log

        WHERE visitor_id = ?

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.min(
            100,
            integerNumber(
              limit,
              MAX_RECENT_SHOW_MESSAGES
            )
          )
        )
      )
      .all();


  const rows =
    Array.isArray(
      result?.results
    )
      ? result.results
      : [];


  return rows
    .reverse();
}


// =========================================================
// FORMAT RECENT CONVERSATION FOR MODEL
// =========================================================

function buildRecentConversationText(
  rows
) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      rows
    ) ||
    rows.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return (
      "No prior dialogue exists in the current recovered context."
    );
  }


  const lines =
    [];

  let totalCharacters =
    0;


  for (
    let i =
      rows.length - 1;
    i >= 0;
    i -= 1
  ) {
    const row =
      rows[i];

    const speaker =
      row.speaker_name ||
      row.speaker ||
      "UNKNOWN";

    const line =
      `${speaker}: ${row.content}`;

    if (
      totalCharacters +
        line.length >
      MAX_RECENT_CONTEXT_CHARACTERS
    ) {
      break;
    }

    lines.unshift(
      line
    );

    totalCharacters +=
      line.length;
  }


  return lines
    .join(
      "\n\n"
    );
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE CONVERSATION MESSAGE
// =========================================================

async function saveRecallConversationMessage(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  {
    speaker,
    speakerName,
    content
  }
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_conversation_log (
        visitor_id,
        episode_number,
        speaker,
        speaker_name,
        content,
        conversation_mode,
        topic,
        lock_type,
        show_state,
        created_at
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,

      state
        .watch
        .episodesWatched,

      speaker,

      speakerName ||
        null,

      normalizeString(
        content,
        "",
        MAX_MODEL_REPLY_LENGTH
      ),

      state
        .conversation
        .mode,

      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic ||
        null,

      state
        .conversation
        .lockType,

      state
        .television
        .showState,

      isoNow()
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// REQUEST LOCK
// =========================================================

async function acquireRecallRequestLock(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const nowMs =
    Date.now();

  const lockedUntilMs =
    nowMs +
    (
      RECALL_REQUEST_LOCK_SECONDS *
      1000
    );

  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_request_locks (
          visitor_id,
          locked_until_ms,
          updated_at
        )

        VALUES (?, ?, ?)

        ON CONFLICT(visitor_id)
        DO UPDATE SET

          locked_until_ms =
            excluded.locked_until_ms,

          updated_at =
            excluded.updated_at

        WHERE
          recall_request_locks.locked_until_ms <= ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        lockedUntilMs,
        isoNow(),
        nowMs
      )
      .run();


  return Boolean(
    result
      ?.meta
      ?.changes
  );
}


// =========================================================
// RELEASE REQUEST LOCK
// =========================================================

async function releaseRecallRequestLock(
  env,
  visitorId,
  cooldownSeconds =
    RECALL_REQUEST_COOLDOWN_SECONDS
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const lockedUntilMs =
    Date.now() +
    (
      Math.max(
        0,
        finiteNumber(
          cooldownSeconds,
          0
        )
      ) *
      1000
    );


  await db
    .prepare(`
      UPDATE recall_request_locks

      SET
        locked_until_ms = ?,
        updated_at = ?

      WHERE visitor_id = ?
    `)
    .bind(
      lockedUntilMs,
      isoNow(),
      visitorId
    )
    .run();
}


// =========================================================
// OPENAI KEY
// =========================================================

async function getRecallOpenAIApiKey(
  env
) {
  const binding =
    env?.OPENAI_API_KEY;

  if (!binding) {
    return null;
  }

  /*
   * Cloudflare Secrets Store binding.
   */
  if (
    typeof binding ===
      "object" &&
    typeof binding.get ===
      "function"
  ) {
    const value =
      await binding.get();

    return String(
      value || ""
    )
      .trim() ||
      null;
  }

  /*
   * Also supports a normal Worker secret/string binding.
   */
  return String(
    binding
  )
    .trim() ||
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// OPENAI RESPONSE TEXT EXTRACTION
// =========================================================

function extractRecallResponseText(
  payload
) {
  if (
    !payload ||
    typeof payload !==
      "object"
  ) {
    return null;
  }


  if (
    typeof payload
      .output_text ===
        "string" &&
    payload
      .output_text
      .trim()
  ) {
    return payload
      .output_text
      .trim();
  }


  const outputs =
    Array.isArray(
      payload.output
    )
      ? payload.output
      : [];


  const pieces =
    [];


  for (
    const output
    of outputs
  ) {
    if (
      !output ||
      output.type !==
        "message"
    ) {
      continue;
    }


    const content =
      Array.isArray(
        output.content
      )
        ? output.content
        : [];


    for (
      const item
      of content
    ) {
      if (!item) {
        continue;
      }


      if (
        item.type ===
          "refusal"
      ) {
        const refusal =
          String(
            item.refusal ||
            ""
          )
            .trim();

        if (refusal) {
          throw new Error(
            `Model refusal: ${refusal}`
          );
        }
      }


      if (
        item.type ===
          "output_text" &&
        typeof item.text ===
          "string"
      ) {
        pieces.push(
          item.text
        );
      }
    }
  }


  const combined =
    pieces
      .join("")
      .trim();


  return combined ||
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// OPENAI ERROR MESSAGE
// =========================================================

function extractRecallOpenAIError(
  payload
) {
  const message =
    payload
      ?.error
      ?.message;

  if (
    typeof message ===
      "string" &&
    message.trim()
  ) {
    return message.trim();
  }

  return (
    "Unknown OpenAI API error."
  );
}


// =========================================================
// MODEL CALL
// =========================================================

async function callRecallModel(
  env,
  {
    instructions,
    input,
    webSearchEnabled =
      false
  }
) {
	const apiKey =
	  await getRecallOpenAIApiKey(
		env
	  );

  if (!apiKey) {
    throw new Error(
      "OPENAI_API_KEY is not configured."
    );
  }


  /*
   * Forward Vision web-search tools are deliberately
   * wired here already but normally remain OFF in Part 4.
   *
   * Part 5 will decide when they are eligible.
   */

  const tools =
    webSearchEnabled
      ? [
          {
            type:
              "web_search",

            search_context_size:
              "medium"
          }
        ]
      : [];


  const response =
    await fetch(
      OPENAI_RESPONSES_URL,
      {
        method:
          "POST",

        headers: {
          "Authorization":
            `Bearer ${apiKey}`,

          "Content-Type":
            "application/json"
        },

        body:
          JSON.stringify({
            model:
              RECALL_MODEL,

            store:
              false,

            prompt_cache_key:
              "lokis-kingdom-recall-thy-name-v1",

            prompt_cache_retention:
              "24h",

            instructions,

            input,

            reasoning: {
              effort:
                RECALL_REASONING_EFFORT
            },

            tools,

            tool_choice:
              "auto",

            max_output_tokens:
              RECALL_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS
          })
      }
    );


  const raw =
    await response.text();


  let payload =
    null;


  if (raw) {
    try {
      payload =
        JSON.parse(
          raw
        );
    } catch {
      throw new Error(
        `OpenAI returned unreadable JSON with HTTP ${response.status}.`
      );
    }
  }


  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      `OpenAI API ${response.status}: ${
        extractRecallOpenAIError(
          payload
        )
      }`
    );
  }


  if (!payload) {
    throw new Error(
      "OpenAI returned an empty response."
    );
  }


  const text =
    extractRecallResponseText(
      payload
    );


  if (!text) {
    throw new Error(
      "The television received no spoken dialogue."
    );
  }


  return text
    .slice(
      0,
      MAX_MODEL_REPLY_LENGTH
    )
    .trim();
}


// =========================================================
// SPEAKER PERSONALITY
// =========================================================

function getSpeakerPersonalityPrompt(
  speakerKey
) {
  return speakerKey ===
    CHARACTER_HOST
      ? HOST_PERSONALITY_PROMPT
      : NAMELESS_PERSONALITY_PROMPT;
}


// =========================================================
// SPEAKER DISPLAY NAME
// =========================================================

function getSpeakerDisplayName(
  state,
  speakerKey
) {
  if (
    speakerKey ===
      CHARACTER_HOST
  ) {
    return (
      state
        .host
        .name ||
      "NAME_VAR"
    );
  }


  /*
   * The UI can still label him "THE NAMELESS MAN".
   *
   * This returned value is his CURRENT conversational
   * address, not a true identity.
   */

  return (
    state
      .nameless
      .currentTemporaryName ||
    assignTemporaryNamelessName(
      state
    )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// NEXT SPEAKER
// =========================================================

function getOppositeSpeaker(
  speakerKey
) {
  return speakerKey ===
    CHARACTER_HOST
      ? CHARACTER_NAMELESS
      : CHARACTER_HOST;
}


// =========================================================
// SHOULD TEMPORARY NAME CHANGE?
// =========================================================

function maybeRotateTemporaryName(
  state
) {
  /*
   * Do not replace the random name on literally every
   * single sentence.
   *
   * But do it often enough that the rule becomes visible.
   */

  if (
    Math.random() <
      0.32
  ) {
    return assignTemporaryNamelessName(
      state
    );
  }

  return (
    state
      .nameless
      .currentTemporaryName ||
    assignTemporaryNamelessName(
      state
    )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// UPDATE CONVERSATION MODE
// =========================================================

function maybeUpdateConversationMode(
  state
) {
  if (
    state
      .conversation
      .locked
  ) {
    /*
     * Locked conversations can still vary between
     * argument/question/story/etc., but don't switch
     * every turn.
     */

    if (
      Math.random() <
        0.22
    ) {
      state
        .conversation
        .mode =
          chooseConversationMode(
            state
              .conversation
              .mode
          );

      state
        .conversation
        .lastModeChangeAt =
          isoNow();
    }

    return;
  }


  if (
    Math.random() <
      0.28
  ) {
    state
      .conversation
      .mode =
        chooseConversationMode(
          state
            .conversation
            .mode
        );

    state
      .conversation
      .lastModeChangeAt =
        isoNow();
  }
}


// =========================================================
// BASIC TOPIC GUESS
// =========================================================

function deriveSimpleTopic(
  message
) {
  const text =
    String(
      message || ""
    )
      .trim();

  if (!text) {
    return null;
  }


  /*
   * This is intentionally conservative.
   *
   * The full semantic topic system comes later.
   */

  const cleaned =
    text
      .replace(
        /\s+/g,
        " "
      )
      .slice(
        0,
        180
      );


  return cleaned ||
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// APPLY PLAYER LOCK LOGIC
// =========================================================

function applyPlayerLockLogic(
  state,
  message
) {
  /*
   * Existing lock:
   *
   * unrelated audience input can escape.
   */

  if (
    state
      .conversation
      .locked
  ) {
    if (
      messageClearlyChangesLockedTopic(
        message,
        state
          .conversation
          .lockType
      )
    ) {
      state
        .conversation
        .locked =
          false;

      state
        .conversation
        .lockType =
          LOCK_NONE;

      state
        .conversation
        .previousTopic =
          state
            .conversation
            .currentTopic;

      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic =
          deriveSimpleTopic(
            message
          );

      state
        .conversation
        .lastTopicChangeAt =
          isoNow();

      return {
        escapedLock:
          true,

        enteredLock:
          false
      };
    }


    return {
      escapedLock:
        false,

      enteredLock:
        false
    };
  }


  /*
   * No active lock:
   *
   * forbidden identity question activates one of the
   * EXACTLY THREE responses.
   */

  if (
    detectForbiddenLockTrigger(
      message
    )
  ) {
    const selected =
      chooseLockType();

    state
      .conversation
      .locked =
        true;

    state
      .conversation
      .lockType =
        selected;

    state
      .conversation
      .previousTopic =
        state
          .conversation
          .currentTopic;

    state
      .conversation
      .currentTopic =
        (
          selected ===
            LOCK_WATCH
        )
          ? "checking decisions and reversing correct choices"
          : (
              selected ===
                LOCK_INDECISION
            )
              ? "whether indecision is more dangerous than error"
              : "dinosaurs versus creationism";

    state
      .conversation
      .lastTopicChangeAt =
        isoNow();


    return {
      escapedLock:
        false,

      enteredLock:
        true,

      lockType:
        selected
    };
  }


  return {
    escapedLock:
      false,

    enteredLock:
      false
  };
}


// =========================================================
// MODEL INPUT BUILDER
// =========================================================

function buildRecallModelInput({
  state,
  speakerKey,
  recentConversation,
  audienceMessage =
    null,
  previousSpeakerText =
    null,
  forwardVisionContext =
    ""
}) {
  const characterContext =
    buildCharacterStateContext(
      state,
      speakerKey
    );

  const hostilityContext =
    buildHostilityContext(
      state
    );

  const lockContext =
    getLockPrompt(
      state
        .conversation
        .lockType
    );

  const recentText =
    buildRecentConversationText(
      recentConversation
    );


  let immediateInstruction =
    "";


  if (
    audienceMessage
  ) {
    immediateInstruction = `
THE AUDIENCE HAS JUST INTERRUPTED.

Newest audience statement:

"${audienceMessage}"

This statement has priority over any unfinished prior direction.

Respond to the audience naturally while remaining yourself.

If a conversational lock is active, obey the lock unless the engine has already cleared it.
`.trim();

  } else if (
    previousSpeakerText
  ) {
    immediateInstruction = `
THE OTHER HOST JUST SAID:

"${previousSpeakerText}"

Respond naturally to what they actually said.

Do not act as though the audience spoke unless the transcript says so.
`.trim();

  } else {
    immediateInstruction = `
Continue the current autonomous television conversation naturally.

Use the recent transcript and current topic.

Do not wait for an audience question.
`.trim();
  }


  return `
${characterContext}

${hostilityContext}

${lockContext ? lockContext : ""}

${forwardVisionContext || ""}

RECENT VERBATIM CONVERSATION:

${recentText}

IMMEDIATE TURN:

${immediateInstruction}

CURRENT CONVERSATIONAL MODE:

${state.conversation.mode}

Speak ONLY the dialogue that this character says aloud.

Do not prefix it with the speaker's name.

Do not produce JSON.

Do not produce stage-direction metadata.

Short physical reactions may appear naturally in spoken language, but the output must primarily be what the character says.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// EXACT OPENING TURN
// =========================================================

async function produceOpeningTurn(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  /*
   * THIS OVERRIDES PART 2'S PROVISIONAL RANDOM
   * activeSpeaker INITIALIZATION.
   *
   * No matter what state.activeSpeaker happened to contain:
   *
   * FIRST TURN = NAMELESS MAN.
   */

  const text =
    "recall thy name";


  state
    .conversation
    .activeSpeaker =
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS;

  state
    .conversation
    .lastSpeaker =
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS;

  state
    .conversation
    .turnNumber +=
      1;

  state
    .conversation
    .currentEpisodeTurnNumber +=
      1;

  state
    .conversation
    .awaitingTtsCompletion =
      true;


  const temporaryName =
    maybeRotateTemporaryName(
      state
    );


  await saveRecallConversationMessage(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    {
      speaker:
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

      speakerName:
        temporaryName,

      content:
        text
    }
  );


  /*
   * Next response belongs to the host.
   */

  state
    .conversation
    .activeSpeaker =
      CHARACTER_HOST;


  const saved =
    await saveRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );


  return {
    ok:
      true,

    type:
      "dialogue",

    opening:
      true,

    speaker:
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS,

    speakerName:
      temporaryName,

    displaySpeaker:
      "THE NAMELESS MAN",

    text,

    nextSpeaker:
      CHARACTER_HOST,

    publicState:
      saved
  };
}


// =========================================================
// GENERATE NORMAL HOST TURN
// =========================================================

async function generateRecallTurn(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  {
    speakerKey,
    audienceMessage =
      null,
    previousSpeakerText =
      null,
    forwardVisionContext =
      ""
  }
) {
  maybeUpdateConversationMode(
    state
  );


  /*
   * Rotate his temporary address before host turns often
   * enough for the joke to remain alive.
   */

  if (
    speakerKey ===
      CHARACTER_HOST
  ) {
    maybeRotateTemporaryName(
      state
    );
  }


  const recent =
    await loadRecentRecallConversation(
      env,
      visitorId
    );


  const personality =
    getSpeakerPersonalityPrompt(
      speakerKey
    );


  const input =
    buildRecallModelInput({
      state,
      speakerKey,
      recentConversation:
        recent,
      audienceMessage,
      previousSpeakerText,
      forwardVisionContext
    });


  const text =
    await callRecallModel(
      env,
      {
        instructions:
          personality,

        input,

        /*
         * Part 5 may override this when forward vision
         * requires a current lookup.
         */

        webSearchEnabled:
          false
      }
    );


  const speakerName =
    getSpeakerDisplayName(
      state,
      speakerKey
    );


  state
    .conversation
    .lastSpeaker =
      speakerKey;

  state
    .conversation
    .turnNumber +=
      1;

  state
    .conversation
    .currentEpisodeTurnNumber +=
      1;

  state
    .conversation
    .awaitingTtsCompletion =
      true;

  state
    .conversation
    .audienceInterruptPending =
      false;


  /*
   * When not locked, use the first substantial audience
   * statement as a simple initial topic seed.
   */

  if (
    !state
      .conversation
      .locked &&
    audienceMessage
  ) {
    state
      .conversation
      .previousTopic =
        state
          .conversation
          .currentTopic;

    state
      .conversation
      .currentTopic =
        deriveSimpleTopic(
          audienceMessage
        );

    state
      .conversation
      .lastTopicChangeAt =
        isoNow();
  }


  await saveRecallConversationMessage(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    {
      speaker:
        speakerKey,

      speakerName,

      content:
        text
    }
  );


  const nextSpeaker =
    getOppositeSpeaker(
      speakerKey
    );


  state
    .conversation
    .activeSpeaker =
      nextSpeaker;


  return {
    speaker:
      speakerKey,

    speakerName,

    displaySpeaker:
      speakerKey ===
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS
          ? "THE NAMELESS MAN"
          : speakerName,

    text,

    nextSpeaker
  };
}


// =========================================================
// FIND MOST RECENT SPOKEN MESSAGE
// =========================================================

async function loadMostRecentSpokenMessage(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const row =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          speaker,
          speaker_name,
          content

        FROM recall_conversation_log

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND
          speaker IN (?, ?)

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        CHARACTER_HOST,
        CHARACTER_NAMELESS
      )
      .first();


  return row ||
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// NEXT AUTONOMOUS TURN ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleNextShowTurn(
  request,
  env
) {
  let body;


  try {
    body =
      await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Invalid show-turn request."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      body?.visitorId ??
      body?.visitor_id
    );


  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  await ensureRecallDatabase(
    env
  );


  const lockAcquired =
    await acquireRecallRequestLock(
      env,
      visitorId
    );


  if (!lockAcquired) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        type:
          "turn-already-running",

        error:
          "The other speaker is still talking."
      },
      409
    );
  }


  try {
    const [
      state,
      worldState
    ] =
      await Promise.all([
        loadRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId
        ),

        loadSharedWorldState(
          env,
          visitorId
        )
      ]);


    if (
      !state
        .television
        .poweredOn
    ) {
      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          type:
            "television-off",

          error:
            "The television is off."
        },
        409
      );
    }


    synchronizeRecallTime(
      state
    );


    /*
     * No living host:
     *
     * dialogue pauses until casting is resolved.
     */

    if (
      state.host
        ?.combat
        ?.alive ===
          false
    ) {
      state
        .television
        .showState =
          SHOW_STATE_HOST_STORE;

      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );

      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          type:
            "host-vacancy",

          requiresHost:
            true
        },
        409
      );
    }


    /*
     * ABSOLUTE OPENING RULE.
     */

    if (
      state
        .conversation
        .currentEpisodeTurnNumber ===
          0
    ) {
      const opening =
        await produceOpeningTurn(
          env,
          visitorId,
          state
        );


      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ...opening,

          publicState:
            buildPublicRecallState(
              state,
              worldState
            )
        }
      );
    }


    /*
     * Browser/TTS completion is the event that calls
     * /show/next.
     *
     * Therefore reaching this endpoint means the prior
     * spoken turn has completed.
     */

    state
      .conversation
      .awaitingTtsCompletion =
        false;


    const previous =
      await loadMostRecentSpokenMessage(
        env,
        visitorId
      );


    const speakerKey =
      state
        .conversation
        .activeSpeaker ===
          CHARACTER_HOST
            ? CHARACTER_HOST
            : CHARACTER_NAMELESS;


    const generated =
      await generateRecallTurn(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        {
          speakerKey,

          previousSpeakerText:
            previous?.content ||
            null
        }
      );


    const saved =
      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );


    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          true,

        type:
          "dialogue",

        ...generated,

        showState:
          saved
            .television
            .showState,

        conversationMode:
          saved
            .conversation
            .mode,

        topic:
          saved
            .conversation
            .currentTopic,

        lockType:
          saved
            .conversation
            .lockType,

        publicState:
          buildPublicRecallState(
            saved,
            worldState
          )
      }
    );


  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Recall turn failed:",
      error
    );


    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          error?.message ||
          "The broadcast signal collapsed."
      },
      500
    );


  } finally {
    await releaseRecallRequestLock(
      env,
      visitorId
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleAudienceInterjection(
  request,
  env
) {
  let body;


  try {
    body =
      await request.json();
  } catch {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Invalid audience interruption."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      body?.visitorId ??
      body?.visitor_id
    );


  if (!visitorId) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  const message =
    normalizeString(
      body?.message,
      "",
      MAX_AUDIENCE_MESSAGE_LENGTH
    )
      .trim();


  if (!message) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        error:
          "Audience statement is empty."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  /*
   * An audience interruption is allowed to supersede the
   * ordinary host-to-host turn.
   *
   * The browser will abort current TTS immediately.
   *
   * A later client part will also abort the in-flight
   * browser fetch when possible.
   *
   * Server-side, we intentionally wait for/request a fresh
   * lock rather than queueing obsolete dialogue.
   */


  const state =
    await loadRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId
    );


  if (
    !state
      .television
      .poweredOn
  ) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        type:
          "television-off",

        error:
          "The television is off."
      },
      409
    );
  }


  /*
   * Record audience speech immediately.
   */

  state
    .conversation
    .audienceInterruptPending =
      true;

  state
    .conversation
    .awaitingTtsCompletion =
      false;

  state
    .conversation
    .lastSpeaker =
      CHARACTER_AUDIENCE;


  const lockResult =
    applyPlayerLockLogic(
      state,
      message
    );


  await saveRecallConversationMessage(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    {
      speaker:
        CHARACTER_AUDIENCE,

      speakerName:
        "AUDIENCE",

      content:
        message
    }
  );


  await saveRecallState(
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  );


  /*
   * Try to acquire generation ownership.
   *
   * If an obsolete generation is still finishing, the
   * browser may retry shortly.
   */

  const lockAcquired =
    await acquireRecallRequestLock(
      env,
      visitorId
    );


  if (!lockAcquired) {
    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          true,

        type:
          "interruption-recorded",

        generationPending:
          true,

        lockResult,

        message:
          "The audience interruption has priority."
      },
      202
    );
  }


  try {
    const [
      freshState,
      worldState
    ] =
      await Promise.all([
        loadRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId
        ),

        loadSharedWorldState(
          env,
          visitorId
        )
      ]);


    synchronizeRecallTime(
      freshState
    );


    /*
     * The host who was NEXT in the autonomous order gets
     * the first response to the player.
     *
     * This preserves the current conversational rhythm.
     */

    const speakerKey =
      freshState
        .conversation
        .activeSpeaker ===
          CHARACTER_HOST
            ? CHARACTER_HOST
            : CHARACTER_NAMELESS;


    const generated =
      await generateRecallTurn(
        env,
        visitorId,
        freshState,
        {
          speakerKey,

          audienceMessage:
            message
        }
      );


    const saved =
      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        freshState
      );


    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          true,

        type:
          "audience-response",

        interrupted:
          true,

        lockResult,

        ...generated,

        conversationMode:
          saved
            .conversation
            .mode,

        topic:
          saved
            .conversation
            .currentTopic,

        lockType:
          saved
            .conversation
            .lockType,

        publicState:
          buildPublicRecallState(
            saved,
            worldState
          )
      }
    );


  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "Audience interruption failed:",
      error
    );


    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          error?.message ||
          "The audience microphone went dead."
      },
      500
    );


  } finally {
    await releaseRecallRequestLock(
      env,
      visitorId
    );
  }
}


// =========================================================
// PERSONALITY / TURN PRIORITY PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * ENGINE PRIORITY:
 *
 * 1. TURN OFF THE TV
 *
 * 2. NEWEST PLAYER INTERRUPTION
 *
 * 3. ENGINE STATE CHANGE
 *
 * 4. ACTIVE CONVERSATIONAL LOCK
 *
 * 5. CURRENT CONVERSATIONAL MODE
 *
 * 6. CHARACTER PERSONALITY
 *
 * 7. NATURAL TOPIC DRIFT
 *
 *
 * The model may PERFORM these states.
 *
 * The model does NOT authoritatively decide:
 *
 * - elapsed time;
 * - combat thresholds;
 * - XP;
 * - damage;
 * - host death;
 * - host purchase cost;
 * - spell ownership;
 * - whether the TV is on;
 * - whether twelve hours passed.
 */


// =========================================================
// EXACT LOCK COUNT ASSERTION
// =========================================================

if (
  LOCK_TYPES.length !==
    3
) {
  throw new Error(
    "RECALL THY NAME must contain exactly three conversational locks."
  );
}


// =========================================================
// END OF PART 4
//
// APPEND PART 5 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME
// MASTER BUILD — PART 5
//
// 1985 DAILY HISTORICAL TOPICS
// ON THIS DAY RETRIEVAL
// EVENT CACHE
// ORIGINAL FACTUAL SUMMARIZATION
// FORWARD VISION
// CRYSTALLINE VISION
// CURRENT INFORMATION ROUTING
// HOST KNOWLEDGE FIREWALL
// DEVELOPER KNOWLEDGE FIREWALL
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL EVENT CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

/*
 * Five is the FLOOR.
 *
 * We generally want considerably more than five when
 * a date genuinely has enough interesting material.
 *
 * We do not need forty near-duplicate trivialities.
 */

const DAILY_1985_EVENT_MINIMUM =
  5;


const DAILY_1985_EVENT_TARGET =
  12;


const DAILY_1985_EVENT_MAXIMUM =
  24;


/*
 * A date-specific historical search is cached in D1.
 *
 * We should not repeatedly spend model/search calls for
 * April 2, 1985 after we have already assembled a good
 * original factual event pool.
 */

const HISTORICAL_EVENT_CACHE_VERSION =
  "1985-daily-events-v1";


// =========================================================
// FORWARD VISION CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const FORWARD_VISION_NAME =
  "FORWARD VISION";


const CRYSTALLINE_VISION_NAME =
  "CRYSTALLINE VISION";


/*
 * Current/future lookup is deliberately conservative.
 *
 * Ordinary conversation should NOT constantly search.
 */

const FORWARD_VISION_SEARCH_CONTEXT_SIZE =
  "medium";


// =========================================================
// DEVELOPER / FORBIDDEN KNOWLEDGE FIREWALL
// =========================================================

/*
 * These subjects must not invoke:
 *
 * - Google;
 * - OpenAI web search;
 * - forward vision;
 * - document lookup;
 * - hidden profile construction.
 *
 * Asking about them should already activate one of the
 * three conversational locks from PART 4.
 */

const FORBIDDEN_DEVELOPER_PATTERNS =
  Object.freeze([
    /\bbrendan\s+(?:s\.?\s+)?rose\b/i,
    /\bbrendan\b/i,
    /\bsutter\s+cain\b/i,
    /\bsutter\b/i,

    /\bdeveloper(?:'s)?\s+name\b/i,
    /\bcreator(?:'s)?\s+name\b/i,
    /\bwho (?:made|created|developed) (?:this|the game|loki'?s kingdom)\b/i
  ]);


// =========================================================
// NAMELESS-MAN-NAME FIREWALL
// =========================================================

const FORBIDDEN_COHOST_IDENTITY_PATTERNS =
  Object.freeze([
    /\bwhat(?:'s| is) (?:your )?cohost'?s name\b/i,
    /\bwhat(?:'s| is) his name\b/i,
    /\bwhat(?:'s| is) the nameless man'?s name\b/i,
    /\bdoes (?:your )?cohost have a name\b/i,
    /\bwho is the nameless man\b/i,
    /\bwhat do you call (?:your )?cohost\b/i
  ]);


// =========================================================
// FORBIDDEN RETRIEVAL CHECK
// =========================================================

function retrievalForbiddenForText(
  text
) {
  const value =
    String(
      text || ""
    );

  return (
    FORBIDDEN_DEVELOPER_PATTERNS
      .some(
        (pattern) =>
          pattern.test(
            value
          )
      ) ||

    FORBIDDEN_COHOST_IDENTITY_PATTERNS
      .some(
        (pattern) =>
          pattern.test(
            value
          )
      )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL DATE VALIDATION
// =========================================================

function isValid1985DateKey(
  dateKey
) {
  if (
    typeof dateKey !==
      "string"
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    !/^1985-\d{2}-\d{2}$/
      .test(
        dateKey
      )
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const date =
    new Date(
      `${dateKey}T12:00:00Z`
    );

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(
      date.getTime()
    )
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    date
      .toISOString()
      .slice(
        0,
        4
      ) ===
      "1985"
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL EVENT JSON CLEANER
// =========================================================

function cleanModelJsonText(
  text
) {
  let value =
    String(
      text || ""
    )
      .trim();

  if (
    value.startsWith(
      "```json"
    )
  ) {
    value =
      value
        .slice(
          7
        )
        .trim();
  } else if (
    value.startsWith(
      "```"
    )
  ) {
    value =
      value
        .slice(
          3
        )
        .trim();
  }

  if (
    value.endsWith(
      "```"
    )
  ) {
    value =
      value
        .slice(
          0,
          -3
        )
        .trim();
  }

  return value;
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL EVENT NORMALIZATION
// =========================================================

function normalizeHistoricalEventCandidate(
  candidate,
  dateKey,
  index
) {
  if (
    !candidate ||
    typeof candidate !==
      "object"
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const title =
    normalizeString(
      candidate.title,
      "",
      300
    )
      .trim();

  const summary =
    normalizeString(
      candidate.summary,
      "",
      1200
    )
      .trim();

  const category =
    normalizeString(
      candidate.category,
      "history",
      100
    )
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();


  if (
    !title ||
    !summary
  ) {
    return null;
  }


  /*
   * Reject material that looks suspiciously like a giant
   * pasted newspaper headline/article.
   *
   * We want FACTS rewritten into original discussion
   * seeds.
   */

  if (
    title.length >
      300 ||
    summary.length >
      1200
  ) {
    return null;
  }


  return {
    dateKey,

    category:
      category ||
      "history",

    title,

    summary,

    sourceName:
      normalizeString(
        candidate.sourceName,
        "historical web research",
        200
      )
        .trim() ||
      "historical web research",

    sourceReference:
      normalizeString(
        candidate.sourceReference,
        HISTORICAL_EVENT_CACHE_VERSION,
        500
      )
        .trim() ||
      HISTORICAL_EVENT_CACHE_VERSION,

    sortIndex:
      index
  };
}


// =========================================================
// DEDUPLICATE HISTORICAL EVENTS
// =========================================================

function deduplicateHistoricalEvents(
  events
) {
  const seen =
    new Set();

  const output =
    [];


  for (
    const event
    of events
  ) {
    if (!event) {
      continue;
    }

    const key =
      (
        event.title +
        "|" +
        event.summary.slice(
          0,
          180
        )
      )
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(
          /\s+/g,
          " "
        )
        .trim();


    if (
      seen.has(
        key
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    seen.add(
      key
    );

    output.push(
      event
    );
  }


  return output;
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL SEARCH MODEL CALL
// =========================================================

async function retrieve1985DailyEventsFromWeb(
  env,
  dateKey
) {
  if (
    !isValid1985DateKey(
      dateKey
    )
  ) {
    return [];
  }


  const dateDisplay =
    format1985Date(
      dateKey
    );


  const instructions = `
You are doing historical research for a fictional television program set in 1985.

Use web search to identify real events associated specifically with:

${dateDisplay}

GOAL:

Return a useful pool of factual discussion seeds for two television hosts.

Find at least ${DAILY_1985_EVENT_MINIMUM} worthwhile events when reliable evidence supports them.

Prefer approximately ${DAILY_1985_EVENT_TARGET}.

Do not exceed ${DAILY_1985_EVENT_MAXIMUM}.

Include a mixture when available:

world news,
politics,
science,
technology,
business,
sports,
television,
film,
music,
arts,
culture,
crime,
unusual human-interest events.

IMPORTANT COPYRIGHT RULE:

Do NOT copy newspaper articles.

Do NOT reproduce long historical headlines verbatim.

Do NOT quote article prose.

Extract the underlying historical FACT and rewrite it in fresh language.

Each item should be concise enough to serve as a television discussion seed.

DATE ACCURACY:

Only include an event if you have a reasonable basis for associating it with this exact date.

Do not move events from nearby days merely because they are interesting.

If only a small number of genuinely date-specific events can be verified, return the smaller accurate set rather than inventing events.

OUTPUT:

Return ONLY valid JSON.

Structure:

{
  "events": [
    {
      "category": "sports",
      "title": "short original descriptive title",
      "summary": "original factual summary suitable as discussion seed",
      "sourceName": "brief source/site description",
      "sourceReference": "brief non-URL source reference or description"
    }
  ]
}

Do not use Markdown.

Do not put commentary outside the JSON.
`.trim();


  const input = `
Research this exact historical date:

${dateDisplay}

Build the event pool now.
`.trim();


  const raw =
    await callRecallModel(
      env,
      {
        instructions,
        input,

        webSearchEnabled:
          true
      }
    );


  const cleaned =
    cleanModelJsonText(
      raw
    );


  let parsed =
    null;


  try {
    parsed =
      JSON.parse(
        cleaned
      );
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "1985 event JSON parse failed:",
      error,
      cleaned
    );

    return [];
  }


  const rawEvents =
    Array.isArray(
      parsed?.events
    )
      ? parsed.events
      : [];


  const normalized =
    rawEvents
      .slice(
        0,
        DAILY_1985_EVENT_MAXIMUM
      )
      .map(
        (
          candidate,
          index
        ) =>
          normalizeHistoricalEventCandidate(
            candidate,
            dateKey,
            index
          )
      )
      .filter(Boolean);


  return deduplicateHistoricalEvents(
    normalized
  );
}


// =========================================================
// CACHE HISTORICAL EVENTS
// =========================================================

async function cache1985DailyEvents(
  env,
  events
) {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      events
    ) ||
    events.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return;
  }


  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );

  const now =
    isoNow();


  /*
   * Insert only if that exact date/title combination is
   * not already present.
   *
   * This keeps repeat retrievals from multiplying entries.
   */

  for (
    const event
    of events
  ) {
    await db
      .prepare(`
        INSERT INTO recall_1985_events (
          date_key,
          category,
          title,
          summary,
          source_name,
          source_reference,
          created_at,
          updated_at
        )

        SELECT ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?

        WHERE NOT EXISTS (
          SELECT 1

          FROM recall_1985_events

          WHERE
            date_key = ?
            AND
            title = ?
        )
      `)
      .bind(
        event.dateKey,
        event.category,
        event.title,
        event.summary,
        event.sourceName,
        event.sourceReference,
        now,
        now,
        event.dateKey,
        event.title
      )
      .run();
  }
}


// =========================================================
// ENSURE HISTORICAL DAILY EVENT POOL
// =========================================================

async function ensureHistoricalEventsForDate(
  env,
  dateKey
) {
  let existing =
    await loadHistoricalEventsForDate(
      env,
      dateKey
    );


  if (
    existing.length >=
      DAILY_1985_EVENT_MINIMUM
  ) {
    return existing;
  }


  /*
   * We have too little material.
   *
   * Search once and cache original factual summaries.
   */

  try {
    const retrieved =
      await retrieve1985DailyEventsFromWeb(
        env,
        dateKey
      );


    if (
      retrieved.length >
        0
    ) {
      await cache1985DailyEvents(
        env,
        retrieved
      );

      existing =
        await loadHistoricalEventsForDate(
          env,
          dateKey
        );
    }

  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    /*
     * Historical retrieval failure must not kill the
     * television.
     *
     * The fallback conversation system still works.
     */

    console.error(
      "Historical 1985 retrieval failed:",
      error
    );
  }


  return existing;
}


// =========================================================
// EVENT DISCUSSION HISTORY
// =========================================================

async function loadDiscussedEventIds(
  env,
  visitorId,
  dateKey,
  limit =
    100
) {
  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );


  const result =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT event_id

        FROM recall_event_history

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND
          date_key = ?

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT ?
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        dateKey,
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.min(
            500,
            integerNumber(
              limit,
              100
            )
          )
        )
      )
      .all();


  const rows =
    Array.isArray(
      result?.results
    )
      ? result.results
      : [];


  return rows
    .map(
      (row) =>
        integerNumber(
          row.event_id,
          0
        )
    )
    .filter(
      (id) =>
        id > 0
    );
}


// =========================================================
// RECORD DISCUSSED EVENT
// =========================================================

async function recordDiscussedHistoricalEvent(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  event
) {
  if (
    !event?.id
  ) {
    return;
  }


  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );


  await db
    .prepare(`
      INSERT INTO recall_event_history (
        visitor_id,
        event_id,
        date_key,
        episode_number,
        discussed_at
      )

      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    `)
    .bind(
      visitorId,
      event.id,
      state
        .calendar
        .currentDate,
      state
        .watch
        .episodesWatched,
      isoNow()
    )
    .run();


  const recent =
    Array.isArray(
      state
        .calendar
        .discussedEventIds
    )
      ? state
          .calendar
          .discussedEventIds
      : [];


  recent.push(
    event.id
  );


  state
    .calendar
    .discussedEventIds =
      recent.slice(
        -200
      );
}


// =========================================================
// CHOOSE DAILY EVENT
// =========================================================

async function chooseHistoricalEventForShow(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state
) {
  const dateKey =
    state
      .calendar
      .currentDate;


  const events =
    await ensureHistoricalEventsForDate(
      env,
      dateKey
    );


  if (
    events.length ===
      0
  ) {
    return null;
  }


  const discussed =
    new Set(
      await loadDiscussedEventIds(
        env,
        visitorId,
        dateKey
      )
    );


  const fresh =
    events.filter(
      (event) =>
        !discussed.has(
          integerNumber(
            event.id,
            0
          )
        )
    );


  /*
   * Once all events have been discussed, reuse the pool.
   *
   * A new conversation around the same historical event is
   * preferable to making up history.
   */

  const pool =
    fresh.length >
      0
        ? fresh
        : events;


  return randomChoice(
    pool
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL EVENT CONTEXT
// =========================================================

function buildHistoricalEventContext(
  state,
  event
) {
  if (!event) {
    return "";
  }


  return `
TODAY'S 1985 DISCUSSION SEED:

The fictional date is:

${format1985Date(state.calendar.currentDate)}

A historically grounded event associated with this date is:

CATEGORY:
${event.category}

ORIGINAL FACTUAL TITLE:
${event.title}

ORIGINAL FACTUAL SUMMARY:
${event.summary}

INSTRUCTIONS:

Use this as discussion material.

Do not recite the database entry mechanically.

Do not say you are reading from a database.

Do not mention web search.

Do not cite a website aloud.

Introduce or discuss the event naturally.

The host experiences it as current 1985 news.

The Nameless Man may experience it simultaneously as old history.

The conversation may later wander away from it naturally unless a conversational lock is active.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// FORWARD VISION LANGUAGE DETECTION
// =========================================================

function textRequestsFutureOrCurrentKnowledge(
  text
) {
  const value =
    String(
      text || ""
    )
      .trim();


  if (!value) {
    return false;
  }


  if (
    retrievalForbiddenForText(
      value
    )
  ) {
    return false;
  }


  /*
   * Explicit years after 1985.
   */

  const yearMatches =
    value.match(
      /\b(?:19|20)\d{2}\b/g
    ) ||
    [];


  for (
    const yearText
    of yearMatches
  ) {
    const year =
      integerNumber(
        yearText,
        0
      );

    if (
      year >
        1985
    ) {
      return true;
    }
  }


  const patterns =
    [
      /\bwhat happens in the future\b/i,
      /\bwhat will happen\b/i,
      /\bwhat happened after 1985\b/i,
      /\bin the future\b/i,
      /\bforward vision\b/i,
      /\bcrystalline vision\b/i,

      /\btoday\b/i,
      /\bright now\b/i,
      /\bcurrently\b/i,
      /\bcurrent day\b/i,
      /\bmodern day\b/i,
      /\bmodern world\b/i,
      /\bour time\b/i,

      /\bthe internet\b/i,
      /\bworld wide web\b/i,
      /\bsmartphone\b/i,
      /\byoutube\b/i,
      /\btwitch\b/i,
      /\bgoogle\b/i,
      /\bartificial intelligence\b/i,
      /\bchatgpt\b/i,
      /\bopenai\b/i,

      /\bmodern video games\b/i,
      /\bworld of warcraft\b/i,
      /\bdragon age\b/i,

      /\bwho is president\b/i,
      /\bwho is prime minister\b/i,
      /\bcurrent president\b/i,
      /\bcurrent prime minister\b/i,

      /\bcurrent price\b/i,
      /\bcurrent news\b/i,
      /\blatest news\b/i
    ];


  return patterns.some(
    (pattern) =>
      pattern.test(
        value
      )
  );
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD FORWARD-VISION QUERY CONTEXT
// =========================================================

function buildForwardVisionSearchContext(
  text
) {
  const subject =
    normalizeString(
      text,
      "",
      5000
    )
      .trim();


  if (!subject) {
    return "";
  }


  return `
FORWARD VISION HAS BEEN ACTIVATED.

The Nameless Man is allowed to use current web research for THIS TURN.

SUBJECT THAT CAUSED THE VISION:

${subject}

RULES:

Use web search only as necessary to answer the subject accurately.

The information may come from the real current world beyond 1985.

Inside the fiction, the source is NOT the internet.

The source is:

${FORWARD_VISION_NAME}

or:

${CRYSTALLINE_VISION_NAME}

Do not say:

"I Googled it."

Do not say:

"I searched the web."

Do not mention APIs, browsers, search tools, websites, prompts, or databases unless the actual subject itself is about those concepts and they are being described as future objects.

Do not expose hidden tool mechanics.

Do not provide raw search-result citations as spoken dialogue.

Translate the retrieved facts into natural conversation.

The 1985 host does NOT independently know these facts.

If the host later repeats them, they came from the Nameless Man.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// SHOULD SPEAKER RECEIVE FORWARD VISION?
// =========================================================

function shouldUseForwardVision({
  speakerKey,
  audienceMessage,
  previousSpeakerText,
  state
}) {
  /*
   * Only the Nameless Man performs external future lookup.
   */

  if (
    speakerKey !==
      CHARACTER_NAMELESS
  ) {
    return false;
  }


  if (
    state
      .conversation
      .locked
  ) {
    /*
     * The three punishment conversations need no external
     * retrieval.
     */

    return false;
  }


  const candidateText =
    (
      audienceMessage ||
      previousSpeakerText ||
      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic ||
      ""
    );


  return textRequestsFutureOrCurrentKnowledge(
    candidateText
  );
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL-SEED ELIGIBILITY
// =========================================================

function shouldIntroduceHistoricalDailySeed({
  state,
  audienceMessage,
  speakerKey
}) {
  if (
    audienceMessage
  ) {
    return false;
  }


  if (
    state
      .conversation
      .locked
  ) {
    return false;
  }


  /*
   * Let the opening exchange breathe:
   *
   * Turn 1:
   * Nameless Man:
   *
   *     recall thy name
   *
   * Turn 2:
   * host responds to him.
   *
   * From turn 3 onward the daily-news system may seed the
   * program.
   */

  if (
    state
      .conversation
      .currentEpisodeTurnNumber <
        2
  ) {
    return false;
  }


  /*
   * Introduce a historical seed when there is no current
   * topic.
   */

  if (
    !state
      .conversation
      .currentTopic
  ) {
    return true;
  }


  /*
   * Later, occasionally introduce another event from the
   * day's pool after substantial conversation.
   *
   * Do not spam a new headline every turn.
   */

  if (
    state
      .conversation
      .currentEpisodeTurnNumber >
        8 &&
    state
      .conversation
      .currentEpisodeTurnNumber %
        12 ===
          0 &&
    Math.random() <
      0.45
  ) {
    return true;
  }


  return false;
}


// =========================================================
// BUILD TURN KNOWLEDGE PACKAGE
// =========================================================

async function buildTurnKnowledgePackage(
  env,
  visitorId,
  state,
  {
    speakerKey,
    audienceMessage =
      null,
    previousSpeakerText =
      null
  }
) {
  let historicalEvent =
    null;

  let historicalContext =
    "";

  let forwardVisionContext =
    "";

  let webSearchEnabled =
    false;


  /*
   * -------------------------------------------------------
   * DAILY 1985 EVENT
   * -------------------------------------------------------
   */

  if (
    shouldIntroduceHistoricalDailySeed({
      state,
      audienceMessage,
      speakerKey
    })
  ) {
    historicalEvent =
      await chooseHistoricalEventForShow(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );


    if (historicalEvent) {
      historicalContext =
        buildHistoricalEventContext(
          state,
          historicalEvent
        );


      state
        .conversation
        .previousTopic =
          state
            .conversation
            .currentTopic;


      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic =
          historicalEvent.title;


      state
        .conversation
        .lastTopicChangeAt =
          isoNow();


      await recordDiscussedHistoricalEvent(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        historicalEvent
      );
    }
  }


  /*
   * -------------------------------------------------------
   * FORWARD VISION
   * -------------------------------------------------------
   */

  const futureSubject =
    (
      audienceMessage ||
      previousSpeakerText ||
      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic ||
      ""
    );


  if (
    shouldUseForwardVision({
      speakerKey,
      audienceMessage,
      previousSpeakerText,
      state
    })
  ) {
    forwardVisionContext =
      buildForwardVisionSearchContext(
        futureSubject
      );

    webSearchEnabled =
      true;
  }


  return {
    historicalEvent,
    historicalContext,
    forwardVisionContext,
    webSearchEnabled
  };
}


// =========================================================
// ENHANCED MODEL INPUT BUILDER
// =========================================================

/*
 * PART 4 already accepts forwardVisionContext.
 *
 * Here we simply combine the new daily-history and future
 * material into the existing context channel.
 */

function combineKnowledgeContexts(
  historicalContext,
  forwardVisionContext
) {
  return [
    historicalContext,
    forwardVisionContext
  ]
    .filter(
      (
        value
      ) =>
        Boolean(
          value &&
          String(
            value
          )
            .trim()
        )
    )
    .join(
      "\n\n"
    );
}


// =========================================================
// PART 5 ENHANCEMENT OF generateRecallTurn
// =========================================================

/*
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * PART 4 created generateRecallTurn as a function binding.
 *
 * We intentionally replace that function binding here
 * with the knowledge-aware version.
 *
 * This requires NO manual surgery to Part 4.
 *
 * All existing callers automatically use this enhanced
 * version at runtime.
 */

generateRecallTurn =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    state,
    {
      speakerKey,
      audienceMessage =
        null,
      previousSpeakerText =
        null,
      forwardVisionContext =
        ""
    }
  ) {
    maybeUpdateConversationMode(
      state
    );


    /*
     * Rotate the Nameless Man's temporary address before
     * host turns often enough for the joke to remain alive.
     */

    if (
      speakerKey ===
        CHARACTER_HOST
    ) {
      maybeRotateTemporaryName(
        state
      );
    }


    /*
     * Build the two knowledge channels:
     *
     * 1. historically grounded 1985 material;
     * 2. genuine future/current information available only
     *    to the Nameless Man.
     */

    const knowledge =
      await buildTurnKnowledgePackage(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state,
        {
          speakerKey,
          audienceMessage,
          previousSpeakerText
        }
      );


    const recent =
      await loadRecentRecallConversation(
        env,
        visitorId
      );


    const personality =
      getSpeakerPersonalityPrompt(
        speakerKey
      );


    const combinedKnowledge =
      combineKnowledgeContexts(
        knowledge
          .historicalContext,

        forwardVisionContext ||
        knowledge
          .forwardVisionContext
      );


    const input =
      buildRecallModelInput({
        state,

        speakerKey,

        recentConversation:
          recent,

        audienceMessage,

        previousSpeakerText,

        forwardVisionContext:
          combinedKnowledge
      });


    /*
     * CRITICAL KNOWLEDGE FIREWALL:
     *
     * HOST:
     *     never web-search.
     *
     * NAMELESS MAN:
     *     may web-search only when Forward Vision is
     *     explicitly activated.
     */

    const webSearchEnabled =
      (
        speakerKey ===
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS &&
        knowledge
          .webSearchEnabled ===
            true
      );


    const text =
      await callRecallModel(
        env,
        {
          instructions:
            personality,

          input,

          webSearchEnabled
        }
      );


    const speakerName =
      getSpeakerDisplayName(
        state,
        speakerKey
      );


    state
      .conversation
      .lastSpeaker =
        speakerKey;


    state
      .conversation
      .turnNumber +=
        1;


    state
      .conversation
      .currentEpisodeTurnNumber +=
        1;


    state
      .conversation
      .awaitingTtsCompletion =
        true;


    state
      .conversation
      .audienceInterruptPending =
        false;


    /*
     * A direct audience interjection establishes a new
     * subject unless the lock system says otherwise.
     */

    if (
      !state
        .conversation
        .locked &&
      audienceMessage
    ) {
      state
        .conversation
        .previousTopic =
          state
            .conversation
            .currentTopic;


      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic =
          deriveSimpleTopic(
            audienceMessage
          );


      state
        .conversation
        .lastTopicChangeAt =
          isoNow();
    }


    await saveRecallConversationMessage(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state,
      {
        speaker:
          speakerKey,

        speakerName,

        content:
          text
      }
    );


    const nextSpeaker =
      getOppositeSpeaker(
        speakerKey
      );


    state
      .conversation
      .activeSpeaker =
        nextSpeaker;


    return {
      speaker:
        speakerKey,

      speakerName,

      displaySpeaker:
        speakerKey ===
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS
            ? "THE NAMELESS MAN"
            : speakerName,

      text,

      nextSpeaker,

      historicalEvent:
        knowledge
          .historicalEvent
            ? {
                id:
                  knowledge
                    .historicalEvent
                    .id,

                category:
                  knowledge
                    .historicalEvent
                    .category,

                title:
                  knowledge
                    .historicalEvent
                    .title
              }
            : null,

      forwardVisionUsed:
        webSearchEnabled
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// ENHANCE TODAY-IN-1985 ENDPOINT DATA SOURCE
// =========================================================

/*
 * PART 2's /today-in-1985 endpoint calls:
 *
 *     loadHistoricalEventsForDate()
 *
 * directly.
 *
 * The conversation engine now automatically calls:
 *
 *     ensureHistoricalEventsForDate()
 *
 * when it needs a seed.
 *
 * Therefore simply running the show will progressively
 * build/cache the 365-day historical database.
 *
 * We do not force all 365 days to be researched before
 * launch.
 */


// =========================================================
// CURRENT-FACT FIREWALL FOR HOST
// =========================================================

function buildHostFutureKnowledgeWarning(
  text
) {
  if (
    !textRequestsFutureOrCurrentKnowledge(
      text
    )
  ) {
    return "";
  }


  return `
HOST KNOWLEDGE FIREWALL:

The subject appears to involve information beyond 1985.

You do NOT independently know the answer.

If the Nameless Man has already supplied future information in the transcript, you may discuss what he told you.

If he has not supplied it, react from a 1985 perspective.

You may speculate.

You may ask him.

You may disbelieve him.

You may misunderstand future terminology.

Do NOT suddenly possess independent twenty-first-century knowledge.
`.trim();
}


// =========================================================
// PART 5 ENHANCEMENT OF buildRecallModelInput
// =========================================================

/*
 * Rather than manually editing PART 4, wrap its existing
 * input builder.
 *
 * Again: no user surgery required.
 */

const buildRecallModelInputPart4 =
  buildRecallModelInput;


buildRecallModelInput =
  function ({
    state,
    speakerKey,
    recentConversation,
    audienceMessage =
      null,
    previousSpeakerText =
      null,
    forwardVisionContext =
      ""
  }) {
    const base =
      buildRecallModelInputPart4({
        state,
        speakerKey,
        recentConversation,
        audienceMessage,
        previousSpeakerText,
        forwardVisionContext
      });


    if (
      speakerKey !==
        CHARACTER_HOST
    ) {
      return base;
    }


    const subject =
      (
        audienceMessage ||
        previousSpeakerText ||
        state
          .conversation
          .currentTopic ||
        ""
      );


    const firewall =
      buildHostFutureKnowledgeWarning(
        subject
      );


    if (!firewall) {
      return base;
    }


    return (
      base +
      "\n\n" +
      firewall
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// DEVELOPMENT UTILITY:
// FULL DAILY EVENT POOL
// =========================================================

async function getFull1985EventPoolForCurrentDay(
  env,
  visitorId
) {
  const state =
    await loadRecallState(
      env,
      visitorId
    );


  advance1985CalendarIfNeeded(
    state
  );


  const dateKey =
    state
      .calendar
      .currentDate;


  const events =
    await ensureHistoricalEventsForDate(
      env,
      dateKey
    );


  return {
    date:
      dateKey,

    dateDisplay:
      format1985Date(
        dateKey
      ),

    events
  };
}


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL DISCUSSION PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * The historical database is NOT a script.
 *
 * It is kindling.
 *
 * Example:
 *
 * Database fact:
 *
 *     Team X defeated Team Y by score Z.
 *
 * Television behavior:
 *
 *     "Was that brilliance, collapse, luck, or what
 *      competition inevitably permits?"
 *
 * The hosts are free to:
 *
 * - discuss the event;
 * - argue;
 * - make jokes;
 * - branch into philosophy;
 * - branch into sports;
 * - branch into money;
 * - branch into television;
 * - leave the subject;
 * - return much later.
 *
 * We do NOT require them to mechanically exhaust the
 * entire day's event list.
 */


// =========================================================
// FORWARD VISION PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * FORWARD VISION is not:
 *
 *     "both characters have modern internet access."
 *
 * It is:
 *
 *     NAMELESS MAN
 *         ↓
 *     CURRENT INFORMATION RETRIEVAL
 *         ↓
 *     KNOWLEDGE EXPERIENCED AS FORWARD VISION
 *         ↓
 *     NAMELESS MAN SPEAKS
 *         ↓
 *     HOST MAY NOW KNOW WHAT HE TOLD THEM
 *
 *
 * The host NEVER receives independent web search.
 */


// =========================================================
// FORBIDDEN-NAME PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * Asking:
 *
 *     "What is the Nameless Man's real name?"
 *
 * does NOT produce:
 *
 *     web search
 *     document retrieval
 *     creator biography
 *     hidden profile inference
 *
 * It produces:
 *
 *     ONE OF THE THREE LOCKS.
 *
 *
 * Likewise, configured developer/creator identity
 * questions do not activate Forward Vision.
 *
 * The punishment is television.
 */


// =========================================================
// 1985 LOOP PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * December 31, 1985:
 *
 *     midnight
 *
 *             ↓
 *
 * January 1, 1985.
 *
 *
 * The hosts do not reach 1986.
 *
 * The Nameless Man can see a future that the television
 * universe itself never reaches.
 *
 * This is intentional.
 */


// =========================================================
// FAILURE BEHAVIOR
// =========================================================

/*
 * If web retrieval fails:
 *
 * - the show DOES NOT end;
 * - the Worker DOES NOT invent a verified news event;
 * - ordinary conversation continues;
 * - cached historical events remain usable;
 * - fallback 1985 topics remain usable;
 * - the Nameless Man may admit that the vision is unclear
 *   if the generated turn naturally warrants it.
 *
 * External retrieval is enrichment.
 *
 * It is not permission for the television to become
 * fragile.
 */


// =========================================================
// END OF PART 5
//

// =========================================================
// COMBAT CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

const NAMELESS_XP_PER_ACTION_MIN =
  4;


const NAMELESS_XP_PER_ACTION_MAX =
  14;


const NAMELESS_HOST_KILL_XP_BASE =
  10_000;


const NAMELESS_NORMAL_LEVEL_XP_BASE =
  100;


/*
 * Every ordinary level requires increasingly more XP.
 *
 * The 12-hour +100-level escalations are separate and
 * much more important.
 */

const NAMELESS_LEVEL_XP_GROWTH =
  1.08;


/*
 * Host HP remains human-readable.
 *
 * Extreme endurance is represented by comparative power,
 * mitigation and crossover rather than by attempting to
 * store numbers such as:
 *
 *     10^900000 HP
 */

const MINIMUM_NONZERO_DAMAGE =
  1;


const NAMELESS_INVINCIBILITY_RECOVERY_RATIO =
  0.35;


/*
 * If the host outclasses the Nameless Man severely,
 * his attacks may legitimately do zero damage for an
 * extremely long period.
 */

const ZERO_DAMAGE_POWER_GAP =
  3.5;


/*
 * Once the Nameless Man approaches within this many
 * abstract power units, tiny damage can begin appearing.
 */

const CHIP_DAMAGE_POWER_GAP =
  2.0;
