      buildCombatActionContext(
        state,
        combatEvent
      );


    const memoryContext =
      buildRecallMemoryContext(
        memories
      );


    const repetitionContext =
      buildRepetitionControlContext(
        recent,
        speakerKey,
        state
          .conversation
          .locked
      );


    const input =
      buildRecallModelInput({
        state,

        speakerKey,

        recentConversation:
          recent,

        audienceMessage,

        previousSpeakerText,

        forwardVisionContext:
          combinedKnowledge,

        combatContext,

        memoryContext,

        repetitionContext
      });


    const webSearchEnabled =
      (
        speakerKey ===
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS &&
        knowledge
          .webSearchEnabled ===
            true
      );


    let text =
      await callRecallModel(
        env,
        {
          instructions:
            personality,

          input,

          webSearchEnabled
        }
      );


    /*
     * One anti-loop retry maximum.
     *
     * Locked subjects may repeat semantically.
     *
     * Exact/near-exact wording should not.
     */

    const duplicate =
      (
        isExactDialogueDuplicate(
          text,
          recent,
          speakerKey
        ) ||

        isNearDialogueDuplicate(
          text,
          recent,
          speakerKey,
          state
            .conversation
            .locked
              ? 0.90
              : 0.84
        )
      );


    if (
      duplicate
    ) {
      try {
        text =
          await regenerateForRepetition(
            env,
            {
              personality,

              originalInput:
                input,

              firstText:
                text,

              recentRows:
                recent,

              speakerKey,

              webSearchEnabled
            }
          );

      } catch (
        error
      ) {
        console.error(
          "Anti-repetition retry failed:",
          error
        );
      }
    }


    const speakerName =
      getSpeakerDisplayName(
        state,
        speakerKey
      );


    state
      .conversation
      .lastSpeaker =
        speakerKey;


    state
      .conversation
      .turnNumber +=
        1;


    state
      .conversation
      .currentEpisodeTurnNumber +=
        1;


    state
      .conversation
      .awaitingTtsCompletion =
        true;


    state
      .conversation
      .audienceInterruptPending =
        false;


    /*
     * Direct player interjection wins topic authority.
     */

    if (
      !state
        .conversation
        .locked &&
      audienceMessage
    ) {
      state
        .conversation
        .previousTopic =
          state
            .conversation
            .currentTopic;


      state
        .conversation
        .currentTopic =
          deriveSimpleTopic(
            audienceMessage
          );


      state
        .conversation
        .lastTopicChangeAt =
          isoNow();


      modifyRelationshipValue(
        state,
        "audienceTowardShow",
        "topicChanges",
        1
      );

    } else {
      /*
       * Autonomous dialogue may naturally wander.
       */

      await maybeUpdateTopicFromGeneratedDialogue(
        env,
        state,
        text
      );
    }


    applyDeterministicRelationshipEffects(
      state,
      {
        speakerKey,
        text,
        combatEvent,
        audienceMessage
      }
    );


    await saveRecallConversationMessage(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state,
      {
        speaker:
          speakerKey,

        speakerName,

        content:
          text
      }
    );


    /*
     * Durable memory and old-history compression.
     *
     * These calls happen AFTER the new line is persisted.
     */

    const memoriesCreated =
      await maybeExtractDurableMemories(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );


    const longSummaryUpdated =
      await maybeUpdateLongTermSummary(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );


    const hostDead =
      state.host
        ?.combat
        ?.alive ===
          false;


    const nextSpeaker =
      hostDead
        ? null
        : getOppositeSpeaker(
            speakerKey
          );


    if (
      hostDead
    ) {
      state
        .conversation
        .activeSpeaker =
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS;


      state
        .television
        .showState =
          SHOW_STATE_HOST_DEAD;

    } else {
      state
        .conversation
        .activeSpeaker =
          nextSpeaker;
    }


    return {
      speaker:
        speakerKey,

      speakerName,

      displaySpeaker:
        speakerKey ===
          CHARACTER_NAMELESS
            ? "THE NAMELESS MAN"
            : speakerName,

      text,

      nextSpeaker,

      combatEvent,

      combatElapsedSeconds:
        state
          .combat
          .elapsedCombatSeconds,

      majorEscalationsTriggered:
        combatTime
          .escalations,

      historicalEvent:
        knowledge
          .historicalEvent
            ? {
                id:
                  knowledge
                    .historicalEvent
                    .id,

                category:
                  knowledge
                    .historicalEvent
                    .category,

                title:
                  knowledge
                    .historicalEvent
                    .title
              }
            : null,

      forwardVisionUsed:
        webSearchEnabled,

      memoryMaintenance: {
        durableMemoriesCreated:
          memoriesCreated.length,

        longSummaryUpdated
      }
    };
  };


// =========================================================
// AUDIENCE LOCK-ENTRY RELATIONSHIP TRACKING
// =========================================================

const applyPlayerLockLogicPart4 =
  applyPlayerLockLogic;


applyPlayerLockLogic =
  function (
    state,
    message
  ) {
    const result =
      applyPlayerLockLogicPart4(
        state,
        message
      );


    if (
      result
        ?.enteredLock
    ) {
      modifyRelationshipValue(
        state,
        "audienceTowardShow",
        "forbiddenQuestions",
        1
      );
    }


    if (
      result
        ?.escapedLock
    ) {
      modifyRelationshipValue(
        state,
        "audienceTowardShow",
        "topicChanges",
        1
      );
    }


    return result;
  };


// =========================================================
// HIRING RELATIONSHIP RESET HOOK
// =========================================================

const handleHireHostPart3 =
  handleHireHost;


handleHireHost =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {
    /*
     * We cannot easily intercept state inside PART 3's
     * already-running function after it has saved.
     *
     * Instead, let PART 3 complete first, then normalize the
     * newly active relationship state in a second tiny
     * server-authoritative save.
     */

    const clone =
      request.clone();


    let body =
      null;


    try {
      body =
        await clone.json();
    } catch {
      body =
        null;
    }


    const response =
      await handleHireHostPart3(
        request,
        env
      );


    if (
      !response.ok ||
      !body
    ) {
      return response;
    }


    const visitorId =
      normalizeVisitorId(
        body?.visitorId ??
        body?.visitor_id
      );


    if (!visitorId) {
      return response;
    }


    try {
      const state =
        await loadRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId
        );


      resetRelationshipForNewHost(
        state
      );


      await saveRecallMemory(
        env,
        visitorId,
        {
          characterKey:
            "show",

          summary:
            (
              `A new host named ${state.host.name} was hired for recall thy name. ` +
              `Species: ${state.host.species}. Archetype: ${state.host.archetypeName || state.host.archetype}.`
            ),

          importance:
            8
        }
      );


      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );


    } catch (
      error
    ) {
      /*
       * Hiring itself already succeeded.
       *
       * A memory-maintenance error must not reverse the
       * economic transaction.
       */

      console.error(
        "New-host relationship initialization failed:",
        error
      );
    }


    return response;
  };


// =========================================================
// LONG-RUN MEMORY PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * THE MODEL DOES NOT RECEIVE:
 *
 *     400 HOURS OF RAW TRANSCRIPT
 *
 * on every call.
 *
 *
 * It receives:
 *
 * 1. RECENT VERBATIM CONTEXT
 *
 * 2. LONG-TERM SUMMARIZED HISTORY
 *
 * 3. PERSISTENT IMPORTANT FACTS
 *
 * 4. RELATIONSHIP STATE
 *
 *
 * This mirrors the controller specification.
 */


// =========================================================
// REPETITION PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * ORDINARY CONVERSATION:
 *
 *     semantic continuation = GOOD
 *
 *     exact looping = BAD
 *
 *
 * LOCKED CONVERSATION:
 *
 *     topic repetition = INTENTIONAL
 *
 *     exact wording repetition = STILL BAD
 *
 *
 * Therefore:
 *
 * "indecision is dangerous because refusing to decide
 * becomes a decision"
 *
 * may be revisited.
 *
 * But the exact same 500-word speech should not fire every
 * six minutes for 400 hours.
 */


// =========================================================
// OBSESSION PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * Anti-repetition does NOT mean:
 *
 *     characters can never repeat themselves.
 *
 *
 * Humans repeat.
 *
 * Angry people repeat.
 *
 * Obsessed people repeat.
 *
 * Catchphrases repeat.
 *
 * Running jokes repeat.
 *
 * "recall thy name" repeats across hosts.
 *
 *
 * We are preventing BROKEN LOOPS,
 * not personality.
 */


// =========================================================
// CURRENT HOST VS NAMELESS CONTINUITY
// =========================================================

/*
 * CURRENT HOST:
 *
 *     relationship starts largely fresh.
 *
 *
 * NAMELESS MAN:
 *
 *     remains the same historical individual.
 *
 *
 * Therefore:
 *
 * Host #7 does not inherit Host #6's irritation score.
 *
 * But the Nameless Man may remember that six previous
 * hosts existed and how those tenures ended.
 */


// =========================================================
// HOST DEATH PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * A host may have survived:
 *
 *     2 hours
 *
 * or:
 *
 *     2 years.
 *
 *
 * Their eventual death should inherit whatever emotional
 * weight the actual history produced.
 *
 * The engine does NOT force:
 *
 *     grief
 *
 * or:
 *
 *     celebration.
 *
 *
 * It preserves enough history for the character response
 * to emerge from the relationship.
 */


// =========================================================
// TOPIC-DRIFT PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * NO LOCK:
 *
 * conversation may naturally move:
 *
 *     dinosaurs
 *         ↓
 *     museums
 *         ↓
 *     school field trips
 *         ↓
 *     packed lunches
 *         ↓
 *     sandwiches
 *         ↓
 *     expensive restaurants
 *         ↓
 *     money
 *
 *
 * LOCK:
 *
 * absolutely not.
 *
 *
 * The player can always break the lock by introducing an
 * unrelated subject.
 */


// =========================================================
// PART 7 STATE MIGRATION NOTE
// =========================================================

/*
 * Older PART 1 saves do not yet contain:
 *
 *     state.relationship
 *
 * That is fine.
 *
 * ensureExtendedRelationshipState()
 *
 * lazily creates it without wiping any existing save.
 *
 * This is deliberate because updates should preserve old
 * player state whenever reasonably possible.
 */


// =========================================================
// END OF PART 7
//
// APPEND PART 8 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME
// MASTER BUILD — PART 8
//
// COMPLETE TELEVISION WEB INTERFACE
// AUTONOMOUS TTS TURN CHAIN
// PLAYER INTERRUPTION
// LIVE WATCH CLOCK
// LIVE SHOW CLOCK
// LIVE COMBAT DISPLAY
// HOST STORE INTERFACE
// VOICE CONTROLS
// TURN ON TV
// TURN OFF THE TV
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// MAIN HTML
// =========================================================

  <script>

    (() => {

      "use strict";


      // ===================================================
      // CLIENT CONFIGURATION
      // ===================================================

      const VISITOR_KEY =
        "lokis-kingdom-visitor-id";


      const VOICE_KEY =
        "recall-thy-name-voice-enabled";


      const HEARTBEAT_MS =
        10000;


      const TURN_RETRY_MS =
        1400;


      const INTERRUPTION_RETRY_MS =
        1200;


      const MAX_VISIBLE_MESSAGES =
        300;


      // ===================================================
      // DOM
      // ===================================================

      const offScreen =
        document.getElementById(
          "offScreen"
        );


      const showInterface =
        document.getElementById(
          "showInterface"
        );


      const messages =
        document.getElementById(
          "messages"
        );


      const powerButton =
        document.getElementById(
          "powerButton"
        );


      const voiceButton =
        document.getElementById(
          "voiceButton"
        );


      const storeButton =
        document.getElementById(
          "storeButton"
        );


      const hostStore =
        document.getElementById(
          "hostStore"
        );


      const closeStoreButton =
        document.getElementById(
          "closeStoreButton"
        );


      const candidateGrid =
        document.getElementById(
          "candidateGrid"
        );


      const storeGold =
        document.getElementById(
          "storeGold"
        );


      const audienceForm =
        document.getElementById(
          "audienceForm"
        );


      const audienceInput =
        document.getElementById(
          "audienceInput"
        );


      const speakButton =
        document.getElementById(
          "speakButton"
        );


      const speakingIndicator =
        document.getElementById(
          "speakingIndicator"
        );


      const loadingCover =
        document.getElementById(
          "loadingCover"
        );


      // ===================================================
      // CLIENT STATE
      // ===================================================

      let visitorId =
        getOrCreateVisitorId();


      let publicState =
        null;


      let televisionOn =
        false;


      let voiceEnabled =
        localStorage.getItem(
          VOICE_KEY
        ) !==
        "false";


      let currentVoice =
        null;


      /*
       * Native Android TTS does not currently expose an onend
       * callback to JavaScript, so Recall keeps one completion
       * timer while native speech is active. stopSpeech() always
       * clears it before another line begins.
       */
      let nativeSpeechCompletionTimer =
        null;


      let currentGenerationController =
        null;


      let currentInterruptionController =
        null;


      let autonomousLoopEnabled =
        false;


      let waitingForSpeech =
        false;


      let heartbeatTimer =
        null;


      let liveTimer =
        null;


      let localWatchSeconds =
        0;


      let localShowSeconds =
        0;


      let localCombatSeconds =
        0;


      let lastLocalTick =
        Date.now();


      let turnInProgress =
        false;


      let interruptionInProgress =
        false;


      let hostVacancy =
        false;


      let latestServerEpisode =
        0;


      // ===================================================
      // VISITOR ID
      // ===================================================

      function readSharedVisitorCookie() {

        const prefix =
          encodeURIComponent(
            VISITOR_KEY
          ) +
          "=";

        const parts =
          document.cookie.split(
            ";"
          );

        for (
          const part of parts
        ) {
          const trimmed =
            part.trim();

          if (
            trimmed.startsWith(
              prefix
            )
          ) {
            const value =
              decodeURIComponent(
                trimmed.slice(
                  prefix.length
                )
              );

            if (
              /^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{8,200}$/
                .test(
                  value
                )
            ) {
              return value;
            }
          }
        }

        return null;
      }


      function writeSharedVisitorCookie(
        value
      ) {
        document.cookie =
          encodeURIComponent(
            VISITOR_KEY
          ) +
          "=" +
          encodeURIComponent(
            value
          ) +
          "; Max-Age=315360000" +
          "; Path=/" +
          "; Domain=lokiskingdom.com" +
          "; SameSite=Lax" +
          "; Secure";
      }


      function getOrCreateVisitorId() {

        /*
         * The parent-domain cookie is authoritative.
         *
         * localStorage is origin-scoped, so the same key on
         * recall-thy-name.lokiskingdom.com can otherwise point
         * at a different visitor than lokiskingdom.com.
         */
        const cookieValue =
          readSharedVisitorCookie();

        if (cookieValue) {
          try {
            localStorage.setItem(
              VISITOR_KEY,
              cookieValue
            );
          } catch (error) {
            console.warn(
              "Could not mirror shared visitor ID into localStorage:",
              error
            );
          }

          return cookieValue;
        }


        let value =
          null;

        try {
          value =
            localStorage.getItem(
              VISITOR_KEY
            );
        } catch (error) {
          console.warn(
            "Could not read local visitor ID:",
            error
          );
        }


        if (
          !value ||
          !/^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{8,200}$/
            .test(
              value
            )
        ) {
          value =
            (
              "visitor-" +
              crypto.randomUUID()
            );
        }


        try {
          localStorage.setItem(
            VISITOR_KEY,
            value
          );
        } catch (error) {
          console.warn(
            "Could not store local visitor ID:",
            error
          );
        }

        writeSharedVisitorCookie(
          value
        );

        return value;
      }


      // ===================================================
      // FETCH JSON
      // ===================================================

      async function fetchJson(
        path,
        options
      ) {

        const response =
          await fetch(
            path,
            options
          );


        let data =
          null;


        try {
          data =
            await response.json();

        } catch (
          error
        ) {
          throw new Error(
            "The television returned unreadable data."
          );
        }


        return {
          response,
          data
        };
      }


      // ===================================================
      // HTML ESCAPE VIA DOM
      // ===================================================

      function setText(
        element,
        value
      ) {

        if (!element) {
          return;
        }


        element.textContent =
          (
            value ===
              null ||
            value ===
              undefined
          )
            ? ""
            : String(
                value
              );
      }


      // ===================================================
      // DURATION FORMAT
      // ===================================================

      function formatClock(
        seconds
      ) {

        const total =
          Math.max(
            0,
            Math.floor(
              Number(
                seconds
              ) ||
              0
            )
          );


        const hours =
          Math.floor(
            total /
            3600
          );


        const minutes =
          Math.floor(
            (
              total %
              3600
            ) /
            60
          );


        const secs =
          total %
          60;


        return (
          String(
            hours
          ) +
          ":" +
          String(
            minutes
          )
            .padStart(
              2,
              "0"
            ) +
          ":" +
          String(
            secs
          )
            .padStart(
              2,
              "0"
            )
        );
      }


      function formatLifetimeDuration(
        seconds
      ) {

        const total =
          Math.max(
            0,
            Math.floor(
              Number(
                seconds
              ) ||
              0
            )
          );


        const totalHours =
          Math.floor(
            total /
            3600
          );


        const minutes =
          Math.floor(
            (
              total %
              3600
            ) /
            60
          );


        const secs =
          total %
          60;


        const days =
          Math.floor(
            totalHours /
            24
          );


        const remainderHours =
          totalHours %
          24;


        return {
          hours:
            (
              totalHours +
              " hours, " +
              minutes +
              " minutes, " +
              secs +
              " seconds"
            ),

          days:
            (
              "[" +
              days +
              " days, " +
              remainderHours +
              " hours, " +
              minutes +
              " minutes, " +
              secs +
              " seconds]"
            )
        };
      }


      // ===================================================
      // MESSAGE APPEND
      // ===================================================

      function appendMessage(
        text,
        role,
        speakerName
      ) {

        if (!text) {
          return;
        }


        const element =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        element.className =
          (
            "message " +
            (
              role ||
              "system"
            )
          );


        if (
          speakerName
        ) {

          const name =
            document.createElement(
              "span"
            );


          name.className =
            "message-name";


          name.textContent =
            speakerName;


          element.appendChild(
            name
          );
        }


        const body =
          document.createElement(
            "span"
          );


        body.textContent =
          text;


        element.appendChild(
          body
        );


        messages.appendChild(
          element
        );


        while (
          messages.children.length >
            MAX_VISIBLE_MESSAGES
        ) {
          messages.removeChild(
            messages.firstElementChild
          );
        }


        messages.scrollTop =
          messages.scrollHeight;
      }


      // ===================================================
      // COMBAT EVENT DISPLAY
      // ===================================================

      function appendCombatEvent(
        event
      ) {

        if (!event) {
          return;
        }


        if (
          event.announcement
        ) {
          appendMessage(
            event.announcement,
            "combat-event",
            "CRYPT NETWORK"
          );
        }


        let attacker =
          event.attacker ===
            "host"
              ? (
                  publicState &&
                  publicState.host &&
                  publicState.host.name
                    ? publicState.host.name
                    : "HOST"
                )
              : "THE NAMELESS MAN";


        let line =
          (
            attacker +
            " — " +
            event.attackName +
            " — " +
            event.damage +
            " DAMAGE"
          );


        if (
          event.infiniteDamage
        ) {
          line +=
            " [INFINITE DAMAGE]";
        }


        appendMessage(
          line,
          "combat-event",
          "COMBAT"
        );


        if (
          event.invincibility &&
          event.invincibility.triggered
        ) {
          appendMessage(
            (
              "INVINCIBILITY RECOVERY — " +
              event.invincibility.recoveredHp +
              " HP"
            ),
            "combat-event",
            "ENGINE"
          );
        }


        if (
          event.death &&
          event.death.died
        ) {

          appendMessage(
            (
              event.death.hostName +
              " IS DEAD."
            ),
            "combat-event",
            "CRYPT NETWORK"
          );


          hostVacancy =
            true;
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // PUBLIC STATE RENDERING
      // ===================================================

      function renderState(
        state
      ) {

        if (!state) {
          return;
        }


        publicState =
          state;


        if (
          typeof state.television
            ?.poweredOn ===
              "boolean"
        ) {
          televisionOn =
            state
              .television
              .poweredOn;
        }


        hostVacancy =
          (
            state.host
              ?.combat
              ?.alive ===
                false ||
            state
              .television
              ?.showState ===
                "host-dead" ||
            state
              .television
              ?.showState ===
                "host-store"
          );


        localWatchSeconds =
          Number(
            state.statistics
              ?.totalWatchSeconds
          ) ||
          localWatchSeconds ||
          0;


        localShowSeconds =
          Number(
            state.television
              ?.elapsedShowSeconds
          ) ||
          0;


        localCombatSeconds =
          Number(
            state.combat
              ?.elapsedCombatSeconds
          ) ||
          0;


        latestServerEpisode =
          Number(
            state.statistics
              ?.episodesWatched
          ) ||
          0;


        lastLocalTick =
          Date.now();


        renderPowerState();

        renderStatistics();

        renderCharacterState();

        renderShowState();

        renderCalendar();
      }


      // ===================================================
      // POWER STATE
      // ===================================================

      function renderPowerState() {

        if (
          televisionOn
        ) {
          offScreen.style.display =
            "none";


          showInterface.classList.add(
            "visible"
          );


          powerButton.textContent =
            "TURN OFF THE TV";


          powerButton.classList.add(
            "off"
          );


        } else {

          offScreen.style.display =
            "flex";


          showInterface.classList.remove(
            "visible"
          );


          powerButton.textContent =
            "TURN ON TV";


          powerButton.classList.remove(
            "off"
          );
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // STATISTICS
      // ===================================================

      function renderStatistics() {

        const lifetime =
          formatLifetimeDuration(
            localWatchSeconds
          );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "episodesWatched"
          ),
          latestServerEpisode
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "watchTime"
          ),
          lifetime.hours
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "watchDays"
          ),
          lifetime.days
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "showClock"
          ),
          formatClock(
            localShowSeconds
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "combatClock"
          ),
          (
            "COMBAT: " +
            formatClock(
              localCombatSeconds
            )
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "episodeChip"
          ),
          (
            "EPISODE " +
            latestServerEpisode
          )
        );


        const topic =
          publicState
            ?.conversation
            ?.currentTopic ||
          "—";


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "topicDisplay"
          ),
          topic
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "topicChip"
          ),
          (
            "TOPIC: " +
            topic
          )
        );
      }


      // ===================================================
      // CHARACTER RENDERING
      // ===================================================

      function hpPercent(
        hp,
        maxHp
      ) {

        const current =
          Number(
            hp
          ) ||
          0;


        const maximum =
          Math.max(
            1,
            Number(
              maxHp
            ) ||
            1
          );


        return Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            100,
            (
              current /
              maximum
            ) *
            100
          )
        );
      }


      function renderCharacterState() {

        const host =
          publicState
            ?.host ||
          {};


        const hostCombat =
          host.combat ||
          {};


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "hostName"
          ),
          host.name ||
          "—"
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "hostDescription"
          ),
          (
            (
              host.species ||
              "human"
            ) +
            " — " +
            (
              host.archetypeName ||
              host.archetype ||
              "television host"
            )
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "hostLevel"
          ),
          (
            "LEVEL " +
            (
              hostCombat.level ??
              "—"
            ) +
            " — FIXED"
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "hostHp"
          ),
          (
            "HP " +
            Math.max(
              0,
              Math.floor(
                Number(
                  hostCombat.hp
                ) ||
                0
              )
            ) +
            "/" +
            Math.max(
              0,
              Math.floor(
                Number(
                  hostCombat.maxHp
                ) ||
                0
              )
            )
          )
        );


        document.getElementById(
          "hostHpFill"
        ).style.width =
          (
            hpPercent(
              hostCombat.hp,
              hostCombat.maxHp
            ) +
            "%"
          );


        const nameless =
          publicState
            ?.nameless ||
          {};


        const namelessCombat =
          nameless.combat ||
          {};


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "namelessAlias"
          ),
          (
            "CALLED: " +
            (
              nameless.temporaryName ||
              "—"
            )
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "namelessLevel"
          ),
          (
            "LEVEL " +
            (
              namelessCombat.level ??
              1
            )
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "namelessXp"
          ),
          (
            "XP " +
            (
              namelessCombat.xp ??
              0
            )
          )
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "namelessHp"
          ),
          (
            "HP " +
            Math.floor(
              Number(
                namelessCombat.hp
              ) ||
              0
            ) +
            "/" +
            Math.floor(
              Number(
                namelessCombat.maxHp
              ) ||
              0
            )
          )
        );


        document.getElementById(
          "namelessHpFill"
        ).style.width =
          (
            hpPercent(
              namelessCombat.hp,
              namelessCombat.maxHp
            ) +
            "%"
          );


        const powerDifference =
          publicState
            ?.combat
            ?.powerDifference;


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "combatPower"
          ),
          (
            "POWER GAP: " +
            (
              Number.isFinite(
                Number(
                  powerDifference
                )
              )
                ? Number(
                    powerDifference
                  ).toFixed(
                    2
                  )
                : "—"
            )
          )
        );


        const lastAttack =
          publicState
            ?.combat
            ?.lastAttack;


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "combatAction"
          ),
          lastAttack
            ? (
                (
                  lastAttack.attacker ===
                    "host"
                      ? (
                          host.name ||
                          "HOST"
                        )
                      : "NAMELESS MAN"
                ) +
                ": " +
                lastAttack.name +
                " / " +
                lastAttack.damage +
                " DMG"
              )
            : "—"
        );


        setText(
          document.getElementById(
            "playerGold"
          ),
          (
            "GOLD: " +
            (
              publicState
                ?.player
                ?.money ??
              0
            )
          )
        );
      }


      // ===================================================
      // SHOW STATE RENDERING
      // ===================================================

      function renderShowState() {

        const showState =
          publicState
            ?.television
            ?.showState ||
          (
            televisionOn
              ? "conversation"
              : "off"
          );


        const chip =
          document.getElementById(
            "showStateChip"
          );


        setText(
          chip,
          String(
            showState
          )
            .toUpperCase()
        );


        chip.classList.remove(
          "alert"
        );


        chip.classList.remove(
          "combat"
        );


        if (
          showState ===
            "hostility"
        ) {
          chip.classList.add(
            "alert"
          );
        }


        if (
          showState ===
            "combat"
        ) {
          chip.classList.add(
            "combat"
          );
        }


        const lockType =
          publicState
            ?.conversation
            ?.lockType;


        const lockChip =
          document.getElementById(
            "lockChip"
          );


        if (
          lockType &&
          lockType !==
            "none"
        ) {
          lockChip.style.display =
            "block";


          setText(
            lockChip,
            (
              "LOCK: " +
              String(
                lockType
              )
                .toUpperCase()
            )
          );


        } else {
          lockChip.style.display =
            "none";
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // CALENDAR RENDERING
      // ===================================================

      function renderCalendar() {

        if (
          publicState
            ?.dateDisplay
        ) {
          setText(
            document.getElementById(
              "dateDisplay"
            ),
            publicState
              .dateDisplay
          );


          return;
        }


        const key =
          publicState
            ?.calendar
            ?.currentDate;


        if (!key) {
          return;
        }


        try {

          const date =
            new Date(
              key +
              "T12:00:00Z"
            );


          const value =
            new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
              "en-US",
              {
                weekday:
                  "long",

                month:
                  "long",

                day:
                  "numeric",

                year:
                  "numeric",

                timeZone:
                  "UTC"
              }
            )
              .format(
                date
              );


          setText(
            document.getElementById(
              "dateDisplay"
            ),
            value
          );


        } catch (
          error
        ) {
          setText(
            document.getElementById(
              "dateDisplay"
            ),
            key
          );
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // LOAD STATE
      // ===================================================

      async function loadState() {

        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              (
                "/state?visitorId=" +
                encodeURIComponent(
                  visitorId
                )
              )
            );


          if (
            !result.response.ok
          ) {
            throw new Error(
              result.data.error ||
              "State could not be loaded."
            );
          }


          renderState(
            result.data
          );


        } catch (
          error
        ) {

          appendMessage(
            (
              error.message ||
              "The television has no signal."
            ),
            "system",
            "SYSTEM"
          );
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // VOICE SELECTION
      // ===================================================

      function selectVoice(
        speaker
      ) {

        if (
          !(
            "speechSynthesis"
            in window
          )
        ) {
          return null;
        }


        const voices =
          window
            .speechSynthesis
            .getVoices();


        if (
          !voices ||
          voices.length ===
            0
        ) {
          return null;
        }


        const hostSex =
          publicState
            ?.host
            ?.sex ||
          "female";


        /*
         * The Nameless Man prefers a lower-pitched English
         * voice when one exists.
         *
         * Host selection is intentionally broad because
         * browsers expose wildly different voice lists.
         */

        if (
          speaker ===
            "nameless-man"
        ) {

          const preferred =
            voices.find(
              (voice) =>
                /david|daniel|mark|george|james|guy|male/i
                  .test(
                    voice.name
                  ) &&
                /^en/i.test(
                  voice.lang
                )
            );


          return preferred ||
            voices.find(
              (voice) =>
                /^en/i.test(
                  voice.lang
                )
            ) ||
            voices[0];
        }


        if (
          hostSex ===
            "female"
        ) {

          const preferred =
            voices.find(
              (voice) =>
                /samantha|victoria|zira|susan|karen|female|aria|jenny/i
                  .test(
                    voice.name
                  ) &&
                /^en/i.test(
                  voice.lang
                )
            );


          return preferred ||
            voices.find(
              (voice) =>
                /^en/i.test(
                  voice.lang
                )
            ) ||
            voices[0];
        }


        const preferred =
          voices.find(
            (voice) =>
              /david|daniel|mark|george|james|guy|male/i
                .test(
                  voice.name
                ) &&
              /^en/i.test(
                voice.lang
              )
          );


        return preferred ||
          voices.find(
            (voice) =>
              /^en/i.test(
                voice.lang
              )
          ) ||
          voices[0];
      }


      // ===================================================
      // STOP SPEECH
      // ===================================================

      function stopSpeech() {

        waitingForSpeech =
          false;


        speakingIndicator.textContent =
          "";


        if (
          nativeSpeechCompletionTimer !==
            null
        ) {
          window.clearTimeout(
            nativeSpeechCompletionTimer
          );

          nativeSpeechCompletionTimer =
            null;
        }


        /*
         * Android bridge if this page is later opened in
         * the Loki's Kingdom app.
         */

        if (
          typeof window.AndroidTTS !==
            "undefined" &&
          typeof window.AndroidTTS.stop ===
            "function"
        ) {
          try {
            window.AndroidTTS.stop();

          } catch (
            error
          ) {
            console.error(
              error
            );
          }
        }


        if (
          "speechSynthesis"
          in window
        ) {
          window
            .speechSynthesis
            .cancel();
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // SPEAK
      // ===================================================

      function speakDialogue(
        text,
        speaker,
        onComplete
      ) {

        stopSpeech();


        if (
          !voiceEnabled
        ) {
          window.setTimeout(
            onComplete,
            50
          );


          return;
        }


        const cleanText =
          String(
            text || ""
          ).trim();


        if (
          !cleanText
        ) {
          window.setTimeout(
            onComplete,
            50
          );


          return;
        }


        waitingForSpeech =
          true;


        const displayName =
          speaker ===
            "nameless-man"
              ? "THE NAMELESS MAN"
              : (
                  publicState
                    ?.host
                    ?.name ||
                  "HOST"
                );


        speakingIndicator.textContent =
          (
            displayName +
            " IS SPEAKING..."
          );


        let finished =
          false;


        const completeOnce =
          () => {

            if (
              finished
            ) {
              return;
            }


            finished =
              true;


            waitingForSpeech =
              false;


            speakingIndicator.textContent =
              "";


            if (
              nativeSpeechCompletionTimer !==
                null
            ) {
              window.clearTimeout(
                nativeSpeechCompletionTimer
              );

              nativeSpeechCompletionTimer =
                null;
            }


            if (
              televisionOn &&
              autonomousLoopEnabled &&
              !interruptionInProgress &&
              !hostVacancy
            ) {
              onComplete();
            }
          };


        /*
         * =================================================
         * ANDROID NATIVE TTS — SAME PRIMARY PATH AS SALLY
         * =================================================
         *
         * Android WebView speechSynthesis is inconsistent.
         * Sally avoids that problem by preferring the native
         * window.AndroidTTS bridge whenever the APK exposes it.
         *
         * Recall now does the same thing.
         */
        const nativeSpeechSupported =
          typeof window.AndroidTTS !==
            "undefined" &&
          typeof window.AndroidTTS.speak ===
            "function";


        if (
          nativeSpeechSupported
        ) {
          try {
            window.AndroidTTS.speak(
              cleanText
            );


            /*
             * The existing bridge has no speech-finished callback.
             * Preserve the autonomous television loop with a bounded
             * duration estimate until the APK exposes one.
             */
            const wordCount =
              Math.max(
                1,
                cleanText
                  .split(/\s+/)
                  .filter(Boolean)
                  .length
              );


            const punctuationPauseCount =
              (
                cleanText.match(
                  /[.!?;:,]/g
                ) ||
                []
              ).length;


            const estimatedMilliseconds =
              Math.max(
                900,
                Math.min(
                  120000,
                  Math.round(
                    (
                      wordCount / 2.45
                    ) *
                      1000 +
                    punctuationPauseCount *
                      95 +
                    550
                  )
                )
              );


            nativeSpeechCompletionTimer =
              window.setTimeout(
                completeOnce,
                estimatedMilliseconds
              );


            return;

          } catch (
            error
          ) {
            console.error(
              "Android TTS failed; falling back to browser speech.",
              error
            );
          }
        }


        /*
         * Browser fallback for desktop and ordinary mobile browsers.
         */
        if (
          !(
            "speechSynthesis"
            in window
          ) ||
          !(
            "SpeechSynthesisUtterance"
            in window
          )
        ) {
          completeOnce();
          return;
        }


        /*
         * Mobile browsers may populate their voice list after load.
         * selectVoice() is deliberately called again for every line.
         */
        currentVoice =
          selectVoice(
            speaker
          );


        const utterance =
          new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(
            cleanText
          );


        if (
          currentVoice
        ) {
          utterance.voice =
            currentVoice;


          utterance.lang =
            currentVoice.lang;
        } else {
          utterance.lang =
            "en-US";
        }


        if (
          speaker ===
            "nameless-man"
        ) {
          utterance.rate =
            0.93;


          utterance.pitch =
            0.91;

        } else {
          utterance.rate =
            0.97;


          utterance.pitch =
            1.0;
        }


        utterance.volume =
          1;


        utterance.onend =
          completeOnce;


        utterance.onerror =
          completeOnce;


        try {
          window
            .speechSynthesis
            .speak(
              utterance
            );

        } catch (
          error
        ) {
          console.error(
            "Browser TTS failed:",
            error
          );

          completeOnce();
        }
      }

      // ===================================================
      // VOICE TOGGLE
      // ===================================================

      function renderVoiceButton() {

        voiceButton.textContent =
          voiceEnabled
            ? "VOICE: ON"
            : "VOICE: OFF";
      }


      voiceButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        () => {

          voiceEnabled =
            !voiceEnabled;


          localStorage.setItem(
            VOICE_KEY,
            String(
              voiceEnabled
            )
          );


          if (
            !voiceEnabled
          ) {
            stopSpeech();


            if (
              televisionOn &&
              autonomousLoopEnabled &&
              !turnInProgress &&
              !hostVacancy
            ) {
              window.setTimeout(
                requestNextTurn,
                100
              );
            }
          }


          renderVoiceButton();
        }
      );


      // ===================================================
      // GENERATION ABORT
      // ===================================================

      function abortCurrentGeneration() {

        if (
          currentGenerationController
        ) {
          try {
            currentGenerationController
              .abort();

          } catch (
            error
          ) {
            console.error(
              error
            );
          }
        }


        currentGenerationController =
          null;


        turnInProgress =
          false;
      }


      // ===================================================
      // HANDLE GENERATED DIALOGUE
      // ===================================================

      function handleDialogueResponse(
        data
      ) {

        if (!data) {
          return;
        }


        if (
          data.publicState
        ) {
          renderState(
            data.publicState
          );
        }


        if (
          data.combatEvent
        ) {
          appendCombatEvent(
            data.combatEvent
          );
        }


        if (
          data.text
        ) {

          const role =
            data.speaker ===
              "nameless-man"
                ? "nameless"
                : "host";


          const name =
            data.speaker ===
              "nameless-man"
                ? "THE NAMELESS MAN"
                : (
                    data.speakerName ||
                    publicState
                      ?.host
                      ?.name ||
                    "HOST"
                  );


          appendMessage(
            data.text,
            role,
            name
          );


          if (
            data.combatEvent
              ?.death
              ?.died
          ) {
            /*
             * The Nameless Man still gets to finish the line
             * associated with the killing attack.
             */

            speakDialogue(
              data.text,
              data.speaker,
              () => {

                hostVacancy =
                  true;


                openHostStore();
              }
            );


            return;
          }


          speakDialogue(
            data.text,
            data.speaker,
            () => {

              if (
                televisionOn &&
                autonomousLoopEnabled &&
                !hostVacancy
              ) {
                requestNextTurn();
              }
            }
          );
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // AUTONOMOUS NEXT TURN
      // ===================================================

      async function requestNextTurn() {

        if (
          !televisionOn ||
          !autonomousLoopEnabled ||
          turnInProgress ||
          interruptionInProgress ||
          hostVacancy
        ) {
          return;
        }


        turnInProgress =
          true;


        currentGenerationController =
          new AbortController();


        loadingCover.classList.add(
          "visible"
        );


        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              "/show/next",
              {
                method:
                  "POST",

                headers: {
                  "content-type":
                    "application/json"
                },

                body:
                  JSON.stringify({
                    visitorId:
                      visitorId
                  }),

                signal:
                  currentGenerationController
                    .signal
              }
            );


          if (
            result.response.status ===
              409 &&
            result.data.type ===
              "turn-already-running"
          ) {

            window.setTimeout(
              requestNextTurn,
              TURN_RETRY_MS
            );


            return;
          }


          if (
            result.response.status ===
              409 &&
            result.data.type ===
              "host-vacancy"
          ) {

            hostVacancy =
              true;


            await openHostStore();


            return;
          }


          if (
            !result.response.ok
          ) {
            throw new Error(
              result.data.error ||
              "Broadcast turn failed."
            );
          }


          handleDialogueResponse(
            result.data
          );


        } catch (
          error
        ) {

          if (
            error &&
            error.name ===
              "AbortError"
          ) {
            return;
          }


          appendMessage(
            (
              error.message ||
              "The broadcast signal broke."
            ),
            "system",
            "SYSTEM"
          );


          if (
            televisionOn &&
            autonomousLoopEnabled &&
            !hostVacancy
          ) {
            window.setTimeout(
              requestNextTurn,
              2500
            );
          }


        } finally {

          turnInProgress =
            false;


          currentGenerationController =
            null;


          loadingCover.classList.remove(
            "visible"
          );
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // TURN TV ON
      // ===================================================

      async function turnTelevisionOn() {

        powerButton.disabled =
          true;


        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              "/tv/on",
              {
                method:
                  "POST",

                headers: {
                  "content-type":
                    "application/json"
                },

                body:
                  JSON.stringify({
                    visitorId:
                      visitorId
                  })
              }
            );


          if (
            !result.response.ok
          ) {
            throw new Error(
              result.data.error ||
              "The television would not turn on."
            );
          }


          renderState(
            result.data
          );


          televisionOn =
            true;


          autonomousLoopEnabled =
            true;


          hostVacancy =
            Boolean(
              result.data.requiresHost
            ) ||
            (
              result.data.host
                ?.combat
                ?.alive ===
                  false
            );


          renderPowerState();


          startHeartbeat();


          startLiveTicker();


          if (
            hostVacancy
          ) {

            appendMessage(
              "THE POSITION OF HOST IS VACANT.",
              "system",
              "CRYPT NETWORK"
            );


            await openHostStore();


          } else {

            requestNextTurn();
          }


        } catch (
          error
        ) {

          appendMessage(
            (
              error.message ||
              "The television would not turn on."
            ),
            "system",
            "SYSTEM"
          );


        } finally {

          powerButton.disabled =
            false;
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // TURN TV OFF
      // ===================================================

      async function turnTelevisionOff() {

        /*
         * Highest priority operation.
         */

        autonomousLoopEnabled =
          false;


        televisionOn =
          false;


        stopSpeech();


        abortCurrentGeneration();


        if (
          currentInterruptionController
        ) {
          try {
            currentInterruptionController
              .abort();

          } catch (
            error
          ) {
            console.error(
              error
            );
          }
        }


        currentInterruptionController =
          null;


        interruptionInProgress =
          false;


        stopHeartbeat();


        renderPowerState();


        powerButton.disabled =
          true;


        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              "/tv/off",
              {
                method:
                  "POST",

                headers: {
                  "content-type":
                    "application/json"
                },

                body:
                  JSON.stringify({
                    visitorId:
                      visitorId
                  })
              }
            );


          if (
            result.response.ok
          ) {
            renderState(
              result.data
            );
          }


        } catch (
          error
        ) {

          console.error(
            error
          );


        } finally {

          powerButton.disabled =
            false;


          renderPowerState();
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // POWER BUTTON
      // ===================================================

      powerButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        async () => {

          if (
            televisionOn
          ) {
            await turnTelevisionOff();

          } else {
            await turnTelevisionOn();
          }
        }
      );


      // ===================================================
      // HEARTBEAT
      // ===================================================

      async function sendHeartbeat() {

        if (
          !televisionOn
        ) {
          return;
        }


        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              "/tv/heartbeat",
              {
                method:
                  "POST",

                headers: {
                  "content-type":
                    "application/json"
                },

                body:
                  JSON.stringify({
                    visitorId:
                      visitorId
                  })
              }
            );


          if (
            !result.response.ok
          ) {
            return;
          }


          const data =
            result.data;


          if (
            Number.isFinite(
              Number(
                data.statistics
                  ?.totalWatchSeconds
              )
            )
          ) {
            localWatchSeconds =
              Number(
                data.statistics
                  .totalWatchSeconds
              );
          }


          if (
            Number.isFinite(
              Number(
                data.elapsedShowSeconds
              )
            )
          ) {
            localShowSeconds =
              Number(
                data.elapsedShowSeconds
              );
          }


          if (
            data.showState &&
            publicState
          ) {
            publicState
              .television
              .showState =
                data.showState;
          }


          if (
            data.combatStartedNow
          ) {

            appendMessage(
              "THE COMBAT PHASE HAS BEGUN.",
              "combat-event",
              "CRYPT NETWORK"
            );


            /*
             * Pull full combat state.
             */

            await loadState();
          }


          if (
            data.hostilityStartedNow
          ) {
            appendMessage(
              "The atmosphere in the studio has changed.",
              "system",
              "CRYPT NETWORK"
            );
          }


          lastLocalTick =
            Date.now();


          renderStatistics();

          renderShowState();


        } catch (
          error
        ) {
          console.error(
            "Heartbeat failed:",
            error
          );
        }
      }


      function startHeartbeat() {

        stopHeartbeat();


        heartbeatTimer =
          window.setInterval(
            sendHeartbeat,
            HEARTBEAT_MS
          );


        sendHeartbeat();
      }


      function stopHeartbeat() {

        if (
          heartbeatTimer
        ) {
          window.clearInterval(
            heartbeatTimer
          );


          heartbeatTimer =
            null;
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // LIVE ONE-SECOND DISPLAY TICKER
      // ===================================================

      function startLiveTicker() {

        if (
          liveTimer
        ) {
          return;
        }


        lastLocalTick =
          Date.now();


        liveTimer =
          window.setInterval(
            () => {

              const now =
                Date.now();


              let elapsed =
                (
                  now -
                  lastLocalTick
                ) /
                1000;


              lastLocalTick =
                now;


              if (
                !televisionOn
              ) {
                return;
              }


              /*
               * This is display prediction only.
               *
               * Server remains authoritative.
               */

              elapsed =
                Math.max(
                  0,
                  Math.min(
                    2,
                    elapsed
                  )
                );


              localWatchSeconds +=
                elapsed;


              localShowSeconds +=
                elapsed;


              if (
                publicState
                  ?.television
                  ?.showState ===
                    "combat"
              ) {
                localCombatSeconds +=
                  elapsed;
              }


              renderStatistics();

            },
            1000
          );
      }


      // ===================================================
      // PLAYER INTERJECTION
      // ===================================================

      async function interject(
        message
      ) {

        const text =
          String(
            message ||
            ""
          )
            .trim();


        if (
          !text ||
          !televisionOn
        ) {
          return;
        }


        /*
         * PLAYER INTERRUPTION > CURRENT SPEECH.
         */

        stopSpeech();


        abortCurrentGeneration();


        autonomousLoopEnabled =
          false;


        interruptionInProgress =
          true;


        appendMessage(
          text,
          "audience",
          "AUDIENCE"
        );


        audienceInput.value =
          "";


        speakButton.disabled =
          true;


        speakingIndicator.textContent =
          "AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION...";


        if (
          currentInterruptionController
        ) {
          try {
            currentInterruptionController
              .abort();

          } catch (
            error
          ) {
            console.error(
              error
            );
          }
        }


        currentInterruptionController =
          new AbortController();


        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              "/show/interject",
              {
                method:
                  "POST",

                headers: {
                  "content-type":
                    "application/json"
                },

                body:
                  JSON.stringify({
                    visitorId:
                      visitorId,

                    message:
                      text
                  }),

                signal:
                  currentInterruptionController
                    .signal
              }
            );


          /*
           * The server may have still been finishing a turn
           * that the browser abandoned.
           *
           * The interjection itself has already been stored.
           */

          if (
            result.response.status ===
              202 &&
            result.data
              .generationPending
          ) {

            appendMessage(
              "The interruption has reached the studio.",
              "system",
              "CRYPT NETWORK"
            );


            await waitForInterruptedTurnToClear();


            return;
          }


          if (
            !result.response.ok
          ) {
            throw new Error(
              result.data.error ||
              "The audience microphone failed."
            );
          }


          if (
            result.data.publicState
          ) {
            renderState(
              result.data.publicState
            );
          }


          if (
            result.data.combatEvent
          ) {
            appendCombatEvent(
              result.data.combatEvent
            );
          }


          if (
            result.data.text
          ) {

            const role =
              result.data.speaker ===
                "nameless-man"
                  ? "nameless"
                  : "host";


            const name =
              result.data.speaker ===
                "nameless-man"
                  ? "THE NAMELESS MAN"
                  : (
                      result.data.speakerName ||
                      publicState
                        ?.host
                        ?.name ||
                      "HOST"
                    );


            appendMessage(
              result.data.text,
              role,
              name
            );


            interruptionInProgress =
              false;


            autonomousLoopEnabled =
              true;


            speakButton.disabled =
              false;


            speakDialogue(
              result.data.text,
              result.data.speaker,
              () => {

                if (
                  televisionOn &&
                  !hostVacancy
                ) {
                  requestNextTurn();
                }
              }
            );
          }


        } catch (
          error
        ) {

          if (
            error &&
            error.name ===
              "AbortError"
          ) {
            return;
          }


          appendMessage(
            (
              error.message ||
              "The audience microphone failed."
            ),
            "system",
            "SYSTEM"
          );


          interruptionInProgress =
            false;


          autonomousLoopEnabled =
            true;


          speakButton.disabled =
            false;


          if (
            televisionOn &&
            !hostVacancy
          ) {
            window.setTimeout(
              requestNextTurn,
              TURN_RETRY_MS
            );
          }


        } finally {

          currentInterruptionController =
            null;


          if (
            !waitingForSpeech
          ) {
            speakingIndicator.textContent =
              "";
          }
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // INTERRUPTED TURN RECOVERY
      // ===================================================

      /*
       * If the abandoned server generation still owns the
       * request lock, give it a moment to finish.
       *
       * Then resume from authoritative persisted state.
       *
       * We do NOT repeatedly POST the same audience message,
       * because that would duplicate the audience entry.
       */

      async function waitForInterruptedTurnToClear() {

        let attempts =
          0;


        const retry =
          async () => {

            if (
              !televisionOn
            ) {
              return;
            }


            attempts +=
              1;


            await loadState();


            if (
              attempts <
                6
            ) {

              window.setTimeout(
                retry,
                INTERRUPTION_RETRY_MS
              );


              return;
            }


            interruptionInProgress =
              false;


            autonomousLoopEnabled =
              true;


            speakButton.disabled =
              false;


            speakingIndicator.textContent =
              "";


            if (
              !hostVacancy
            ) {
              requestNextTurn();
            }
          };


        window.setTimeout(
          retry,
          INTERRUPTION_RETRY_MS
        );
      }


      // ===================================================
      // AUDIENCE FORM
      // ===================================================

      audienceForm.addEventListener(
        "submit",
        async (
          event
        ) => {

          event.preventDefault();


          const text =
            audienceInput
              .value
              .trim();


          if (!text) {
            return;
          }


          await interject(
            text
          );
        }
      );


      audienceInput.addEventListener(
        "keydown",
        (
          event
        ) => {

          if (
            event.key ===
              "Enter" &&
            !event.shiftKey
          ) {
            event.preventDefault();


            if (
              !speakButton.disabled
            ) {
              audienceForm
                .requestSubmit();
            }
          }
        }
      );


      // ===================================================
      // HOST STORE
      // ===================================================

      async function openHostStore() {

        hostStore.classList.add(
          "visible"
        );


        candidateGrid.innerHTML =
          "";


        storeGold.textContent =
          "LOADING CAST...";


        try {

          const result =
            await fetchJson(
              (
                "/host-store?visitorId=" +
                encodeURIComponent(
                  visitorId
                )
              )
            );


          if (
            !result.response.ok
          ) {
            throw new Error(
              result.data.error ||
              "Casting office unavailable."
            );
          }


          storeGold.textContent =
            (
              "GOLD: " +
              (
                result.data.playerGold ??
                0
              )
            );


          const candidates =
            Array.isArray(
              result.data.candidates
            )
              ? result.data.candidates
              : [];


          for (
            const candidate
            of candidates
          ) {
            candidateGrid.appendChild(
              buildCandidateCard(
                candidate,
                result.data.playerGold
              )
            );
          }


        } catch (
          error
        ) {

          candidateGrid.innerHTML =
            "";


          const failure =
            document.createElement(
              "div"
            );


          failure.className =
            "candidate";


          failure.textContent =
            (
              error.message ||
              "The casting office is closed."
            );


          candidateGrid.appendChild(
            failure
          );
        }
      }


      // ===================================================
      // CANDIDATE CARD
      // ===================================================

      function buildCandidateCard(
        candidate,
        playerGold
      ) {

        const card =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        card.className =
          "candidate";


        const title =
          document.createElement(
            "h3"
          );


        title.textContent =
          candidate.name ||
          "NAME_VAR";


        card.appendChild(
          title
        );


        const descriptionLines =
          [
            (
              (
                candidate.sex ||
                "unknown"
              ) +
              " / " +
              (
                candidate.species ||
                "unknown"
              )
            ),

            (
              "ARCHETYPE: " +
              (
                candidate.archetype
                  ?.name ||
                candidate.archetype ||
                "unknown"
              )
            ),

            (
              "CLOTHES: " +
              (
                candidate.appearance
                  ?.clothing ||
                "none"
              )
            ),

            (
              "SHOES: " +
              (
                candidate.appearance
                  ?.footwear ||
                "none"
              )
            ),

            (
              "POWER: " +
              (
                candidate.powerClass
                  ?.label ||
                "unknown"
              )
            )
          ];


        for (
          const line
          of descriptionLines
        ) {

          const detail =
            document.createElement(
              "div"
            );


          detail.className =
            "candidate-detail";


          detail.textContent =
            line;


          card.appendChild(
            detail
          );
        }


        const match =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        match.className =
          "candidate-match";


        match.textContent =
          (
            (
              candidate.matchup
                ?.class ||
              "UNKNOWN MATCH"
            ) +
            (
              candidate.matchup
                ?.estimatedYears
                ? (
                    " — APPROX. " +
                    candidate.matchup
                      .estimatedYears +
                    " YEARS"
                  )
                : ""
            )
          );


        card.appendChild(
          match
        );


        const matchDescription =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        matchDescription.className =
          "candidate-detail";


        matchDescription.textContent =
          candidate.matchup
            ?.description ||
          "";


        card.appendChild(
          matchDescription
        );


        const price =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        price.className =
          "candidate-price";


        price.textContent =
          (
            "$" +
            Number(
              candidate.purchasePrice ||
              0
            )
              .toLocaleString()
          );


        card.appendChild(
          price
        );


        /*
         * NAME
         */

        const nameInput =
          document.createElement(
            "input"
          );


        nameInput.type =
          "text";


        nameInput.maxLength =
          80;


        nameInput.value =
          candidate.name ||
          "";


        nameInput.placeholder =
          "Host name";


        card.appendChild(
          nameInput
        );


        /*
         * SEX
         */

        const sexSelect =
          document.createElement(
            "select"
          );


        for (
          const value
          of [
            "female",
            "male"
          ]
        ) {

          const option =
            document.createElement(
              "option"
            );


          option.value =
            value;


          option.textContent =
            value.toUpperCase();


          option.selected =
            (
              candidate.sex ===
                value
            );


          sexSelect.appendChild(
            option
          );
        }


        card.appendChild(
          sexSelect
        );


        /*
         * HAIR COLOR
         */

        const hairColor =
          document.createElement(
            "select"
          );


        const hairColors =
          candidate
            .customizationOptions
            ?.hairColors ||
          [];


        for (
          const entry
          of hairColors
        ) {

          const option =
            document.createElement(
              "option"
            );


          option.value =
            entry.id;


          option.textContent =
            (
              entry.name +
              (
                entry.price
                  ? (
                      " +$" +
                      Number(
                        entry.price
                      )
                        .toLocaleString()
                    )
                  : ""
              )
            );


          option.selected =
            (
              candidate.cosmetics
                ?.hairColor ===
                  entry.id
            );


          hairColor.appendChild(
            option
          );
        }


        card.appendChild(
          hairColor
        );


        /*
         * HAIR STYLE
         */

        const hairstyle =
          document.createElement(
            "select"
          );


        const hairstyles =
          candidate
            .customizationOptions
            ?.hairstyles ||
          [];


        for (
          const entry
          of hairstyles
        ) {

          const option =
            document.createElement(
              "option"
            );


          option.value =
