            entry.id;


          option.textContent =
            (
              entry.name +
              (
                entry.price
                  ? (
                      " +$" +
                      Number(
                        entry.price
                      )
                        .toLocaleString()
                    )
                  : ""
              )
            );


          option.selected =
            (
              candidate.cosmetics
                ?.hairstyle ===
                  entry.id
            );


          hairstyle.appendChild(
            option
          );
        }


        card.appendChild(
          hairstyle
        );


        const hire =
          document.createElement(
            "button"
          );


        hire.type =
          "button";


        hire.textContent =
          "HIRE HOST";


        if (
          Number(
            playerGold ||
            0
          ) <
          Number(
            candidate
              .purchasePrice ||
            0
          )
        ) {
          hire.title =
            "You may not be able to afford this configuration.";
        }


        hire.addEventListener(
          "click",
          async () => {

            hire.disabled =
              true;


            hire.textContent =
              "HIRING...";


            try {

              const result =
                await fetchJson(
                  "/host-store/hire",
                  {
                    method:
                      "POST",

                    headers: {
                      "content-type":
                        "application/json"
                    },

                    body:
                      JSON.stringify({
                        visitorId:
                          visitorId,

                        candidateId:
                          candidate
                            .candidateId,

                        customization: {
                          name:
                            nameInput
                              .value
                              .trim(),

                          sex:
                            sexSelect
                              .value,

                          hairColor:
                            hairColor
                              .value,

                          hairstyle:
                            hairstyle
                              .value
                        }
                      })
                  }
                );


              if (
                !result.response.ok
              ) {

                if (
                  result.data.type ===
                    "insufficient-gold"
                ) {
                  throw new Error(
                    (
                      "YOU NEED $" +
                      Number(
                        result.data
                          .requiredGold ||
                        0
                      )
                        .toLocaleString() +
                      ". YOU HAVE $" +
                      Number(
                        result.data
                          .currentGold ||
                        0
                      )
                        .toLocaleString() +
                      "."
                    )
                  );
                }


                throw new Error(
                  result.data.error ||
                  "The host could not be hired."
                );
              }


              appendMessage(
                (
                  result.data.host.name +
                  " HAS BEEN HIRED."
                ),
                "system",
                "CRYPT NETWORK"
              );


              if (
                result.data.publicState
              ) {
                renderState(
                  result.data
                    .publicState
                );
              }


              hostVacancy =
                false;


              hostStore.classList.remove(
                "visible"
              );


              /*
               * NEW HOST:
               *
               * Nameless Man opens with:
               *
               * recall thy name
               */

              autonomousLoopEnabled =
                true;


              await loadState();


              requestNextTurn();


            } catch (
              error
            ) {

              window.alert(
                error.message ||
                "Hiring failed."
              );


              hire.disabled =
                false;


              hire.textContent =
                "HIRE HOST";
            }
          }
        );


        card.appendChild(
          hire
        );


        return card;
      }


      // ===================================================
      // STORE BUTTONS
      // ===================================================

      storeButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        () => {

          openHostStore();
        }
      );


      closeStoreButton.addEventListener(
        "click",
        () => {

          hostStore.classList.remove(
            "visible"
          );
        }
      );


      // ===================================================
      // INITIALIZATION
      // ===================================================

      async function initialize() {

        renderVoiceButton();

        createJukeboxInterface();


        if (
          "speechSynthesis"
          in window
        ) {

          window
            .speechSynthesis
            .getVoices();


          window
            .speechSynthesis
            .onvoiceschanged =
              () => {

                currentVoice =
                  null;
              };
        }


        await loadState();


        startLiveTicker();


        if (
          televisionOn
        ) {

          autonomousLoopEnabled =
            true;


          startHeartbeat();


          /*
           * Refresh-resume behavior:
           *
           * the server owns the real persistent state.
           *
           * If a host is alive, simply continue the show.
           */

          if (
            hostVacancy
          ) {
            await openHostStore();

          } else {
            requestNextTurn();
          }
        }


        audienceInput.focus();
      }

      // ===================================================
      // EFSMB JUKEBOX — PART 9 CLIENT HOOK
      // ===================================================

      /*
       * The actual music catalogue remains owned by
       * Loki's Kingdom / DesktopMusicApp.
       *
       * recall thy name opens that existing working
       * jukebox inside a television overlay instead of
       * duplicating thousands of catalogue entries.
       *
       * This means additions to the real jukebox remain
       * available here automatically.
       */


      let jukeboxOverlay =
        null;


      let jukeboxFrame =
        null;


      let jukeboxVisible =
        false;


      function createJukeboxInterface() {

        if (
          jukeboxOverlay
        ) {
          return;
        }


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * BUTTON
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        const button =
          document.createElement(
            "button"
          );


        button.id =
          "jukeboxButton";


        button.type =
          "button";


        button.className =
          "small-button";


        button.textContent =
          "JUKEBOX";


        /*
         * Place beside the existing television controls.
         */

        const controls =
          document.querySelector(
            ".controls"
          );


        if (
          controls
        ) {
          controls.appendChild(
            button
          );
        }


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * OVERLAY
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        jukeboxOverlay =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        jukeboxOverlay.id =
          "jukeboxOverlay";


        Object.assign(
          jukeboxOverlay.style,
          {
            display:
              "none",

            position:
              "fixed",

            inset:
              "0",

            zIndex:
              "10000",

            background:
              "rgba(0,0,0,0.96)",

            padding:
              "12px"
          }
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * PANEL
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        const panel =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        Object.assign(
          panel.style,
          {
            width:
              "min(1400px, 100%)",

            height:
              "100%",

            margin:
              "0 auto",

            display:
              "flex",

            flexDirection:
              "column",

            background:
              "#080808",

            border:
              "1px solid #444"
          }
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * HEADER
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        const header =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        Object.assign(
          header.style,
          {
            display:
              "flex",

            alignItems:
              "center",

            justifyContent:
              "space-between",

            gap:
              "12px",

            padding:
              "10px 12px",

            borderBottom:
              "1px solid #333",

            background:
              "#111"
          }
        );


        const title =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        title.textContent =
          "THE EFSMB JUKEBOX";


        Object.assign(
          title.style,
          {
            fontFamily:
              "Georgia, serif",

            fontSize:
              "20px"
          }
        );


        const close =
          document.createElement(
            "button"
          );


        close.type =
          "button";


        close.className =
          "small-button";


        close.textContent =
          "RETURN TO TV";


        header.appendChild(
          title
        );


        header.appendChild(
          close
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * STATUS
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        const status =
          document.createElement(
            "div"
          );


        status.id =
          "jukeboxStatus";


        status.textContent =
          "LOADING JUKEBOX...";


        Object.assign(
          status.style,
          {
            padding:
              "5px 12px",

            color:
              "#888",

            fontFamily:
              "Arial, sans-serif",

            fontSize:
              "11px",

            borderBottom:
              "1px solid #222"
          }
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * IFRAME
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        jukeboxFrame =
          document.createElement(
            "iframe"
          );


        jukeboxFrame.title =
          "THE EFSMB JUKEBOX";


        jukeboxFrame.allow =
          "autoplay";


        Object.assign(
          jukeboxFrame.style,
          {
            display:
              "block",

            flex:
              "1",

            width:
              "100%",

            minHeight:
              "0",

            border:
              "0",

            background:
              "#000"
          }
        );


        panel.appendChild(
          header
        );


        panel.appendChild(
          status
        );


        panel.appendChild(
          jukeboxFrame
        );


        jukeboxOverlay.appendChild(
          panel
        );


        document.body.appendChild(
          jukeboxOverlay
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * OPEN
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        button.addEventListener(
          "click",
          async () => {

            jukeboxVisible =
              true;


            jukeboxOverlay.style.display =
              "block";


            /*
             * Do NOT turn off the TV.
             *
             * The television show continues underneath.
             *
             * The player is merely looking at the jukebox.
             */


            if (
              !jukeboxFrame.src
            ) {

              try {

                const result =
                  await fetchJson(
                    (
                      "/jukebox/catalog?visitorId=" +
                      encodeURIComponent(
                        visitorId
                      )
                    )
                  );


                if (
                  !result.response.ok
                ) {
                  throw new Error(
                    result.data.error ||
                    "The jukebox could not be located."
                  );
                }


                const url =
                  result.data
                    .jukeboxUrl;


                if (!url) {
                  throw new Error(
                    "No jukebox URL was returned."
                  );
                }


                status.textContent =
                  (
                    result.data.mode ===
                      "existing-efsmb-jukebox"
                      ? "CONNECTED TO LOKI'S KINGDOM MUSIC CATALOGUE"
                      : "JUKEBOX CONNECTED"
                  );


                jukeboxFrame.src =
                  url;


              } catch (
                error
              ) {

                status.textContent =
                  (
                    error.message ||
                    "JUKEBOX SIGNAL LOST"
                  );
              }
            }
          }
        );


        /*
         * -------------------------------------------------
         * CLOSE
         * -------------------------------------------------
         */

        close.addEventListener(
          "click",
          () => {

            jukeboxVisible =
              false;


            jukeboxOverlay.style.display =
              "none";


            audienceInput.focus();
          }
        );


        /*
         * ESC also returns to television.
         */

        window.addEventListener(
          "keydown",
          (
            event
          ) => {

            if (
              event.key ===
                "Escape" &&
              jukeboxVisible
            ) {

              jukeboxVisible =
                false;


              jukeboxOverlay.style.display =
                "none";


              audienceInput.focus();
            }
          }
        );
      }
      initialize();

    })();

  </script>

</body>

</html>
`;


// =========================================================
// PART 8 CLIENT/SERVER OWNERSHIP PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * BROWSER OWNS:
 *
 * - rendering;
 * - TTS playback;
 * - stopping TTS;
 * - local visual interpolation of clocks;
 * - player text input;
 * - requesting the next turn;
 * - showing the Host Store.
 *
 *
 * SERVER OWNS:
 *
 * - actual watch seconds;
 * - actual show elapsed time;
 * - combat threshold;
 * - hostility threshold;
 * - damage;
 * - XP;
 * - levels;
 * - host death;
 * - host purchase;
 * - stored host;
 * - long-term memory;
 * - historical event cache;
 * - persistent conversation state.
 */


// =========================================================
// TTS LOOP PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * SERVER RETURNS DIALOGUE
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * BROWSER DISPLAYS IT
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * TTS SPEAKS IT
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * onend
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * /show/next
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * OTHER HOST SPEAKS
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * REPEAT
 *
 *
 * Therefore the player can simply sit there.
 *
 * No CONTINUE button is required for ordinary operation.
 */


// =========================================================
// INTERRUPTION PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * PLAYER TYPES:
 *
 *     "Do you believe in ghosts?"
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * BROWSER:
 *
 * - immediately stops TTS;
 * - aborts its current fetch where possible;
 * - freezes ordinary autonomous chaining;
 * - POSTs /show/interject;
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * RESPONDING HOST ADDRESSES GHOSTS
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * TTS COMPLETES
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * OTHER HOST RESPONDS
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * NORMAL AUTONOMOUS LOOP CONTINUES.
 *
 *
 * COMBAT DOES NOT RESET.
 */


// =========================================================
// WATCH CLOCK PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * SERVER COUNTER:
 *
 *     canonical
 *
 *
 * BROWSER COUNTER:
 *
 *     visual interpolation between heartbeats
 *
 *
 * Therefore the player sees:
 *
 *     412 hours, 47 minutes, 6 seconds
 *
 * tick:
 *
 *     412 hours, 47 minutes, 7 seconds
 *
 * live on screen.
 *
 *
 * The bracketed representation remains:
 *
 *     [17 days, 4 hours, 47 minutes, 7 seconds]
 */


// =========================================================
// REFRESH PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * REFRESH:
 *
 *     browser loses only ephemeral DOM rendering;
 *
 *     server retains:
 *
 * - episode count;
 * - accumulated watch time;
 * - current host;
 * - host HP;
 * - Nameless Man;
 * - Nameless level;
 * - Nameless XP;
 * - Nameless spells;
 * - conversation topic;
 * - lock state;
 * - relationship history;
 * - memories;
 * - combat state;
 * - combat elapsed time;
 * - historical date.
 *
 *
 * The show resumes from authoritative state.
 */


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT:
// JUKEBOX IS NOT YET DRAWN IN PART 8
// =========================================================

/*
 * PART 1 already reserves:
 *
 *     /jukebox/catalog
 *
 * and:
 *
 *     state.jukebox
 *
 *
 * The television interface is deliberately complete enough
 * for PART 9 to insert the actual working jukebox without
 * rewriting this page architecture.
 */


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT:
// HOST STORE REMAINS CASTING
// =========================================================

/*
 * The UI intentionally displays:
 *
 *     PRICE
 *
 * separately from:
 *
 *     POWER
 *
 * and:
 *
 *     EXPECTED MATCHUP.
 *
 *
 * Therefore the joke is visible.
 *
 *
 * Example:
 *
 *     ONE-HIT WEAKLING
 *     PRESTIGE ATHLETIC SNEAKERS
 *     $5,000+
 *
 *
 * beside:
 *
 *     GOD-TITAN
 *     NAKED
 *     $1,000-ish
 *
 *
 * Power does not become the price axis merely because the
 * interface exposes both.
 */


// =========================================================
// END OF PART 8
//
// APPEND PART 9 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME
// MASTER BUILD — PART 9
//
// THE EFSMB JUKEBOX
// EXISTING LOKI'S KINGDOM MUSIC SYSTEM
// CATALOGUE CONNECTION
// MUSIC STATE
// NON-DESTRUCTIVE INTEGRATION
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// JUKEBOX CONFIGURATION
// =========================================================

/*
 * We deliberately DO NOT duplicate musicCatalog.js
 * inside this Worker.
 *
 * Loki's Kingdom already owns the canonical catalogue.
 *
 * recall thy name therefore treats the actual existing
 * jukebox as another machine in the same kingdom.
 */

const EFSMB_JUKEBOX_URL =
  "https://www.lokiskingdom.com/";


const EFSMB_R2_BASE =
  "https://pub-4ae7f49887bc46e9a03de6e38886b609.r2.dev/";


// =========================================================
// JUKEBOX CATALOG ENDPOINT
// =========================================================

async function handleJukeboxCatalog(
  request,
  env,
  url
) {

  const visitorId =
    normalizeVisitorId(
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitorId"
        ) ??
      url
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "visitor_id"
        )
    );


  /*
   * The jukebox itself can technically be opened without a
   * game visitor ID.
   *
   * But inside recall thy name we want one common identity.
   */

  if (!visitorId) {

    return jsonResponse(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Missing or invalid visitorId."
      },
      400
    );
  }


  let state =
    null;


  try {

    state =
      await loadRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId
      );


  } catch (
    error
  ) {

    console.error(
      "Jukebox state load failed:",
      error
    );
  }


  return jsonResponse(
    {
      ok:
        true,

      mode:
        "existing-efsmb-jukebox",

      title:
        "THE EFSMB JUKEBOX",

      jukeboxUrl:
        EFSMB_JUKEBOX_URL,

      r2Base:
        EFSMB_R2_BASE,

      integration: {
        catalogueOwner:
          "Loki's Kingdom",

        duplicateCatalogue:
          false,

        embedded:
          true,

        televisionContinues:
          true
      },

      currentState:
        state
          ?.jukebox ||
        createDefaultJukeboxState()
    }
  );
}


// =========================================================
// JUKEBOX STATE NORMALIZER
// =========================================================

function normalizeJukeboxState(
  value
) {

  const source =
    value &&
    typeof value ===
      "object"
      ? value
      : {};


  return {
    enabled:
      source.enabled !==
        false,

    currentAlbumId:
      normalizeString(
        source.currentAlbumId,
        "",
        500
      ) ||
      null,

    currentSongId:
      normalizeString(
        source.currentSongId,
        "",
        500
      ) ||
      null,

    currentSongTitle:
      normalizeString(
        source.currentSongTitle,
        "",
        1000
      ) ||
      null,

    playing:
      Boolean(
        source.playing
      ),

    positionSeconds:
      Math.max(
        0,
        finiteNumber(
          source.positionSeconds,
          0
        )
      ),

    volume:
      clamp(
        finiteNumber(
          source.volume,
          1
        ),
        0,
        1
      ),

    shuffle:
      Boolean(
        source.shuffle
      ),

    repeat:
      Boolean(
        source.repeat
      )
  };
}


// =========================================================
// JUKEBOX / TELEVISION AUDIO PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * JUKEBOX AUDIO DOES NOT STOP THE SHOW.
 *
 *
 * The player can choose:
 *
 * 1. TV voices only.
 *
 * 2. Music only by disabling TV VOICE.
 *
 * 3. Music + host speech simultaneously.
 *
 *
 * We do NOT automatically turn off:
 *
 *     recall thy name
 *
 * simply because a record is playing.
 *
 *
 * This matters because the intended television can be an
 * endurance experience.
 */


// =========================================================
// WHY THE CATALOGUE IS NOT COPIED INTO THIS WORKER
// =========================================================

/*
 * Loki's Kingdom's existing desktop jukebox uses:
 *
 *     musicCatalog.js
 *
 *     ↓
 *
 *     DesktopMusicApp
 *
 *     ↓
 *
 *     R2 AUDIO
 *
 *
 * The catalogue contains thousands of tracks.
 *
 *
 * COPYING THE CATALOG HERE WOULD CREATE:
 *
 * - two giant catalogues;
 * - two places that must be updated;
 * - catalogue drift;
 * - a much larger Worker;
 * - unnecessary maintenance;
 * - another place for filename mistakes.
 *
 *
 * INSTEAD:
 *
 *     RECALL THY NAME
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 *     EMBED CANONICAL JUKEBOX
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 *     LOKI'S KINGDOM
 *
 *
 * One collection.
 *
 * One actual player.
 */


// =========================================================
// EXISTING PLAYER CAPABILITIES
// =========================================================

/*
 * The canonical jukebox already provides the important
 * basic player behavior:
 *
 * - album selection;
 * - song selection;
 * - current album;
 * - now playing;
 * - HTML audio controls;
 * - previous;
 * - next;
 * - artwork;
 * - lyrics.
 *
 *
 * recall thy name therefore receives those capabilities
 * without creating a watered-down imitation.
 */


// =========================================================
// JUKEBOX DOES NOT DRIVE CONVERSATION
// =========================================================

/*
 * Playing a song does NOT automatically force:
 *
 *     "LET'S TALK ABOUT THIS SONG"
 *
 *
 * The television conversation remains autonomous.
 *
 *
 * Later, if desired, a specific engine mechanic may allow:
 *
 *     NOW PLAYING
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 *     HOSTS NOTICE SONG
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 *     OPTIONAL DISCUSSION
 *
 *
 * But that is not silently invented here.
 */


// =========================================================
// PLAYER INTERRUPTION WHILE JUKEBOX IS OPEN
// =========================================================

/*
 * The current Part 9 overlay covers the television UI while
 * open.
 *
 * RETURN TO TV:
 *
 *     immediately restores access to the audience textbox.
 *
 *
 * The show itself has continued underneath.
 *
 *
 * Therefore the jukebox is not:
 *
 *     TURN OFF THE TV.
 *
 *
 * It is merely another screen the viewer is looking at.
 */


// =========================================================
// MUSIC VS TTS
// =========================================================

/*
 * It is perfectly legal for:
 *
 *     EFSMB SONG
 *
 * and:
 *
 *     HOST TTS
 *
 * to play simultaneously.
 *
 *
 * If that sounds insane:
 *
 *     VOICE: OFF
 *
 * exists.
 *
 *
 * We do not secretly pause the music every time a host
 * speaks.
 */


// =========================================================
// MUSIC CATALOGUE UPDATE PRINCIPLE
// =========================================================

/*
 * New album added to:
 *
 *     musicCatalog.js
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * Loki's Kingdom rebuild/deploy
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * Canonical jukebox contains album
 *
 *         ↓
 *
 * recall thy name sees the updated canonical jukebox.
 *
 *
 * NO RECALL THY NAME WORKER UPDATE REQUIRED.
 */


// =========================================================
// FUTURE DIRECT-CATALOG MODE
// =========================================================

/*
 * If Loki's Kingdom later exposes:
 *
 *     /music-catalog.json
 *
 * we can replace the iframe approach with a completely
 * native dropdown inside recall thy name.
 *
 *
 * Nothing in the persistence architecture prevents this.
 *
 *
 * The present mode is intentionally:
 *
 *     USE THE PLAYER THAT ALREADY WORKS.
 */


// =========================================================
// PART 9 COMPLETION ASSERTION
// =========================================================

if (
  typeof handleJukeboxCatalog !==
    "function"
) {

  throw new Error(
    "RECALL THY NAME jukebox endpoint was not initialized."
  );
}


// =========================================================
// END OF PART 9
//
// APPEND PART 10 DIRECTLY BELOW THIS LINE.
// =========================================================
// =========================================================
// RECALL THY NAME
// MASTER BUILD — PART 10
//
// FINAL INTEGRATION
// SAVE MIGRATION
// RELATIONSHIP PERSISTENCE FIX
// ORIGINAL HOST INITIALIZATION
// AUDIENCE INTERRUPTION RECOVERY
// STATE HARDENING
// COMPATIBILITY GUARDS
// FINAL BUILD ASSERTIONS
// =========================================================


// =========================================================
// FINAL BUILD VERSION
// =========================================================

const RECALL_FINAL_BUILD =
  "recall-thy-name-v1-complete";


const RECALL_FINAL_BUILD_DATE =
  "2026-08-12";


// =========================================================
// IMPORTANT PART 10 FIX:
//
// PART 7 INTRODUCED:
//
//     state.relationship
//
// but the original PART 1 state normalizer did not know
// that field existed.
//
// saveRecallState() always normalizes before saving.
//
// Therefore, without this Part 10 migration layer,
// relationship history could be discarded during a save.
//
// Part 10 teaches the state normalizer about the later
// relationship system.
//
// NO OLD SAVE WIPE.
// =========================================================

const normalizeRecallStateBeforePart10 =
  normalizeRecallState;


normalizeRecallState =
  function (
    candidate
  ) {

    const source =
      (
        candidate &&
        typeof candidate ===
          "object"
      )
        ? candidate
        : {};


    const normalized =
      normalizeRecallStateBeforePart10(
        source
      );


    // =====================================================
    // RELATIONSHIP PERSISTENCE
    // =====================================================

    if (
      source.relationship &&
      typeof source.relationship ===
        "object"
    ) {

      normalized.relationship =
        structuredClone(
          source.relationship
        );

    } else {

      normalized.relationship =
        createDefaultExtendedRelationshipState();
    }


    ensureExtendedRelationshipState(
      normalized
    );


    // =====================================================
    // HOST MIGRATION
    // =====================================================

    migrateCurrentHostState(
      normalized
    );


    // =====================================================
    // NAMELESS MAN MIGRATION
    // =====================================================

    migrateNamelessState(
      normalized
    );


    // =====================================================
    // COMBAT MIGRATION
    // =====================================================

    migrateCombatState(
      normalized
    );


    // =====================================================
    // JUKEBOX MIGRATION
    // =====================================================

    normalized.jukebox =
      normalizeJukeboxState(
        source.jukebox ??
        normalized.jukebox
      );


    normalized.version =
      RECALL_FINAL_BUILD;


    return normalized;
  };


// =========================================================
// CURRENT HOST SAVE MIGRATION
// =========================================================

function migrateCurrentHostState(
  state
) {

  if (
    !state.host ||
    typeof state.host !==
      "object"
  ) {

    state.host =
      createDefaultHost();
  }


  const host =
    state.host;


  /*
   * The original bootstrap host in PART 1 was created
   * before the Host Store existed.
   *
   * Therefore its placeholder name may literally be:
   *
   *     NAME_VAR
   *
   * That is not intended to be the actual character name.
   *
   * Convert that untouched bootstrap host into a proper
   * generated television host.
   *
   * IMPORTANT:
   *
   * A player-created host who deliberately names themselves
   * "NAME_VAR" should not subsequently be changed.
   *
   * We therefore identify the untouched original host using
   * the zero-price / null-candidate bootstrap signature.
   */

  const untouchedBootstrapHost =
    (
      host.name ===
        "NAME_VAR" &&

      (
        host.store
          ?.candidateId ??
        null
      ) ===
        null &&

      finiteNumber(
        host.store
          ?.purchasePrice,
        0
      ) ===
        0
    );


  if (
    untouchedBootstrapHost
  ) {

    const sex =
      (
        host.sex ===
          "male" ||
        host.sex ===
          "female"
      )
        ? host.sex
        : (
            Math.random() <
              0.5
              ? "female"
              : "male"
          );


    host.sex =
      sex;


    host.name =
      generateHostName(
        sex
      );


    if (
      !host.personalitySeed
    ) {

      host.personalitySeed = {
        sociability:
          randomInteger(
            -30,
            30
          ),

        seriousness:
          randomInteger(
            -30,
            30
          ),

        argumentativeness:
          randomInteger(
            -30,
            30
          ),

        humor:
          randomInteger(
            -30,
            30
          ),

        curiosity:
          randomInteger(
            -30,
            30
          ),

        confidence:
          randomInteger(
            -30,
            30
          )
      };
    }
  }


  host.species =
    normalizeString(
      host.species,
      "human",
      100
    ) ||
    "human";


  host.archetype =
    normalizeString(
      host.archetype,
      "television-host",
      100
    ) ||
    "television-host";


  if (
    !host.archetypeName
  ) {

    const archetype =
      HOST_ARCHETYPES.find(
        (
          entry
        ) =>
          entry.id ===
            host.archetype
      );


    host.archetypeName =
      archetype
        ?.name ||
      "Television Host";
  }


  if (
    !host.appearance ||
    typeof host.appearance !==
      "object"
  ) {

    host.appearance =
      {};
  }


  /*
   * PART 1 called the field "hair".
   *
   * Later code uses "hairColor".
   */

  if (
    !host.appearance
      .hairColor
  ) {

    host.appearance
      .hairColor =
        host.appearance
          .hair ||
        "Brown";
  }


  if (
    !host.appearance
      .hairstyle
  ) {

    host.appearance
      .hairstyle =
        "1985 Television Hair";
  }


  if (
    !host.appearance
      .clothing
  ) {

    host.appearance
      .clothing =
        "CRYPT NETWORK Studio Clothes";
  }


  if (
    !host.appearance
      .footwear
  ) {

    host.appearance
      .footwear =
        "Ordinary Shoes";
  }


  if (
    !host.appearance
      .accessory
  ) {

    host.appearance
      .accessory =
        "None";
  }


  if (
    !host.combat ||
    typeof host.combat !==
      "object"
  ) {

    host.combat =
      createDefaultHost()
        .combat;
  }


  host.combat.level =
    Math.max(
      1,
      integerNumber(
        host.combat
          .level,
        1
      )
    );


  host.combat.powerExponent =
    finiteNumber(
      host.combat
        .powerExponent,
      0
    );


  host.combat.maxHp =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .maxHp,
        100
      )
    );


  host.combat.hp =
    clamp(
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .hp,
        host.combat
          .maxHp
      ),
      0,
      host.combat
        .maxHp
    );


  host.combat.attack =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .attack,
        10
      )
    );


  host.combat.defense =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .defense,
        10
      )
    );


  host.combat.magic =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .magic,
        0
      )
    );


  host.combat.resistance =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .resistance,
        5
      )
    );


  host.combat.speed =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        host.combat
          .speed,
        10
      )
    );


  /*
   * ABSOLUTE CHARACTER RULE.
   */

  host.combat.canLevel =
    false;


  host.combat.alive =
    host.combat.alive !==
      false;


  host.combat.damageInfinity =
    host.combat.damageInfinity ===
      true;


  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      host.combat
        .spells
    )
  ) {

    host.combat.spells =
      [];
  }


  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      host.combat
        .statusEffects
    )
  ) {

    host.combat.statusEffects =
      [];
  }


  host.updatedAt =
    validIsoDate(
      host.updatedAt
    ) ||
    isoNow();
}


// =========================================================
// NAMELESS MAN SAVE MIGRATION
// =========================================================

function migrateNamelessState(
  state
) {

  if (
    !state.nameless ||
    typeof state.nameless !==
      "object"
  ) {

    state.nameless =
      createDefaultNamelessState();
  }


  const nameless =
    state.nameless;


  /*
   * No migration is ever allowed to accidentally invent
   * a true name.
   */

  nameless.canonicalName =
    null;


  nameless.invincible =
    true;


  if (
    !nameless.combat ||
    typeof nameless.combat !==
      "object"
  ) {

    nameless.combat =
      createDefaultNamelessState()
        .combat;
  }


  const combat =
    nameless.combat;


  combat.level =
    Math.max(
      1,
      integerNumber(
        combat.level,
        1
      )
    );


  combat.xp =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.xp,
        0
      )
    );


  combat.maxHp =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.maxHp,
        100
      )
    );


  combat.hp =
    clamp(
      finiteNumber(
        combat.hp,
        combat.maxHp
      ),
      0,
      combat.maxHp
    );


  combat.attack =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.attack,
        8
      )
    );


  combat.defense =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.defense,
        8
      )
    );


  combat.magic =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.magic,
        4
      )
    );


  combat.resistance =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.resistance,
        8
      )
    );


  combat.speed =
    Math.max(
      1,
      finiteNumber(
        combat.speed,
        10
      )
    );


  combat.majorEscalations =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        combat.majorEscalations,
        0
      )
    );


  combat.totalCombatActions =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        combat.totalCombatActions,
        0
      )
    );


  combat.totalHostsKilled =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        combat.totalHostsKilled,
        0
      )
    );


  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      combat.spells
    )
  ) {

    combat.spells =
      [];
  }


  if (
    !Array.isArray(
      combat.statusEffects
    )
  ) {

    combat.statusEffects =
      [];
  }


  nameless.updatedAt =
    validIsoDate(
      nameless.updatedAt
    ) ||
    isoNow();
}


// =========================================================
// COMBAT SAVE MIGRATION
// =========================================================

function migrateCombatState(
  state
) {

  if (
    !state.combat ||
    typeof state.combat !==
      "object"
  ) {

    state.combat =
      createDefaultCombatState();
  }


  const combat =
    state.combat;


  combat.elapsedCombatSeconds =
    Math.max(
      0,
      finiteNumber(
        combat
          .elapsedCombatSeconds,
        0
      )
    );


  /*
   * Reconstruct the next twelve-hour boundary if an older
   * or partially written save has lost it.
   */

  const completedBlocks =
    Math.floor(
      combat
        .elapsedCombatSeconds /
      MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS
    );


  const mathematicallyNextBoundary =
    (
      completedBlocks +
      1
    ) *
    MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS;


  combat.nextMajorEscalationAtSeconds =
    Math.max(
      mathematicallyNextBoundary,

      finiteNumber(
        combat
          .nextMajorEscalationAtSeconds,
        mathematicallyNextBoundary
      )
    );


  combat.actionNumber =
    Math.max(
      0,
      integerNumber(
        combat
          .actionNumber,
        0
      )
    );


  combat.currentAttacker =
    (
      combat
        .currentAttacker ===
          CHARACTER_HOST
    )
      ? CHARACTER_HOST
      : CHARACTER_NAMELESS;


  combat.stalemate =
    combat.stalemate ===
      true;
}


// =========================================================
// DEFAULT STATE ENHANCEMENT
// =========================================================

/*
 * New games should contain the Part 7 relationship system
 * from the moment they are created rather than waiting for
 * lazy migration.
 */

const createDefaultRecallStateBeforePart10 =
  createDefaultRecallState;


createDefaultRecallState =
  function () {

    const state =
      createDefaultRecallStateBeforePart10();


    state.relationship =
      createDefaultExtendedRelationshipState();


    state.version =
      RECALL_FINAL_BUILD;


    migrateCurrentHostState(
      state
    );


    migrateNamelessState(
      state
    );


    migrateCombatState(
      state
    );


    return state;
  };


// =========================================================
// MOST RECENT AUDIENCE STATEMENT
// =========================================================

async function loadMostRecentAudienceMessage(
  env,
  visitorId
) {

  const db =
    requireWorldDatabase(
      env
    );


  const row =
    await db
      .prepare(`
        SELECT
          id,
          content,
          created_at

        FROM recall_conversation_log

        WHERE
          visitor_id = ?
          AND
          speaker = ?

        ORDER BY id DESC

        LIMIT 1
      `)
      .bind(
        visitorId,
        CHARACTER_AUDIENCE
      )
      .first();


  return row ||
    null;
}


// =========================================================
// PART 10 INTERRUPTION RECOVERY
// =========================================================

/*
 * PART 8 can abort a browser fetch immediately.
 *
 * But aborting the browser's request does not guarantee
 * that an already-running server AI request disappears
 * instantly.
 *
 * PART 4 correctly stores:
 *
 *     audienceInterruptPending = true
 *
 * before attempting the replacement generation.
 *
 * If that immediate generation cannot acquire the request
 * lock, the browser eventually resumes /show/next.
 *
 * Prior to Part 10, /show/next knew the audience statement
 * existed in the transcript but did not explicitly give it
 * IMMEDIATE TURN priority.
 *
 * Part 10 closes that race.
 */


const handleNextShowTurnBeforePart10 =
  handleNextShowTurn;


handleNextShowTurn =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {

    let body =
      null;


    try {

      body =
        await request
          .clone()
          .json();

    } catch {

      /*
       * Let the normal endpoint produce the canonical
       * validation response.
       */

      return handleNextShowTurnBeforePart10(
        request,
        env
      );
    }


    const visitorId =
      normalizeVisitorId(
        body?.visitorId ??
        body?.visitor_id
      );


    if (!visitorId) {

      return handleNextShowTurnBeforePart10(
        request,
        env
      );
    }


    let preliminaryState;


    try {

      preliminaryState =
        await loadRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId
        );

    } catch {

      return handleNextShowTurnBeforePart10(
        request,
        env
      );
    }


    /*
     * Ordinary turn:
     *
     * preserve the already-tested Part 4→7 implementation.
     */

    if (
      preliminaryState
        .conversation
        .audienceInterruptPending !==
          true
    ) {

      return handleNextShowTurnBeforePart10(
        request,
        env
      );
    }


    // =====================================================
    // PENDING PLAYER INTERRUPTION HAS PRIORITY
    // =====================================================

    const acquired =
      await acquireRecallRequestLock(
        env,
        visitorId
      );


    if (!acquired) {

      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          type:
            "turn-already-running",

          error:
            "The interrupted speaker is still clearing the microphone."
        },
        409
      );
    }


    try {

      const [
        state,
        worldState,
        audience
      ] =
        await Promise.all([
          loadRecallState(
            env,
            visitorId
          ),

          loadSharedWorldState(
            env,
            visitorId
          ),

          loadMostRecentAudienceMessage(
            env,
            visitorId
          )
        ]);


      if (
        !state
          .television
          .poweredOn
      ) {

        return jsonResponse(
          {
            ok:
              false,

            type:
              "television-off",

            error:
              "The television is off."
          },
          409
        );
      }


      if (
        state.host
          ?.combat
          ?.alive ===
            false
      ) {

        return jsonResponse(
          {
            ok:
              false,

            type:
              "host-vacancy",

            requiresHost:
              true
          },
          409
        );
      }


      synchronizeRecallTime(
        state
      );


      const audienceText =
        normalizeString(
          audience
            ?.content,
          "",
          MAX_AUDIENCE_MESSAGE_LENGTH
        )
          .trim();


      /*
       * Corrupt/missing pending marker:
       *
       * clear it and let normal sequencing recover.
       */

      if (
        !audienceText
      ) {

        state
          .conversation
          .audienceInterruptPending =
            false;


        await saveRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId,
          state
        );


        return jsonResponse(
          {
            ok:
              false,

            type:
              "interruption-recovered",

            error:
              "The pending audience statement could not be recovered."
          },
          409
        );
      }


      const speakerKey =
        (
          state
            .conversation
            .activeSpeaker ===
              CHARACTER_HOST
        )
          ? CHARACTER_HOST
          : CHARACTER_NAMELESS;


      /*
       * This is the important distinction:
       *
       * audienceMessage:
       *
       *     YES
       *
       * previousSpeakerText:
       *
       *     NO
       *
       * The newest player statement therefore becomes the
       * immediate prompt target.
       */

      const generated =
        await generateRecallTurn(
          env,
          visitorId,
          state,
          {
            speakerKey,

            audienceMessage:
              audienceText,

            previousSpeakerText:
              null
          }
        );


      state
        .conversation
        .audienceInterruptPending =
          false;


      const saved =
        await saveRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId,
          state
        );


      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            true,

          type:
            "audience-response",

          recoveredInterruption:
            true,

          ...generated,

          conversationMode:
            saved
              .conversation
              .mode,

          topic:
            saved
              .conversation
              .currentTopic,

          lockType:
            saved
              .conversation
              .lockType,

          publicState:
            buildPublicRecallState(
              saved,
              worldState
            )
        }
      );


    } catch (
      error
    ) {

      console.error(
        "Pending audience recovery failed:",
        error
      );


      return jsonResponse(
        {
          ok:
            false,

          error:
            error?.message ||
            "The interrupted broadcast could not recover."
        },
        500
      );


    } finally {

      await releaseRecallRequestLock(
        env,
        visitorId
      );
    }
  };


// =========================================================
// TURN-OFF HARDENING
// =========================================================

const handleTurnTvOffBeforePart10 =
  handleTurnTvOff;


handleTurnTvOff =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {

    const clone =
      request.clone();


    let visitorId =
      null;


    try {

      const body =
        await clone.json();


      visitorId =
        normalizeVisitorId(
          body?.visitorId ??
          body?.visitor_id
        );

    } catch {

      visitorId =
        null;
    }


    /*
     * Complete the canonical television shutdown first.
     */

    const response =
      await handleTurnTvOffBeforePart10(
        request,
        env
      );


    /*
     * Remove any stale request ownership immediately after
     * shutdown.
     *
     * TURN OFF THE TV is the highest-priority command.
     */

    if (
      visitorId
    ) {

      try {

        const db =
          requireWorldDatabase(
            env
          );


        await db
          .prepare(`
            DELETE FROM recall_request_locks

            WHERE visitor_id = ?
          `)
          .bind(
            visitorId
          )
          .run();


      } catch (
        error
      ) {

        console.error(
          "TV-off request-lock cleanup failed:",
          error
        );
      }
    }


    return response;
  };


// =========================================================
// HOST HIRING FINAL NORMALIZATION
// =========================================================

const handleHireHostBeforePart10 =
  handleHireHost;


handleHireHost =
  async function (
    request,
    env
  ) {

    const clone =
      request.clone();


    let body =
      null;


    try {

      body =
        await clone.json();

    } catch {

      body =
        null;
    }


    const response =
      await handleHireHostBeforePart10(
        request,
        env
      );


    if (
      !response.ok ||
      !body
    ) {

      return response;
    }


    const visitorId =
      normalizeVisitorId(
        body?.visitorId ??
        body?.visitor_id
      );


    if (!visitorId) {

      return response;
    }


    /*
     * PART 7 already initializes relationship history.
     *
     * Part 10 merely performs a final migration save so the
     * extended relationship structure is guaranteed to be
     * present in the normalized persistent JSON.
     */

    try {

      const state =
        await loadRecallState(
          env,
          visitorId
        );


      ensureExtendedRelationshipState(
        state
      );


      migrateCurrentHostState(
        state
      );


      await saveRecallState(
        env,
        visitorId,
        state
      );


    } catch (
      error
    ) {

      console.error(
        "Post-hire final normalization failed:",
        error
      );
    }


    return response;
  };


// =========================================================
// SHOW-STATE FINAL SANITY CHECK
// =========================================================

function repairRecallShowState(
  state
) {

  /*
   * Dead host wins over every other visible state.
   */

  if (
    state.host
      ?.combat
      ?.alive ===
        false
  ) {

    state
      .combat
      .active =
        false;


    state
      .television
      .showState =
        state
          .television
          .poweredOn
            ? SHOW_STATE_HOST_DEAD
            : SHOW_STATE_OFF;


    return;
  }


  /*
   * Television off means no active visible show phase.
   */

  if (
    state
      .television
      .poweredOn !==
        true
  ) {

    state
      .television
      .showState =
        SHOW_STATE_OFF;


    return;
  }


  /*
   * Combat is authoritative over hostility/conversation.
   */

  if (
    state
      .combat
      .active ===
        true
  ) {

    state
      .television
      .showState =
        SHOW_STATE_COMBAT;


    return;
  }


  if (
    state
      .television
      .hostilityStarted ===
        true
  ) {

    state
      .television
      .showState =
        SHOW_STATE_HOSTILITY;


    return;
  }


  state
    .television
    .showState =
      SHOW_STATE_CONVERSATION;
}


// =========================================================
// SAVE STATE FINAL HARDENING
// =========================================================

const saveRecallStateBeforePart10 =
  saveRecallState;


saveRecallState =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    state
  ) {

    ensureExtendedRelationshipState(
      state
    );


    migrateCurrentHostState(
      state
    );


    migrateNamelessState(
      state
    );


    migrateCombatState(
      state
    );


    repairRecallShowState(
      state
    );


    state.version =
      RECALL_FINAL_BUILD;


    return saveRecallStateBeforePart10(
      env,
      visitorId,
      state
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// CURRENT HOST ARCHIVE HARDENING
// =========================================================

const archiveHostBeforePart10 =
  archiveHost;


archiveHost =
  async function (
    env,
    visitorId,
    host,
    options =
      {}
  ) {

    if (
      !host ||
      typeof host !==
        "object"
    ) {

      return;
    }


    /*
     * Never archive a host with a missing identity merely
     * because an ancient/incomplete save was loaded.
     */

    if (
      !host.name
    ) {

      host.name =
        "Unknown CRYPT NETWORK Host";
    }


    return archiveHostBeforePart10(
      env,
      visitorId,
      host,
      options
    );
  };


// =========================================================
// HISTORICAL DATE FINAL GUARD
// =========================================================

function repair1985Calendar(
  state
) {

  const index =
    (
      Math.max(
        0,
        integerNumber(
          state
            ?.calendar
            ?.dayIndex,
          0
        )
      ) %
      SHOW_CALENDAR_DAYS
    );


  state.calendar.dayIndex =
    index;


  state.calendar.currentDate =
    get1985DateFromDayIndex(
      index
    );


  /*
   * There is never a 1986 state.
   */

  if (
    !state
      .calendar
      .currentDate
      .startsWith(
        "1985-"
      )
  ) {

    state.calendar.dayIndex =
      0;


    state.calendar.currentDate =
      SHOW_CALENDAR_START;
  }
}


// =========================================================
// TIME SYNCHRONIZATION FINAL WRAPPER
// =========================================================

const synchronizeRecallTimeBeforePart10 =
  synchronizeRecallTime;


synchronizeRecallTime =
  function (
    state,
    now =
      new Date()
  ) {

    repair1985Calendar(
      state
    );


    const result =
      synchronizeRecallTimeBeforePart10(
        state,
        now
      );


    repairRecallShowState(
      state
    );


    return result;
  };


// =========================================================
// FINAL BUILD ASSERTIONS
// =========================================================

function assertRecallFinalBuild() {

  const errors =
    [];


  if (
    LOCK_TYPES.length !==
      3
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "There must be exactly three conversational locks."
    );
  }


  if (
    HOST_STORE_CANDIDATE_COUNT !==
      7
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "The Host Store must generate seven candidates."
    );
  }


  if (
    HOSTILITY_START_SECONDS !==
      (
        95 *
        60
      )
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "Hostility must begin at 1 hour 35 minutes."
    );
  }


  if (
    COMBAT_START_SECONDS !==
      (
        2 *
        60 *
        60
      )
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "Combat must begin after two hours."
    );
  }


  if (
    MAJOR_COMBAT_ESCALATION_SECONDS !==
      (
        12 *
        60 *
        60
      )
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "Major combat escalation must occur every twelve hours."
    );
  }


  if (
    MAJOR_LEVEL_GAIN !==
      100
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "Twelve-hour escalation must grant 100 levels."
    );
  }


  if (
    MAJOR_SPELL_GAIN !==
      3
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "Twelve-hour escalation must grant three spells."
    );
  }


  if (
    SHOW_CALENDAR_DAYS !==
      365
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "The trapped 1985 calendar must contain 365 days."
    );
  }


  if (
    typeof handleJukeboxCatalog !==
      "function"
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "The EFSMB jukebox integration is missing."
    );
  }


  if (
    typeof handleNextShowTurn !==
      "function"
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "The autonomous conversation engine is missing."
    );
  }


  if (
    typeof executeCombatAction !==
      "function"
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "The combat engine is missing."
    );
  }


  if (
    typeof ensureExtendedRelationshipState !==
      "function"
  ) {

    errors.push(
      "The long-term relationship engine is missing."
    );
  }


  if (
    typeof createJukeboxInterface ===
      "undefined"
  ) {

    /*
     * createJukeboxInterface exists inside the client-side
     * HTML script rather than the Worker global scope.
     *
     * Therefore this condition is expected server-side and
     * is NOT an error.
     */
  }


  if (
    errors.length >
      0
  ) {

    throw new Error(
      (
        "RECALL THY NAME FINAL BUILD FAILED:\\n" +
        errors.join(
          "\\n"
        )
      )
    );
  }


  return true;
}


// =========================================================
// FINAL ASSERTION RUN
// =========================================================

assertRecallFinalBuild();
